OAI.EditApproximation.almostLinearEditDistanceRawBand : ∀ (epsilon : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction),
  0 < epsilon.value →
    epsilon.value < 1 →
      (∀ (source target : List ℤ) (N : ℕ),
          source.length + target.length ≤ N →
            2 / 3 ≤
              (OAI.EditApproximation.computedFullLaw (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
                    (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N epsilon).real
                {outcome |
                  OAI.EditDistortion.edit source target ≤
                      OAI.EditApproximation.integerBinaryAnswerRawBand source target N epsilon outcome ∧
                    ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.integerBinaryAnswerRawBand source target N epsilon outcome) ≤
                      (1 + ↑epsilon.value) * ↑(OAI.EditDistortion.edit source target)}) ∧
        (∀ (source target : List ℤ) (N : ℕ),
            source = target →
              ∀
                (outcome :
                  OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
                    (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N),
                OAI.EditApproximation.integerBinaryAnswerRawBand source target N epsilon outcome = 0) ∧
          (∀ (C : ℕ) (eta : ℝ),
              0 < eta →
                ∀ᶠ (N : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
                  ∀ (source target : List ℤ),
                    source.length + target.length ≤ N →
                      (∀ z ∈ source ++ target, z.natAbs ≤ (N + 2) ^ C) →
                        ∫ (outcome :
                            OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome
                              (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
                              (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N),
                            OAI.EditApproximation.integerBinaryWorkRawBand source target N epsilon
                              outcome ∂OAI.EditApproximation.computedFullLaw
                              (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
                              (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N epsilon ≤
                          (↑N + 2) ^ (1 + eta)) ∧
            ∀ (C : ℕ),
              ∀ᶠ (N : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
                ∀ (source target : List ℤ),
                  source.length + target.length ≤ N →
                    (∀ z ∈ source ++ target, z.natAbs ≤ (N + 2) ^ C) →
                      ∀
                        (outcome :
                          OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome
                            (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
                            (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N),
                        OAI.EditApproximation.integerBinarySpaceFootprintRawBand source target N epsilon outcome ≤
                          (N + 2) ^ (C + 65564)
'OAI.EditApproximation.almostLinearEditDistanceRawBand' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue : List Bool → ℕ :=
fun x => List.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue._f
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitSum : Bool → Bool → Bool → Bool :=
fun a b carry => a ^^ b ^^ carry
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitCarry : Bool → Bool → Bool → Bool :=
fun a b carry => a && b || a && carry || b && carry

def OAI.EditApproximation.orderingMatches : Ordering → ℕ → ℕ → Prop :=
fun o a b => o = Ordering.lt ∧ a < b ∨ o = Ordering.eq ∧ a = b ∨ o = Ordering.gt ∧ b < a
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitOrdering : Ordering → Bool → Bool → Ordering :=
fun high a b =>
  match high with
  | Ordering.lt => Ordering.lt
  | Ordering.gt => Ordering.gt
  | Ordering.eq => if a = b then Ordering.eq else if a = true then Ordering.gt else Ordering.lt
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitDifference : Bool → Bool → Bool → Bool :=
fun a b borrow => a ^^ b ^^ borrow
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitBorrow : Bool → Bool → Bool → Bool :=
fun a b borrow => !a && (b || borrow) || b && borrow

fun lo width M i => lo + i * width / M

fun spacing position => spacing * (position / spacing)

def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalRefinementUpdate : ℚ → ℚ → Option ℚ → ℚ :=
fun δ cone x =>
  match x with
  | none => cone
  | some online => min cone (max (cone / (1 + δ)) online)
def OAI.EditApproximation.heightExponent : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N => Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 (N + 2))
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseExponent : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun ell => (Nat.log 2 ell).sqrt.sqrt

def OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ :=
fun g lo hi => List.map (fun i => g * i) (List.range' (lo ⌈/⌉ g) (hi / g + 1 - lo ⌈/⌉ g))
def OAI.EditApproximation.scaledSeedValue : ℕ → ℚ → ℚ → ℚ :=
fun U a gap => max ↑⌈↑U / a⌉₊ |gap|
def OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales : ℕ → List ℕ :=
fun N => List.map (fun k => 2 ^ k) (List.range (Nat.log 2 N + 1))
def OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun b P => max 1 (b / P)

def OAI.EditApproximation.cellGridPoints : ℚ → ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ :=
fun side g cell =>
  List.map (fun i => g * i) (List.range' ⌈↑cell * side / ↑g⌉₊ (⌈(↑cell + 1) * side / ↑g⌉₊ - ⌈↑cell * side / ↑g⌉₊))
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupRoundSpacing : ℕ → ℚ → ℕ :=
fun exponent h => ⌈max 1 (2 ^ (-↑exponent) * h)⌉₊
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementFactor : ℚ → ℕ → ℚ :=
fun A F => max 1 (A / ↑F)
def OAI.EditApproximation.localCellCoordinates : ℚ → ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ :=
fun side p radius =>
  have first := ⌊↑(p - radius) / side⌋₊;
  have last := ⌊↑(p + radius) / side⌋₊;
  List.range' first (last + 1 - first)
def OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedPassCount : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun f initial => (initial - f) ⌈/⌉ (f - 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun f initial pass => initial - pass * (f - 2)

  if h : n = 0 then (0, 1)
  else
    let tail := a (n / 2) ⋯;
    (tail.1 + 1, tail.2 + 2)

def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalActionValue.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → ℚ) →
        ℚ → (Child → ℚ) → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.RationalBellmanAction Parent Child M → ℚ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance L child query action =>
  L * distance query action.center + action.connection + ∑ i, child (action.childState i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N => 2 ^ OAI.EditApproximation.heightExponent N

def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParameters : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.RationalParameters :=
fun ell =>
  { F := ↑ell, initialSlope := 32 * ↑ell, coneSlope := 128 * ↑ell, delta := 1 / ↑ell ^ 10, kappa := 1 / ↑ell ^ 4,
    tau := 1 / ↑ell ^ 4, theta := 1 / ↑ell ^ 40, gamma := 1 / ↑ell ^ 20, eta := ↑ell ^ 5 }

def OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork : ℕ → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun N =>
  have count := max 1 N.bits.length;
  have values :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun e =>
        have word := OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord e;
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue word, e + 3))
      (List.range count);
  (values.1, values.2 + N.bits.length + count + 2)

def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseLoopWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → List α → List α → List α × ℕ :=
fun {α} equal x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List α → List α × ℕ) x (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseLoopWithWork._f equal) x_1
structure OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.{u_1} (κ : Type u_1) : Type u_1
number of parameters: 1
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.value : ℚ
  OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.memory : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ
  OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.freshKeys : List κ
  OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.dictionaryVisits : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.requests : ℕ
constructor:
  OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult.mk.{u_1} {κ : Type u_1} (value : ℚ)
    (memory : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ) (freshKeys : List κ) (dictionaryVisits requests : ℕ) :
    OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult κ
def OAI.EditApproximation.querySortWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → List α → List α × ℕ :=
fun {α} le x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.querySortWithWork._f le)
def OAI.EditApproximation.encodeIntegerSymbolsWithWork : List ℤ → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun x => List.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.encodeIntegerSymbolsWithWork._f
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputSymbolBits : List ℤ → List ℤ → ℕ :=
fun source target => (2 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerMagnitude (source ++ target)).size
def OAI.EditApproximation.dpSingleton.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → α → List α → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] a word =>
  if word = [] then (1, 1)
  else
    have count := OAI.EditApproximation.dpLength word;
    have found := OAI.EditApproximation.dpMember a word;
    (count.1 - if found.1 = true then 1 else 0, count.2 + found.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.dpCandidateAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → List α → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {α} target j k => (OAI.EditApproximation.dpTake k (OAI.EditApproximation.dpDrop j target).1).1.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputInspectionWithWork : List ℤ → List ℤ → (ℕ × ℕ) × ℕ :=
fun source target =>
  have left := OAI.EditApproximation.integerMagnitudeWithWork source;
  have right := OAI.EditApproximation.integerMagnitudeWithWork target;
  have lengths := (OAI.EditApproximation.dpLength source, OAI.EditApproximation.dpLength target);
  have symbols := OAI.EditApproximation.inputBitLengthWithWork (2 * max left.1 right.1);
  have positions := OAI.EditApproximation.inputBitLengthWithWork lengths.2.1;
  ((symbols.1, positions.1), left.2 + right.2 + lengths.1.2 + lengths.2.2 + symbols.2 + positions.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddNilLeftAllocation : List Bool → Bool → ℕ :=
fun right carry =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun right => Bool → ℕ) right OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddNilLeftAllocation._f carry
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractNilLeftAllocation : List Bool → Bool → ℕ :=
fun right borrow =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun right => Bool → ℕ) right OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractNilLeftAllocation._f borrow
def OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordAllocation : List Bool → ℕ :=
fun x => List.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordAllocation._f
def OAI.EditApproximation.dedupByAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → ℕ) → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} equal allocation x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.dedupByAllocation._f equal allocation)
def OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesAllocation : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun e => e + 1) (List.range (max 1 N.bits.length)) + N.bits.length +
    max 1 N.bits.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryRangeValidAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun B range => (Nat.bits 0).length + range.bits.length + OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation range.bits B.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseLoopAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → ℕ) → List α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} equal allocation x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List α → ℕ) x (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseLoopAllocation._f equal allocation) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → ℕ × ℕ) → (α → ℕ) → α → α → ℕ :=
fun {α} code allocation a b =>
  allocation a + allocation b + OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation (code a).1.bits (code b).1.bits +
      (code a).1.size +
    (code b).1.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → Bool → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun left right carry =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun left => List Bool → Bool → List Bool × ℕ) left OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork._f
    right carry
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → Ordering × ℕ :=
fun left right =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun left => List Bool → Ordering × ℕ) left OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork._f right
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → Bool → List Bool × Bool × ℕ :=
fun left right borrow =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun left => List Bool → Bool → List Bool × Bool × ℕ) left
    OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractWithWork._f right borrow
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalBellman.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → ℚ) →
        (Parent → Parent → ℚ) →
          ℚ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.RationalBellmanAction Parent Child M) → (Child → ℚ) → Parent → ℚ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} fallback distance L actions child query =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMinimum (fallback query)
    (List.map (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalActionValue distance L child query) actions)
def OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N => 2 ^ OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N
structure OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction.{u_1, u_2} (Parent : Type u_1) (Child : Type u_2) (M : ℕ) :
  Type (max u_1 u_2)
number of parameters: 3
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction.center : Parent
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction.connection : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction.childState : Fin M → Child
constructor:
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction.mk.{u_1, u_2} {Parent : Type u_1} {Child : Type u_2} {M : ℕ}
    (center : Parent) (connection : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) (childState : Fin M → Child) :
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → List α → List α × ℕ :=
fun {α} equal xs => OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseLoopWithWork equal xs []
inductive OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.{u_1, u_2} : Type u_1 → Type u_2 → Type (max u_1 u_2)
number of parameters: 2
constructors:
OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done : {ι : Type u_1} → {β : Type u_2} → β → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β
OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.read : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {β : Type u_2} →
    ι → (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β
OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {β : Type u_2} → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β
def OAI.EditApproximation.dpCandidate.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → α → List α → List ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] a target previous j k =>
  have suffix := OAI.EditApproximation.dpDrop j target;
  have piece := OAI.EditApproximation.dpTake k suffix.1;
  have one := OAI.EditApproximation.dpSingleton a piece.1;
  have old := OAI.EditApproximation.dpGetD previous (j + k);
  (one.1 + old.1, suffix.2 + piece.2 + one.2 + old.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.dpCellAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → List α → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {α} target j =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.dpCandidateAllocation target j 0 +
      (List.map (OAI.EditApproximation.dpCandidateAllocation target j) (List.range (target.length - j + 1))).sum +
    4 * (target.length - j + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.initialThresholdQ : ℕ → ℚ :=
fun N => ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) ^ 4
def OAI.EditApproximation.initialLambda : ℕ → ℚ :=
fun N => 1 / (64 * ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N))
structure OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult.{u_1} (κ : Type u_1) : Type u_1
number of parameters: 1
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult.value : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult.memory : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction

  OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult.dictionaryVisits : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult.requests : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult.localWork : ℕ

    (memory : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) (freshKeys : List κ)
    (dictionaryVisits requests localWork : ℕ) : OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult κ
structure OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords : Type
number of parameters: 0
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords.tau : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction
  OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords.factor : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction

    (tau factor eta kappa delta : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) : OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords
def OAI.EditApproximation.inspectAccuracyWithWork : ℕ →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Option OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun budget epsilon =>
  have numerator := OAI.EditApproximation.inspectBitPrefix budget epsilon.numerator.bits;
  have denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.inspectBitPrefix budget epsilon.denominator;
  (if (numerator.1 && denominator.1) = true then some epsilon else none, numerator.2 + denominator.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation : List Bool → List Bool → Bool → ℕ :=
fun left right carry =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun left => List Bool → Bool → ℕ) left OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation._f right carry
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation : List Bool → List Bool → Bool → ℕ :=
fun left right borrow =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun left => List Bool → Bool → ℕ) left OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation._f right
    borrow
def OAI.EditApproximation.materializedAssignmentsAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (M : ℕ) → (Fin M → List α) → ℕ :=
fun {α} x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => (Fin x → List α) → ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.materializedAssignmentsAllocation._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → ℕ) → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} equal allocation xs => OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseLoopAllocation equal allocation xs []
def OAI.EditApproximation.querySortAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → ℕ) → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} le allocation x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.querySortAllocation._f le allocation)
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork a.bits b.bits false
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 => List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List Bool → List Bool × ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → Ordering × ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork a.bits b.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalSubtractWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractWithWork a.bits b.bits false;
  (result.1, result.2.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.IntegerParameters :=
fun N =>
  have H := OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N;
  have ell := OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N;
  have p := OAI.EditApproximation.coarseExponent ell;
  { H := H, ell := ell, p := p, B := H ^ p, initialFactor := H ^ (p + 4),
    M := 2 ^ (OAI.EditApproximation.heightExponent N / 20), P := H ^ (p + 20),
    T := 2 ^ ((3 * OAI.EditApproximation.heightExponent N + 3) / 4), S := H / ell ^ 2 }
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool × List Bool × ℕ :=
fun divisor x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork._f divisor)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have compared := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork b a;
  (!decide (compared.1 = Ordering.lt), compared.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) + 4) *
    OAI.EditApproximation.heightExponent N
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedInputsWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → List α → List α × ℕ :=
fun {α} equal le xs =>
  have unique := OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseWithWork equal xs;
  have sorted := OAI.EditApproximation.querySortWithWork le unique.1;
  (sorted.1, unique.2 + sorted.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} → {M : ℕ} → List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) → List Child × ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} actions =>
  have inputs :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => (action.childState i, 1)) (List.finRange M))
      actions;
  (inputs.1, inputs.2 + M + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.largeAccuracyInput : ℕ → ℚ → Prop :=
fun N epsilon =>
  2 ^ 1000 ≤ OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ∧ 10 ≤ ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) * epsilon
def OAI.EditApproximation.dpCell.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → α → List α → List ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] a target previous j =>
  have fallback := OAI.EditApproximation.dpCandidate a target previous j 0;
  have candidates :=
    List.map (OAI.EditApproximation.dpCandidate a target previous j) (List.range (target.length - j + 1));
  have minimum := OAI.EditApproximation.dpMinimum fallback.1 (List.map Prod.fst candidates);
  (minimum.1,
    fallback.2 + (List.map Prod.snd candidates).sum + minimum.2 + (OAI.EditApproximation.dpLength target).2 +
        3 * candidates.length +
      4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.suffixDPAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → List α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} x x_1 => List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List α → ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.suffixDPAllocation._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation a.bits b.bits false
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedInputsAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → Bool × ℕ) → (α → α → ℕ) → (α → α → ℕ) → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} equal le equalAllocation leAllocation xs =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseAllocation equal equalAllocation xs +
    OAI.EditApproximation.querySortAllocation le leAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseWithWork equal xs).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} → {M : ℕ} → List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) → ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} actions =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
    (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => (action.childState i, 1)) (List.finRange M))
    (fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1) (List.finRange M) + M) actions
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork a.bits b.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × List Bool × ℕ :=
fun n d => OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork d.bits n.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun divisor bits =>
  have divided := OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork divisor bits;
  have zeroCheck := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork divided.2.1 [];
  if zeroCheck.1 = Ordering.eq then (divided.1, divided.2.2 + zeroCheck.2 + 3)
  else
    have rounded := OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork divided.1 [true] false;
    (rounded.1, divided.2.2 + zeroCheck.2 + rounded.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wordMinWithWork : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have compared := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork a b;
  (if compared.1 = true then a else b, compared.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wordInsertWithWork : List Bool → List (List Bool) → List (List Bool) × ℕ :=
fun a x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.wordInsertWithWork._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork a b;
  if test.1 = Ordering.lt then ([], test.2 + 1)
  else
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalSubtractWithWork a b;
    (result.1, test.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedPassCount (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N)
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork : List Bool → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun base x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork._f base)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (α → ℕ × ℕ) → α → α → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {α} code a b =>
  have left := code a;
  have right := code b;
  have comparison := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork left.1.bits right.1.bits;
  (comparison.1, left.2 + right.2 + comparison.2 + left.1.size + right.1.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSuffixDP.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → List α → List α → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List α → List ℕ × ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSuffixDP._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedCoarseDepth : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N n => Nat.clog (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).B (max 1 n)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryRangeValidWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun B range =>
  have positive := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork 0 range;
  have bounded := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork range.bits B.bits;
  (decide (positive.1 = Ordering.lt) && bounded.1, positive.2 + bounded.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePaddedReadWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → ℕ → List α → ℕ → Option (Option α) × ℕ :=
fun {α} n source offset =>
  have inside := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork offset n;
  (if inside.1 = Ordering.lt then some source[offset]? else none, inside.2 + n.size + offset.size + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseRangeCopyWithWork : (ℕ → ℕ) → ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun read x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => ℕ → List ℕ × ℕ) x_1 (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseRangeCopyWithWork._f read) x
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation : List Bool → List Bool → ℕ :=
fun x x_1 => List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List Bool → ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation : List Bool → List Bool → ℕ :=
fun divisor x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation._f divisor)
def OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  a.bits.length + b.bits.length +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork a b).1 = Ordering.lt then 0
    else OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation a.bits b.bits false
def OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun n lo hi =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation lo.bits hi.bits +
      OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation hi.bits n.bits +
    if
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork lo.bits hi.bits).1 &&
            (OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork hi.bits n.bits).1) =
          true then
      2
    else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun reference root endpoint =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation endpoint.bits root.bits false +
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation reference
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalSubtractWithWork endpoint root).1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wordInsertAllocation : List Bool → List (List Bool) → ℕ :=
fun a x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.wordInsertAllocation._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortWithWork : List (List Bool) → List (List Bool) × ℕ :=
fun x => List.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortWithWork._f
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryGridRoundWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun spacing position =>
  have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork position spacing;
  have rounded := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork spacing.bits quotient.1;
  (rounded.1, quotient.2.2 + rounded.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun g lo hi =>
  have lower := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivWithWork g.bits lo.bits;
  have upper := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork hi g;
  have stop := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper.1) 1;
  have count :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue stop.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower.1);
  have indices := List.range' (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue count.1);
  have points :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun i =>
        have product := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork g i;
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product.1, product.2 + i.size + 2))
      indices;
  (points.1, lower.2 + upper.2.2 + stop.2 + count.2 + points.2 + g.size + lo.size + hi.size + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun b P =>
  have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork b P;
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork quotient.1 [true];
  (if test.1 = true then [true] else quotient.1, quotient.2.2 + test.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCutWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun lo width M i =>
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork i width;
  have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product.1) M;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue quotient.1);
  (result.1, product.2 + quotient.2.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.triangularMassWithWork : ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun K =>
  have next := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork K 1;
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork K (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next.1);
  have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product.1) 2;
  (quotient.1, next.2 + product.2 + quotient.2.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementBaseWithWork : List Bool → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun ell =>
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork ell 10;
  have increment := OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork power.1 [true] false;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordWithWork increment.1;
  (result.1, power.2 + increment.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPairWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork a b;
  if test.1 = Ordering.lt then
    have square := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork b b;
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue square.1) a;
    (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue result.1, test.2 + square.2 + result.2 + 3)
  else
    have square := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork a a;
    have linear := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue square.1) a;
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue linear.1) b;
    (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue result.1, test.2 + square.2 + linear.2 + result.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryCellValidWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun n exponent cell =>
  have cap :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork n
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord exponent));
  have left := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork cell.1.bits cap.1;
  have right := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork cell.2.bits cap.1;
  (left.1 && right.1, cap.2 + left.2 + right.2 + exponent + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork : (bound : ℕ) → ℕ → 0 < bound → Fin bound × ℕ :=
fun bound value hb =>
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractor_value_17 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_binaryNaturalDivModWithWork_spec_24 := ⋯;
  let result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork value bound;
  (⟨OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue result.2.1, ⋯⟩, result.2.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseDenominatorWithWork : List Bool →
  List Bool → List Bool → List Bool → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun allowanceDen q L branching D =>
  have first := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork L branching;
  have base := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork first.1 q;
  have trimmedBase := OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordWithWork base.1;
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork trimmedBase.1 D;
  have initial := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork allowanceDen q;
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork initial.1 power.1;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordWithWork product.1;
  (result.1, first.2 + base.2 + trimmedBase.2 + power.2 + initial.2 + product.2 + result.2 + 7)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseFramedReadWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  ℕ → ℕ → List α → ℕ → Option (Option α) × ℕ :=
fun {α} k n word i =>
  have lower := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork i k;
  if lower.1 = Ordering.lt then (none, lower.2 + k.size + n.size + i.size + 2)
  else
    have finish := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork k n;
    have upper := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork i (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue finish.1);
    if upper.1 = Ordering.lt then
      have offset := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork i k;
      (some word[OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue offset.1]?,
        lower.2 + finish.2 + upper.2 + offset.2 + k.size + n.size + i.size + 4)
    else (none, lower.2 + finish.2 + upper.2 + k.size + n.size + i.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseDescriptorWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun B d n start =>
  have capacity := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork B.bits d;
  have remaining := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork n start;
  have clipped := OAI.EditApproximation.wordMinWithWork capacity.1 remaining.1;
  (clipped.1, capacity.2 + remaining.2 + clipped.2 + B.size + d.size + n.size + start.size + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseChildOffsetWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun offset capacity index =>
  have displacement := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork index capacity;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork offset (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue displacement.1);
  (result.1, displacement.2 + result.2 + index.size + capacity.size + offset.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInputGuardWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun sourceLo sourceHi targetLo targetHi =>
  have sourceWidth := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork sourceHi sourceLo;
  have targetWidth := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork targetHi targetLo;
  have twice := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 2 (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue sourceWidth.1);
  have compared :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue twice.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue targetWidth.1);
  (decide (compared.1 = Ordering.lt), sourceWidth.2 + targetWidth.2 + twice.2 + compared.2 + 6)
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation : List Bool → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun base x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation._f base)
def OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivAllocation : List Bool → List Bool → ℕ :=
fun divisor bits =>
  have divided := OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork divisor bits;
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation divisor bits +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork divided.2.1 []).1 = Ordering.eq then 0
    else 1 + OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation divided.1 [true] false
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation a.bits b.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation b.bits a.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.triangularMassAllocation : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun K =>
  have next := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork K 1).1;
  have product := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork K (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation K.bits (Nat.bits 1) false +
      OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation K.bits (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next).bits +
    OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation (Nat.bits 2) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product).bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryIntervalRoundWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → (List Bool × List Bool) × ℕ :=
fun spacing lo hi =>
  have left := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryGridRoundWithWork spacing lo;
  have right := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryGridRoundWithWork spacing hi;
  ((left.1, right.1), left.2 + right.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryBase : ℕ → List Bool :=
fun N => (OAI.EditApproximation.refinementBaseWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N).bits).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePreparedSlicesWithWork : List ℕ →
  List ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Option (List ℕ × List ℕ) × ℕ :=
fun source target sourceLo sourceHi targetLo targetHi =>
  have guard := OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInputGuardWithWork sourceLo sourceHi targetLo targetHi;
  if guard.1 = true then (none, guard.2)
  else
    have copiedSource :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.coarseRangeCopyWithWork (fun i => source[i]?.getD 0) sourceLo (sourceHi - sourceLo);
    have copiedTarget :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.coarseRangeCopyWithWork (fun i => target[i]?.getD 0) targetLo (targetHi - targetLo);
    (some (copiedSource.1, copiedTarget.1), guard.2 + copiedSource.2 + copiedTarget.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun g lo hi =>
  have lower := (OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivWithWork g.bits lo.bits).1;
  have upper := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork hi g).1;
  have stop := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper) 1).1;
  have count :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue stop)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower)).1;
  have indices := List.range' (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue count);
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivAllocation g.bits lo.bits + OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation hi g +
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper) 1 +
        OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue stop)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower) +
      indices.length +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun i => OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation g i)
      indices
def OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCutAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun lo width M i =>
  have product := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork i width).1;
  have quotient := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product) M).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation i width +
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product) M +
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue quotient)
def OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun b P =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation b P +
      OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork b P).1 [true] +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryCellValidAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ → ℕ :=
fun n exponent cell =>
  have cap :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork n
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord exponent))).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation n
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord exponent)) +
          OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation cell.1.bits cap +
        OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation cell.2.bits cap +
      exponent +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPairAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  a.bits.length + b.bits.length +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork a b).1 = Ordering.lt then
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation b b +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork b b).1) a
    else
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation a a +
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork a a).1) a +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue
            (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork
                (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork a a).1) a).1)
          b
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryGridRoundAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun spacing position =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation position spacing +
    OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation spacing.bits
      (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork position spacing).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortAllocation : List (List Bool) → ℕ :=
fun x => List.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortAllocation._f
def OAI.EditApproximation.reduceOnlineIntegerAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun K integer =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.triangularMassAllocation K +
      OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.triangularMassWithWork K).1).bits integer.bits +
    integer.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePreparedInputStorage : List ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun source target sourceLo sourceHi targetLo targetHi =>
  match (OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePreparedSlicesWithWork source target sourceLo sourceHi targetLo targetHi).1 with
  | none => 1
  | some (x, y) => 1 + OAI.EditApproximation.storedNaturalBits x + OAI.EditApproximation.storedNaturalBits y
def OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun K => ∑ j ∈ Finset.range K, (j + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalLogApproximation : ℚ → ℕ → ℚ :=
fun r K => 2 * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range K, ((r - 1) / (r + 1)) ^ (2 * k + 1) / (2 * ↑k + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalLinearizationMaximizer.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  Finset ι → List (ι → ℚ) → (ι → ℚ) → ι → ℚ :=
fun {ι} coordinates vertices gradient =>
  (List.argmax (fun p => ∑ i ∈ coordinates, gradient i * p i) vertices).getD fun x => 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.bandCoordinates.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} → [Fintype β] → [DecidableEq β] → [DecidableEq σ] → Finset (β → σ) → Finset (β × σ) :=
fun {β} {σ} [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β] [DecidableEq σ] band =>
  band.biUnion fun action => Finset.image (fun child => (child, action child)) Finset.univ
def OAI.EditApproximation.actionVector.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} → [Fintype β] → [DecidableEq σ] → (β → ℚ) → (β → σ) → β × σ → ℚ :=
fun {β} {σ} [Fintype β] [DecidableEq σ] mass action coordinate =>
  if coordinate.2 = action coordinate.1 then mass coordinate.1 / ∑ child, mass child else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalOptimisticHistory.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  ℚ → (ℕ → ι → ℚ) → (ℕ → ι → ℚ) → ℕ → ι → ℚ :=
fun {ι} κ gain decrement t i => ∑ j ∈ Finset.range t, (gain j i + (1 - 2 * κ) * decrement j i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionBitCount : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N => Nat.clog 2 N
def OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → (d : ℕ) → (Fin d → α × ℕ) → Vector α d × ℕ :=
fun {α} d f =>
  have cells := Vector.ofFn f;
  (Vector.map Prod.fst cells, (Vector.map Prod.snd cells).toList.sum + 6 * d)
def OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer : ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer._f M)
def OAI.EditApproximation.intervalTreeDepth : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun M nx => Nat.clog M (max 1 nx)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMaximum : ℚ → List ℚ → ℚ :=
fun fallback x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMaximum._f fallback)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalDyadicCeiling : ℚ → ℚ :=
fun value => 2 ^ Int.clog 2 value
def OAI.EditApproximation.listedBandCoordinates.{u_1} : {M : ℕ} →
  {σ : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq σ] → List (Fin M → σ) → List (Fin M × σ) :=
fun {M} {σ} [DecidableEq σ] band =>
  (List.flatMap (fun action => List.ofFn fun child => (child, action child)) band).dedup
def OAI.EditApproximation.childGain.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} → (β → ℚ) → (ℕ → β × σ → ℚ) → ℕ → β × σ → ℚ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass table t i => -table t i / mass i.1
def OAI.EditApproximation.childDecrement.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} → (β → ℚ) → (ℕ → β × σ → ℚ) → ℕ → β × σ → ℚ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass table t i => (table t i - table (t + 1) i) / mass i.1
def OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork a b;
  (decide (result.1 = Ordering.eq), result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun reference root endpoint =>
  have difference := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalSubtractWithWork endpoint root;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork reference (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue difference.1);
  (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue result.1, difference.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalOnlineMinimum : List ℚ → Option ℚ :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | [] => none
  | first :: rest => some (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMinimum first rest)
def OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow : ℕ → ℚ → ℚ → ℚ → List ℕ :=
fun N w a F => List.filter (fun b => decide (w / (16 * F) ≤ ↑b ∧ ↑b ≤ 8 * a * w)) (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N)
def OAI.EditApproximation.actionCurrentInputCover.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    [DecidableEq Child] → {M : ℕ} → List (OAI.EditApproximation.RationalBellmanAction Parent Child M) → Finset Child :=
fun {Parent} {Child} [DecidableEq Child] {M} actions =>
  (List.finRange actions.length).toFinset.biUnion fun index => Finset.image (actions.get index).childState Finset.univ
def OAI.EditApproximation.boundedOnlineInteger : (n : ℕ) → 0 < n → ℕ → Fin n :=
fun n hn integer => ⟨integer % n, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.orderedInputs.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  [DecidableEq ι] → (encode : ι → ℕ) → Function.Injective encode → Finset ι → List ι :=
fun {ι} [DecidableEq ι] encode hinjective inputs =>
  let x := LinearOrder.lift' encode hinjective;
  inputs.sort fun x1 x2 => x1 ≤ x2
def OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws : ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun B x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws._f B)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseThresholdContribution : ℚ → ℚ → ℚ → ℚ :=
fun threshold scale value => if threshold ≤ value then max value scale else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseChildThreshold : (B : ℕ) → ℚ → ℚ → Fin B → ℚ :=
fun B Q allowance draw => allowance * ↑(↑draw + 1) / (↑B * Q)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseCommonDenominator : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun allowanceDen q L B D => allowanceDen * q * (L * B * q) ^ D
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalSmoothedGradient.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} → ℕ → (ι → ℚ) → (ι → ℚ) → ι → ℚ :=
fun {ι} G linear p i => -OAI.EditApproximation.rationalLogApproximation (p i + 1 / ↑G ^ 50) (G ^ 60) - 1 + linear i
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalOnlineLinear.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} → ℚ → ℚ → (ℕ → ι → ℚ) → (ℕ → ι → ℚ) → ℕ → ι → ℚ :=
fun {ι} η κ gain decrement t i => η * (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalOptimisticHistory κ gain decrement t i + gain t i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.triangularChoice : (K : ℕ) → Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass K) → Fin K :=
fun x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass x) → Fin x) x
    OAI.EditApproximation.triangularChoice._f x_1
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode : ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J => (d : Fin (J + 1)) × OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M ↑d
def OAI.EditApproximation.finiteRationalOptimizer.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  Finset ι → List (ι → ℚ) → (ι → ℚ) → ℕ → ℕ → ι → ℚ :=
fun {ι} coordinates vertices linear G x =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => ι → ℚ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.finiteRationalOptimizer._f coordinates vertices linear G)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode : ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J => { node // ↑node.fst < J }
def OAI.EditApproximation.finiteOptimizerAction.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    [Fintype β] →
      [DecidableEq β] → [DecidableEq σ] → (band : List (β → σ)) → band ≠ [] → (β → ℚ) → (β × σ → ℚ) → ℕ → ℕ → β → σ :=
fun {β} {σ} [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β] [DecidableEq σ] band hband mass linear G j =>
  have coordinates := OAI.EditApproximation.bandCoordinates band.toFinset;
  have vertices := List.map (OAI.EditApproximation.actionVector mass) band;
  have gradient :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.rationalSmoothedGradient G linear
      (OAI.EditApproximation.finiteRationalOptimizer coordinates vertices linear G j);
  (List.argmax
        (fun action =>
          ∑ coordinate ∈ coordinates, gradient coordinate * OAI.EditApproximation.actionVector mass action coordinate)
        band).getD
    (band.head hband)
def OAI.EditApproximation.finiteOptimizerActionDraw.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    [Fintype β] →
      [DecidableEq β] →
        [DecidableEq σ] →
          (band : List (β → σ)) →
            band ≠ [] →
              (β → ℚ) → (β × σ → ℚ) → ℕ → (K : ℕ) → Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass K) → β → σ :=
fun {β} {σ} [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β] [DecidableEq σ] band hband mass linear G K integer =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.finiteOptimizerAction band hband mass linear G
    ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.triangularChoice K integer)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.width : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a => max a.numerator.bits.length a.denominator.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.denominatorInteger : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary :=
fun a => { negative := false, bits := a.denominator }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
{ numerator := { negative := false, bits := [] }, denominator := [true],
  denominator_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a =>
  have proof_trimBitWordWithWork_value_6 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeWithWork._proof_1;
  have numerator := OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordWithWork a.numerator.bits;
  let denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordWithWork a.denominator;
  ({ numerator := { negative := a.numerator.negative, bits := numerator.1 }, denominator := denominator.1,
      denominator_pos := ⋯ },
    numerator.2 + denominator.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun n =>
  have proof_powerTwoWord_value_13 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo._proof_1;
  { numerator := { negative := false, bits := [true] }, denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord n,
    denominator_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.doubleFraction : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun a =>
  { numerator := { negative := a.numerator.negative, bits := false :: a.numerator.bits }, denominator := a.denominator,
    denominator_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.absolute : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun a =>
  { numerator := { negative := false, bits := a.numerator.bits }, denominator := a.denominator, denominator_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalCeilingWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a =>
  if a.numerator.negative = true then ([], 1)
  else
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivWithWork a.denominator a.numerator.bits;
    (result.1, result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.ofNaturalWord : List Bool → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun bits =>
  { numerator := { negative := false, bits := bits }, denominator := [true],
    denominator_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalFloorWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun a =>
  if a.numerator.negative = true then ([], 1)
  else
    have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork a.denominator a.numerator.bits;
    (quotient.1, quotient.2.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryDataReadBudget.{u_1} : ℕ → ℕ → {κ : Type u_1} → List κ → ℕ :=
fun M n {κ} keys => 1 + keys.length * (24 * max M.size n.size + 9)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork : (ell : List Bool) →
  0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue ell →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) × ℕ :=
fun ell hell =>
  have proof_trimBitWordWithWork_value_6 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitPowerWithWork_value_76 := ⋯;
  have etaPower := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork ell 5;
  let kappaPower := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork ell 4;
  have eta :=
    { numerator := { negative := false, bits := etaPower.1 }, denominator := [true],
      denominator_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 };
  have kappa :=
    { numerator := { negative := false, bits := [true] }, denominator := kappaPower.1, denominator_pos := ⋯ };
  ((eta, kappa), etaPower.2 + kappaPower.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementDecrementWithWork : (ell : List Bool) →
  0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue ell → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun ell hell =>
  have proof_trimBitWordWithWork_value_6 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitPowerWithWork_value_76 := ⋯;
  let denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork ell 10;
  ({ numerator := { negative := false, bits := [true] }, denominator := denominator.1, denominator_pos := ⋯ },
    denominator.2 + 1)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray : Type :=
Option (List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseRootDenominator : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun B q L d => OAI.EditApproximation.coarseDenominatorWithWork (Nat.bits 1) q.bits L.bits B.bits d
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalListEqualWithWork : List ℕ → List ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List ℕ → Bool × ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalListEqualWithWork._f
    x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordAllocation a.numerator.bits +
    OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordAllocation a.denominator
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a => if (OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork a.numerator.bits []).1 = Ordering.eq then 1 else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalCeilingAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a =>
  if a.numerator.negative = true then 0 else OAI.EditApproximation.bitCeilDivAllocation a.denominator a.numerator.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gatheredLookupAllocation.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (κ → κ → Bool × ℕ) → (κ → κ → ℕ) → List κ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → κ → ℕ :=
fun {κ} equal allocation x x_1 x_2 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → κ → ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gatheredLookupAllocation._f equal allocation) x_1 x_2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalFloorAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a =>
  if a.numerator.negative = true then 0 else OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation a.denominator a.numerator.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoAllocation : ℤ → ℕ :=
fun e => if 0 ≤ e then e.toNat + 2 else (-e).toNat + 2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a =>
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork._proof_12;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_eq_3 := ⋯;
  let comparison := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork a.numerator.bits [];
  if h : comparison.1 = Ordering.eq then (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, comparison.2 + 2)
  else
    ({ numerator := { negative := a.numerator.negative, bits := a.denominator }, denominator := a.numerator.bits,
        denominator_pos := ⋯ },
      comparison.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoWithWork : ℤ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun e =>
  if 0 ≤ e then
    ({ numerator := { negative := false, bits := OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord e.toNat }, denominator := [true],
        denominator_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 },
      e.toNat + 3)
  else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo (-e).toNat, (-e).toNat + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.fixedDenominatorWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  (denominator : List Bool) →
    0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue denominator → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a denominator hden =>
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork a.numerator.bits denominator;
  have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork a.denominator product.1;
  have raw :=
    { numerator := { negative := a.numerator.negative, bits := quotient.1 }, denominator := denominator,
      denominator_pos := hden };
  have answer := raw.canonicalizeWithWork;
  (answer.1, product.2 + quotient.2.2 + answer.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finalAnswerWithWork : ℕ →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun N a =>
  have floor := a.naturalFloorWithWork;
  have clamped := OAI.EditApproximation.wordMinWithWork N.bits floor.1;
  (clamped.1, floor.2 + clamped.2 + N.size + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridNatWithWork : (denominator : List Bool) →
  0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue denominator → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun denominator hden n =>
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork n.bits denominator;
  have raw :=
    { numerator := { negative := false, bits := product.1 }, denominator := denominator, denominator_pos := hden };
  have answer := raw.canonicalizeWithWork;
  (answer.1, product.2 + answer.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseBinaryMapWithWork : (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ :=
fun f x =>
  match x with
  | none => (none, 0)
  | some values =>
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork f values;
    (some result.1, result.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.fixedDenominatorAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  (denominator : List Bool) → 0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue denominator → ℕ :=
fun a denominator hden =>
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork a.numerator.bits denominator;
  have quotient := OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModWithWork a.denominator product.1;
  have raw :=
    { numerator := { negative := a.numerator.negative, bits := quotient.1 }, denominator := denominator,
      denominator_pos := hden };
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation a.numerator.bits denominator +
      OAI.EditApproximation.bitDivModAllocation a.denominator product.1 +
    raw.canonicalizeAllocation
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finalAnswerAllocation : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun N a =>
  a.naturalFloorAllocation + OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation N.bits a.naturalFloorWithWork.1 + N.size + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.powerDenominatorWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  (base : List Bool) → 0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a base hbase n =>
  have proof_trimBitWordWithWork_value_6 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitPowerWithWork_value_76 := ⋯;
  let denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork base n;
  have answer := a.fixedDenominatorWithWork denominator.1 ⋯;
  (answer.1, denominator.2 + answer.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.powerDenominatorAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  (base : List Bool) → 0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a base hbase n =>
  have proof_trimBitWordWithWork_value_6 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalizeWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitPowerWithWork_value_76 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation base n +
    a.fixedDenominatorAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork base n).1 ⋯
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ →
  (base : List Bool) → 0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun raw base hbase remaining =>
  have result := raw.1.powerDenominatorWithWork base hbase remaining;
  (result.1, raw.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ →
  ℕ → (base : List Bool) → 0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun raw allocation base hbase remaining => allocation + raw.1.powerDenominatorAllocation base hbase remaining
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild : {M J : ℕ} →
  (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) → ↑node.fst < J → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J :=
fun {M J} node hbelow i => ⟨⟨↑node.fst + 1, ⋯⟩, (node.snd, i)⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.treeLayerCode : (M d : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M d → ℕ :=
fun M x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M x → ℕ) x (OAI.EditApproximation.treeLayerCode._f M)
    x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun M P H => OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass ((H + M * (40 * P + 2) ^ 2 + 2) ^ 80)
def OAI.EditApproximation.treeLayerCodeWithWork : (M d : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M d → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun M x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M x → ℕ × ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.treeLayerCodeWithWork._f M) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryNaturalIndexWithWork : ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun a x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.queryNaturalIndexWithWork._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledSeedWordWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun N pass =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoWithWork
    ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N) pass)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseLetterMatchWithWork : Option (Option ℕ) → Option ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 =>
  match x, x_1 with
  | none, x => (false, 1)
  | some none, none => (true, 1)
  | some (some a), some b => OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a b
  | x, x_2 => (false, 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryNaturalIndexAllocation : ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.queryNaturalIndexAllocation._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalNodeCode : {M J : ℕ} → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J → ℕ :=
fun {M J} node => OAI.EditApproximation.treeLayerCode M (↑node.fst) node.snd + M ^ J * ↑node.fst
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryScaleDispatchWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun N b =>
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N;
  have index := OAI.EditApproximation.queryNaturalIndexWithWork b scales.1;
  (index.1, scales.2 + index.2 + scales.1.length + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryScaleDispatchAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesAllocation N +
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryNaturalIndexAllocation b (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N).1 +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N).1.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun N b =>
  have found := OAI.EditApproximation.queryScaleDispatchWithWork N b;
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork found.1 (max 1 N.bits.length);
  (decide (test.1 = Ordering.lt), found.2 + test.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryScaleDispatchAllocation N b +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.queryScaleDispatchWithWork N b).1.bits.length +
    (max 1 N.bits.length).bits.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex : (N b : ℕ) →
  b ∈ OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N → Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N).length :=
fun N b hb => ⟨List.idxOf b (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N), ⋯⟩
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PositiveDrawRange : ℕ → Type :=
fun B => { n // 0 < ↑n }
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalLocalGroupKey : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J N n exponent =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J ×
    Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N).length × Fin (n * 2 ^ exponent + 1) × Fin (n * 2 ^ exponent + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionSuccessParameter : (N R : ℕ) → 0 < N → N ≤ R → ↑unitInterval :=
fun N R hN hNR => ⟨↑N / ↑R, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledSeedFactor : ℕ → ℕ → ℚ :=
fun N pass =>
  2 ^
    OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N) pass
def OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledSeedMultiplier : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N pass =>
  2 ^
    (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N) pass -
      OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.CommonRangeKey.{u_1} : Type u_1 → ℕ → Type (max u_1 0) :=
fun ι B => ι × OAI.EditApproximation.PositiveDrawRange B
def OAI.EditApproximation.positiveDrawRangeOfLE : (B n : ℕ) →
  0 < n → n ≤ B → OAI.EditApproximation.PositiveDrawRange B :=
fun B n hn hB => ⟨⟨n, ⋯⟩, hn⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.value : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → ℤ :=
fun a => OAI.EditApproximation.signedMagnitude a.negative (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue a.bits)
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.ofInt : ℤ → OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary :=
fun a => { negative := decide (a < 0), bits := a.natAbs.bits }
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.neg : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary :=
fun a => { negative := !a.negative, bits := a.bits }
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.addWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary →
  OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  if a.negative = b.negative then
    have total := OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork a.bits b.bits false;
    ({ negative := a.negative, bits := total.1 }, total.2 + 4)
  else
    have comparison := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork a.bits b.bits;
    match comparison.1 with
    | Ordering.eq => ({ negative := false, bits := [] }, comparison.2 + 4)
    | Ordering.lt =>
      have difference := OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractWithWork b.bits a.bits false;
      ({ negative := b.negative, bits := difference.1 }, comparison.2 + difference.2.2 + 4)
    | Ordering.gt =>
      have difference := OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractWithWork a.bits b.bits false;
      ({ negative := a.negative, bits := difference.1 }, comparison.2 + difference.2.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.mulWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary →
  OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have product := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork a.bits b.bits;
  ({ negative := a.negative ^^ b.negative, bits := product.1 }, product.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.nonzeroWithWork : List Bool → Bool × ℕ :=
fun bits =>
  have comparison := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork bits [];
  (if comparison.1 = Ordering.eq then false else true, comparison.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.addAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary →
  OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  if a.negative = b.negative then OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation a.bits b.bits false
  else
    match (OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork a.bits b.bits).1 with
    | Ordering.eq => 0
    | Ordering.lt => OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation b.bits a.bits false
    | Ordering.gt => OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation a.bits b.bits false
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.mulAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary →
  OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → ℕ :=
fun a b => OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation a.bits b.bits
def OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.ltWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary →
  OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  if a.negative = true then
    if b.negative = true then
      have comparison := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork b.bits a.bits;
      (comparison.1 == Ordering.lt, comparison.2 + 2)
    else
      have left := OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.nonzeroWithWork a.bits;
      have right := OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.nonzeroWithWork b.bits;
      (left.1 || right.1, left.2 + right.2 + 4)
  else
    if b.negative = true then (false, 2)
    else
      have comparison := OAI.EditApproximation.bitCompareWithWork a.bits b.bits;
      (comparison.1 == Ordering.lt, comparison.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.value : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℚ :=
fun a => ↑a.numerator.value / ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue a.denominator)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational :=
fun a => { num := a.numerator.value, den := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue a.denominator, den_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.addWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have first := a.numerator.mulWithWork b.denominatorInteger;
  have second := b.numerator.mulWithWork a.denominatorInteger;
  have numerator := first.1.addWithWork second.1;
  let denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork a.denominator b.denominator;
  ({ numerator := numerator.1, denominator := denominator.1, denominator_pos := ⋯ },
    first.2 + second.2 + numerator.2 + denominator.2 + 8)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.mulWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have numerator := a.numerator.mulWithWork b.numerator;
  let denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulWithWork a.denominator b.denominator;
  ({ numerator := numerator.1, denominator := denominator.1, denominator_pos := ⋯ }, numerator.2 + denominator.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.neg : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun a => { numerator := a.numerator.neg, denominator := a.denominator, denominator_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.ltWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have left := a.numerator.mulWithWork b.denominatorInteger;
  have right := b.numerator.mulWithWork a.denominatorInteger;
  have compared := left.1.ltWithWork right.1;
  (compared.1, left.2 + right.2 + compared.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun n =>
  { numerator := OAI.EditApproximation.SignedBinary.ofInt ↑n, denominator := [true],
    denominator_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridAddWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have total := a.numerator.addWithWork b.numerator;
  have raw := { numerator := total.1, denominator := a.denominator, denominator_pos := ⋯ };
  have answer := raw.canonicalizeWithWork;
  (answer.1, total.2 + answer.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseReadShiftWithWork : (k : ℕ) →
  ℕ → List ℕ → Option (Option ℕ) → ℕ → Fin (2 * k + 1) → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun k n target letter start shift =>
  match letter with
  | none => (0, 1)
  | some letter =>
    have position := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork start ↑shift;
    have actual :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.coarseFramedReadWithWork k n target (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue position.1);
    have matched := OAI.EditApproximation.coarseLetterMatchWithWork actual.1 letter;
    (if matched.1 = true then 0 else 1, position.2 + actual.2 + matched.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.addAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  a.numerator.mulAllocation b.denominatorInteger + b.numerator.mulAllocation a.denominatorInteger +
      (a.numerator.mulWithWork b.denominatorInteger).1.addAllocation (b.numerator.mulWithWork a.denominatorInteger).1 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation a.denominator b.denominator
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.mulAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.numerator.mulAllocation b.numerator + OAI.EditApproximation.bitMulAllocation a.denominator b.denominator
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.ltAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.numerator.mulAllocation b.denominatorInteger + b.numerator.mulAllocation a.denominatorInteger
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalAddWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have raw := a.addWithWork b;
  have result := raw.1.canonicalizeWithWork;
  (result.1, raw.2 + result.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalMulWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have raw := a.mulWithWork b;
  have result := raw.1.canonicalizeWithWork;
  (result.1, raw.2 + result.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dyadicScanWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a candidate x =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dyadicScanWithWork._f a) candidate
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.maxWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have comparison := a.ltWithWork b;
  (if comparison.1 = true then b else a, comparison.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.leWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have result := b.ltWithWork a;
  (!result.1, result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.minWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have comparison := a.ltWithWork b;
  (if comparison.1 = true then a else b, comparison.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScaleAt : (words : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
  (scales : List ℚ) →
    List.map OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.value words = scales →
      Fin scales.length → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun words scales h i => words[↑i]
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gatheredWordLookupWithWork.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (κ → κ → Bool × ℕ) →
    List κ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {κ} equal x x_1 x_2 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gatheredWordLookupWithWork._f equal) x_1 x_2
