def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridZipAddWithWork : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) x
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridZipAddWithWork._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseBinaryRead : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun array i =>
  match array with
  | none => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0
  | some cells => cells[i]?.getD (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalAddAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.addAllocation b + (a.addWithWork b).1.canonicalizeAllocation
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.canonicalMulAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.mulAllocation b + (a.mulWithWork b).1.canonicalizeAllocation
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.minAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.ltAllocation b
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.maxAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.ltAllocation b
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.leAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => b.ltAllocation a
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dyadicScanAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a candidate x =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dyadicScanAllocation._f a) candidate
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.equalAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => b.ltAllocation a + a.ltAllocation b + 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.subWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have result := a.canonicalAddWithWork b.neg;
  (result.1, result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.divWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have inverse := b.invWithWork;
  have result := a.canonicalMulWithWork inverse.1;
  (result.1, inverse.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.powWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.powWithWork._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → List (Fin d) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {d} left right x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListWithWork._f left right)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dyadicCeilingWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a =>
  have result := a.dyadicScanWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo a.width) (2 * a.width);
  (result.1, result.2 + 3 * a.width + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.maximumWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun fallback x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.maximumWithWork._f fallback)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.minimumWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun fallback x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.minimumWithWork._f fallback)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledCeilingWithWork : ℕ →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun multiplier a =>
  have product := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat multiplier).canonicalMulWithWork a;
  have result := product.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  (result.1, product.2 + result.2 + multiplier.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseContributionWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun threshold scale a =>
  have test := threshold.leWithWork a;
  if test.1 = true then
    have result := a.maxWithWork scale;
    (result.1, test.2 + result.2 + 2)
  else
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridNatWithWork a.denominator ⋯ 0;
    (result.1, test.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridScanWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun two previous x =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ)
    x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridScanWithWork._f two) previous
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridZipMinimumWithWork : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) x
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridZipMinimumWithWork._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseBinaryAddWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 =>
  match x, x_1 with
  | none, right => (right, 0)
  | left, none => (left, 0)
  | some left, some right =>
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridZipAddWithWork left right;
    (some result.1, result.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.divAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => b.invAllocation + a.canonicalMulAllocation b.invWithWork.1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.subAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.canonicalAddAllocation b.neg
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimizerUpdateCellAllocation : {d : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Fin d → ℕ :=
fun {d} step complement previous selected i =>
  complement.canonicalMulAllocation (previous.get i) + step.canonicalMulAllocation (selected.get i) +
    (complement.canonicalMulWithWork (previous.get i)).1.canonicalAddAllocation
      (step.canonicalMulWithWork (selected.get i)).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dyadicCeilingAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a =>
  a.dyadicScanAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo a.width) (2 * a.width) + a.width + 2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.roundedSpacingAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun theta envelope =>
  have product := (theta.canonicalMulWithWork envelope).1;
  theta.canonicalMulAllocation envelope + OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.maxAllocation product +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.maxWithWork product).1.naturalCeilingAllocation
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledCeilingAllocation : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun multiplier a =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat multiplier).canonicalMulAllocation a +
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat multiplier).canonicalMulWithWork a).1.naturalCeilingAllocation +
      multiplier.size +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupTermsAllocation : {M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → List (Fin M) → ℕ :=
fun {M} h envelope indices =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun i => (envelope.get i).equalAllocation h + 2) indices
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.logarithmPartialWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun b x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.logarithmPartialWithWork._f b)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {d} left right => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListWithWork left right (List.finRange d)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.distanceWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have difference := a.subWithWork b;
  (difference.1.absolute, difference.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.affineErrorWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun position offset velocity prediction tolerance =>
  have displacement := position.subWithWork offset;
  have product := velocity.canonicalMulWithWork prediction;
  have error := displacement.1.subWithWork product.1;
  have comparison := error.1.absolute.leWithWork tolerance;
  (comparison.1, displacement.2 + product.2 + error.2 + comparison.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandToleranceWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun tau theta b M =>
  have product := tau.canonicalMulWithWork theta;
  have scale := product.1.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have result := scale.1.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M);
  (result.1, product.2 + scale.2 + result.2 + b.size + M.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.medianSeedWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun N multiplier values =>
  have rounded :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledCeilingWithWork multiplier)
      values;
  have sorted := OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortWithWork rounded.1;
  have middle := sorted.1.getD (values.length / 2) [];
  have clamped := OAI.EditApproximation.wordMinWithWork N.bits middle;
  (clamped.1, rounded.2 + sorted.2 + values.length + clamped.2 + N.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupSampleCountWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun M Q b h =>
  have qm :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat Q).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M);
  have numerator := qm.1.canonicalMulWithWork h;
  have ratio := numerator.1.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have ceiling := ratio.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.wordMinWithWork M.bits ceiling.1;
  (result.1, qm.2 + numerator.2 + ratio.2 + ceiling.2 + result.2 + M.size + Q.size + b.size + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read s i =>
  have word := read s i;
  have result := word.1.neg.divWithWork (mass i.1);
  (result.1, word.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.decrementReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read s i =>
  have first := read s i;
  have second := read (s + 1) i;
  have difference := first.1.subWithWork second.1;
  have result := difference.1.divWithWork (mass i.1);
  (result.1, first.2 + second.2 + difference.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledSeedWithWork : ℕ →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun U a gap =>
  have ratio := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).divWithWork a;
  have rounded := ratio.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have result := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.ofNaturalWord rounded.1).maxWithWork gap.absolute;
  (result.1, ratio.2 + rounded.2 + result.2 + U.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun U b A =>
  have square := A.canonicalMulWithWork A;
  have denominator := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulWithWork square.1;
  have lower := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).divWithWork denominator.1;
  have factor := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork A;
  have upper := factor.1.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U);
  have low := lower.1.leWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have high := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).leWithWork upper.1;
  (low.1 && high.1, square.2 + denominator.2 + lower.2 + factor.2 + upper.2 + low.2 + high.2 + U.size + b.size + 7)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCenterGuardWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun b U A =>
  have lower := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2);
  have factor := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork A;
  have upper := factor.1.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have low := lower.1.leWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U);
  have high := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).leWithWork upper.1;
  (low.1 && high.1, lower.2 + factor.2 + upper.2 + low.2 + high.2 + b.size + U.size + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCoordinatesWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun side p radius =>
  have lower := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork p radius;
  have upper := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork p radius;
  have firstQ :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower.1)).divWithWork side;
  have lastQ :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper.1)).divWithWork side;
  have first := firstQ.1.naturalFloorWithWork;
  have last := lastQ.1.naturalFloorWithWork;
  have stop := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue last.1) 1;
  have count :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue stop.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first.1);
  have values := List.range' (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue count.1);
  (values,
    lower.2 + upper.2 + firstQ.2 + lastQ.2 + first.2 + last.2 + stop.2 + count.2 + values.length + p.size +
        radius.size +
      9)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellGridPointsWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun side g cell =>
  have next := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork cell 1;
  have lower := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat cell).canonicalMulWithWork side;
  have upper :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next.1)).canonicalMulWithWork side;
  have firstQ := lower.1.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat g);
  have lastQ := upper.1.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat g);
  have first := firstQ.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have last := lastQ.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have count :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue last.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first.1);
  have indices := List.range' (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue count.1);
  have points :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun i =>
        have product := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork g i;
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue product.1, product.2 + i.size + 2))
      indices;
  (points.1,
    next.2 + lower.2 + upper.2 + firstQ.2 + lastQ.2 + first.2 + last.2 + count.2 + points.2 + indices.length +
          cell.size +
        g.size +
      10)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun b F a initial =>
  have denominator := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork a;
  have lower := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divWithWork denominator.1;
  have factor :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F);
  have upper := factor.1.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have low := lower.1.leWithWork initial;
  have high := initial.leWithWork upper.1;
  (low.1 && high.1, denominator.2 + lower.2 + factor.2 + upper.2 + low.2 + high.2 + b.size + F.size + 8)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun F b a w =>
  have denominator :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F);
  have lower := w.divWithWork denominator.1;
  have factor := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulWithWork a;
  have upper := factor.1.canonicalMulWithWork w;
  have low := lower.1.leWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have high := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).leWithWork upper.1;
  (low.1 && high.1, denominator.2 + lower.2 + factor.2 + upper.2 + low.2 + high.2 + b.size + F.size + 8)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseDivideWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun a divisor =>
  have quotient := a.divWithWork divisor;
  have result := quotient.1.fixedDenominatorWithWork a.denominator ⋯;
  (result.1, quotient.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridLeftWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun two x =>
  match x with
  | [] => ([], 0)
  | head :: rest => two.coarseGridScanWithWork head rest
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseMinimumWithWork : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | [] => (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0, 1)
  | a :: rest => a.minimumWithWork rest
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.powAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.powAllocation._f a)

fun {d} left right x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListAllocation._f left right)

  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=

def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.distanceAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun a b => a.subAllocation b + 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimizerUpdateAllocation : {d : ℕ} →
  ℕ → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → ℕ :=
fun {d} j previous selected =>
  have step :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (j + 2))).1;
  have complement := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subWithWork step).1;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (j + 2)) +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subAllocation step +
        OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation d (step.optimizerUpdateCellAllocation complement previous selected) +
      (j + 2).size +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.affineErrorAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun position offset velocity prediction tolerance =>
  have displacement := (position.subWithWork offset).1;
  have product := (velocity.canonicalMulWithWork prediction).1;
  position.subAllocation offset + velocity.canonicalMulAllocation prediction + displacement.subAllocation product +
    (displacement.subWithWork product).1.absolute.leAllocation tolerance
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandToleranceAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun tau theta b M =>
  have product := (tau.canonicalMulWithWork theta).1;
  have scale := (product.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
  tau.canonicalMulAllocation theta + product.canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
        scale.divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M) +
      b.size +
    M.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun U b A =>
  have square := (A.canonicalMulWithWork A).1;
  have denominator := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulWithWork square).1;
  have lower := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).divWithWork denominator).1;
  have factor := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork A).1;
  have upper := (factor.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U)).1;
  A.canonicalMulAllocation A + (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulAllocation square +
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).divAllocation denominator +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulAllocation A +
            factor.canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U) +
          lower.leAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).leAllocation upper +
      U.size +
    b.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCenterGuardAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun b U A =>
  have lower :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2)).1;
  have factor := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork A).1;
  have upper := (factor.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2) +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulAllocation A +
            factor.canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
          lower.leAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U) +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).leAllocation upper +
      b.size +
    U.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledSeedAllocation : ℕ →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun U a gap =>
  have ratio := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).divWithWork a).1;
  have rounded := ratio.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat U).divAllocation a + ratio.naturalCeilingAllocation +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.ofNaturalWord rounded).maxAllocation gap.absolute +
      U.size +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun b F a initial =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulAllocation a +
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divAllocation
                  ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork a).1 +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulAllocation
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F) +
            ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulWithWork
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1.canonicalMulAllocation
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
          ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divWithWork
                  ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 4).canonicalMulWithWork a).1).1.leAllocation
            initial +
        initial.leAllocation
          (((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulWithWork
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1.canonicalMulWithWork
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1 +
      b.size +
    F.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun F b a w =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F) +
                w.divAllocation
                  ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulWithWork
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1 +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulAllocation a +
            ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulWithWork a).1.canonicalMulAllocation w +
          (w.divWithWork
                  ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 16).canonicalMulWithWork
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1).1.leAllocation
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).leAllocation
          (((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 8).canonicalMulWithWork a).1.canonicalMulWithWork w).1 +
      b.size +
    F.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCoordinatesAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun side p radius =>
  have lower := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork p radius).1;
  have upper := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork p radius).1;
  have firstQ :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower)).divWithWork side).1;
  have lastQ :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper)).divWithWork side).1;
  have first := firstQ.naturalFloorWithWork.1;
  have last := lastQ.naturalFloorWithWork.1;
  have stop := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue last) 1).1;
  have count :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue stop)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation p radius +
                      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation p radius +
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower)).divAllocation
                      side +
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper)).divAllocation
                    side +
                firstQ.naturalFloorAllocation +
              lastQ.naturalFloorAllocation +
            OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue last) 1 +
          OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue stop)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first) +
        OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue count +
      p.size +
    radius.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellGridPointsAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun side g cell =>
  have next := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork cell 1).1;
  have lower := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat cell).canonicalMulWithWork side).1;
  have upper :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next)).canonicalMulWithWork side).1;
  have firstQ := (lower.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat g)).1;
  have lastQ := (upper.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat g)).1;
  have first := firstQ.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  have last := lastQ.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  have count :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue last)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first)).1;
  have indices := List.range' (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue count);
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation cell 1 +
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat cell).canonicalMulAllocation side +
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next)).canonicalMulAllocation
                        side +
                    lower.divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat g) +
                  upper.divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat g) +
                firstQ.naturalCeilingAllocation +
              lastQ.naturalCeilingAllocation +
            OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue last)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue first) +
          OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
            (fun i => OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation g i + i.size) indices +
        indices.length +
      cell.size +
    g.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.medianSeedAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun N multiplier values =>
  have rounded :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledCeilingWithWork multiplier)
        values).1;
  have sorted := (OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortWithWork rounded).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledCeilingAllocation multiplier) values +
        OAI.EditApproximation.wordSortAllocation rounded +
      OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEAllocation N.bits (sorted.getD (values.length / 2) []) +
    N.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupSampleCountAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun M Q b h =>
  have qm :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat Q).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M)).1;
  have numerator := (qm.canonicalMulWithWork h).1;
  have ratio := (numerator.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat Q).canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M) +
                qm.canonicalMulAllocation h +
              numerator.divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
            ratio.naturalCeilingAllocation +
          OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEAllocation M.bits ratio.naturalCeilingWithWork.1 +
        M.size +
      Q.size +
    b.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementUpdateAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Option OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun delta cone x =>
  match x with
  | none => 0
  | some online =>
    have denominator := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.canonicalAddWithWork delta).1;
    have lower := (cone.divWithWork denominator).1;
    have clipped := (lower.maxWithWork online).1;
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.canonicalAddAllocation delta + cone.divAllocation denominator +
        lower.maxAllocation online +
      cone.minAllocation clipped
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coefficientAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun kappa =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulAllocation kappa +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subAllocation
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulWithWork kappa).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → (ℕ → β × σ → ℕ) → ℕ → β × σ → ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read footprint s i => footprint s i + (read s i).1.neg.divAllocation (mass i.1) + 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.decrementReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → (ℕ → β × σ → ℕ) → ℕ → β × σ → ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read footprint s i =>
  footprint s i + footprint (s + 1) i + (read s i).1.subAllocation (read (s + 1) i).1 +
      ((read s i).1.subWithWork (read (s + 1) i).1).1.divAllocation (mass i.1) +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.logarithmWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun r k =>
  have numerator := r.subWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one;
  have denominator := r.canonicalAddWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one;
  have ratio := numerator.1.divWithWork denominator.1;
  have subtotal := ratio.1.logarithmPartialWithWork k;
  have result := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulWithWork subtotal.1;
  (result.1, numerator.2 + denominator.2 + ratio.2 + subtotal.2 + result.2 + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimisticTermReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read coefficient s i =>
  have gain := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadWithWork mass read s i;
  have decrement := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.decrementReadWithWork mass read s i;
  have scaled := coefficient.canonicalMulWithWork decrement.1;
  have total := gain.1.canonicalAddWithWork scaled.1;
  (total.1, gain.2 + decrement.2 + scaled.2 + total.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun N F a w =>
  have count := max 1 N.bits.length;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
      (fun e =>
        have guard := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardWithWork F (2 ^ e) a w;
        (guard.1, guard.2 + e + 2))
      (List.range count);
  (selected.1, selected.2 + count + N.bits.length + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.seedMedianWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun N multiplier values =>
  have median := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.medianSeedWithWork N multiplier values;
  have trimmed := OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordWithWork median.1;
  (trimmed.1, median.2 + trimmed.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridPenaltyWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun two source =>
  have left := two.coarseGridLeftWithWork source;
  have right := two.coarseGridLeftWithWork source.reverse;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseGridZipMinimumWithWork left.1 right.1.reverse;
  (result.1, left.2 + right.2 + result.2 + 3 * source.length)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseChildThresholdWithWork : (B : ℕ) →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Fin B → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun B q allowance draw =>
  have numerator := allowance.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (↑draw + 1));
  have divisor :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat B).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q);
  have result := numerator.1.coarseDivideWithWork divisor.1;
  (result.1, numerator.2 + divisor.2 + result.2 + (↑draw + 1).size + B.size + q.size + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseChildAllowanceWithWork : ℕ →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun L threshold =>
  have result := threshold.coarseDivideWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat L);
  (result.1, result.2 + L.size + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.logarithmPartialAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun b x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.logarithmPartialAllocation._f b)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassAllocation : {M : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → ℕ :=
fun {M} mass =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListAllocation mass
      (Vector.replicate M OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one) (List.finRange M) +
    2 * M
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun values =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListAllocation (Vector.ofFn values.get)
      (Vector.replicate values.length OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one) (List.finRange values.length) +
    3 * values.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalDistanceLeAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun a b radius =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat a).distanceAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
          ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat a).distanceWithWork
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1.leAllocation
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat radius) +
        a.size +
      b.size +
    radius.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun N F a w =>
  have count := max 1 N.bits.length;
  OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation
        (fun e =>
          ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardWithWork F (2 ^ e) a w).1,
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardWithWork F (2 ^ e) a w).2 + e + 2))
        (fun e => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardAllocation F (2 ^ e) a w + e + 1)
        (List.range count) +
      count +
    N.bits.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.seedMedianAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun N multiplier values =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.medianSeedAllocation N multiplier values +
      OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.medianSeedWithWork N multiplier values).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineMinimumAllocation : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | [] => 0
  | first :: rest => first.minimumAllocation rest
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimisticTermReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (ℕ → β × σ → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → β × σ → ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read footprint coefficient s i =>
  have gain := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadWithWork mass read s i).1;
  have decrement := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.decrementReadWithWork mass read s i).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadAllocation mass read footprint s i +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.decrementReadAllocation mass read footprint s i +
        coefficient.canonicalMulAllocation decrement +
      gain.canonicalAddAllocation (coefficient.canonicalMulWithWork decrement).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientCellWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Fin d → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {d} G linear point i =>
  have smoothing := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat G).powWithWork 50;
  have inverse := smoothing.1.invWithWork;
  have shifted := (point.get i).canonicalAddWithWork inverse.1;
  have logarithm := shifted.1.logarithmWithWork (G ^ 60);
  have deficit := logarithm.1.neg.subWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one;
  have result := deficit.1.canonicalAddWithWork (linear.get i);
  (result.1, smoothing.2 + inverse.2 + shifted.2 + logarithm.2 + deficit.2 + result.2 + G.size + 70)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List ℕ × ℕ :=
fun N F a value =>
  have exponents := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsWithWork N F a value;
  have scales :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun e => (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.powerTwoWord e), e + 3)) exponents.1;
  (scales.1, exponents.2 + scales.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.querySeedMedianWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun N multiplier words =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.seedMedianWithWork N multiplier words;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.ofNaturalWord result.1, result.2 + result.1.length + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseBinaryPenaltyWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ :=
fun two x =>
  match x with
  | none => (none, 0)
  | some values =>
    have result := two.coarseGridPenaltyWithWork values;
    (some result.1, result.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.logarithmAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun r n =>
  have numerator := (r.subWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one).1;
  have denominator := (r.canonicalAddWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one).1;
  have ratio := (numerator.divWithWork denominator).1;
  r.subAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one +
          r.canonicalAddAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one +
        numerator.divAllocation denominator +
      ratio.logarithmPartialAllocation n +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulAllocation (ratio.logarithmPartialWithWork n).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun N F a value =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsAllocation N F a value +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun e => e + 1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsWithWork N F a value).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseIndexChildren : {B : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          (Fin B → ℕ) →
            (Fin B →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ) →
              (Fin B → Fin B) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                  List (Fin B) → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ :=
fun {B} k q L two width evaluate draw allowance x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseIndexChildren._f k q L two width evaluate draw allowance) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientCellAllocation : {d : ℕ} →
  ℕ → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Fin d → ℕ :=
fun {d} G linear point i =>
  have smoothing := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat G).powWithWork 50).1;
  have inverse := smoothing.invWithWork.1;
  have shifted := ((point.get i).canonicalAddWithWork inverse).1;
  have logarithm := (shifted.logarithmWithWork (G ^ 60)).1;
  have deficit := (logarithm.neg.subWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one).1;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat G).powAllocation 50 + smoothing.invAllocation +
              (point.get i).canonicalAddAllocation inverse +
            shifted.logarithmAllocation (G ^ 60) +
          logarithm.neg.subAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one +
        deficit.canonicalAddAllocation (linear.get i) +
      G.size +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseIndexedWithWork : {B : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          List ℕ →
            List ℕ →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                (d : ℕ) →
                  ℕ →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B d →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                        ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ :=
fun {B} k q L n source target two x x_1 x_2 x_3 x_4 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B x →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.CoarseBinaryArray × ℕ)
    x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coarseIndexedWithWork._f k q L n source target two) x_1 x_2 x_3 x_4
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientAllocation : {d : ℕ} →
  ℕ → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → ℕ :=
fun {d} G linear point =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation d
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientCellAllocation G linear point)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d × ℕ :=
fun {d} G linear point =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork d (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientCellWithWork G linear point)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → List α → Option (α × OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) × ℕ :=
fun {α} score x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxWithWork._f score)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimizerUpdateWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d × ℕ :=
fun {d} j previous selected =>
  have step :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (j + 2));
  have complement := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subWithWork step.1;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork d fun i =>
      have first := complement.1.canonicalMulWithWork (previous.get i);
      have second := step.1.canonicalMulWithWork (selected.get i);
      have total := first.1.canonicalAddWithWork second.1;
      (total.1, first.2 + second.2 + total.2 + 4);
  (result.1, step.2 + complement.2 + result.2 + (j + 2).size + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassTotalWithWork : {M : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M} mass =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotWithWork mass (Vector.replicate M OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coefficientWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun kappa =>
  have scaled := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulWithWork kappa;
  have difference := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subWithWork scaled.1;
  (difference.1, scaled.2 + difference.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalDistanceLeWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b radius =>
  have distance :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat a).distanceWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have result := distance.1.leWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat radius);
  (result.1, distance.2 + result.2 + a.size + b.size + radius.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineMinimumWithWork : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  Option OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | [] => (none, 1)
  | first :: rest =>
    have result := first.minimumWithWork rest;
    (some result.1, result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementUpdateWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    Option OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun delta cone x =>
  match x with
  | none => (cone, 1)
  | some online =>
    have denominator := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.canonicalAddWithWork delta;
    have lower := cone.divWithWork denominator.1;
    have clipped := lower.1.maxWithWork online;
    have result := cone.minWithWork clipped.1;
    (result.1, denominator.2 + lower.2 + clipped.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.equalWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun a b =>
  have left := a.leWithWork b;
  have right := b.leWithWork a;
  (left.1 && right.1, left.2 + right.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.roundedSpacingWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun theta envelope =>
  have product := theta.canonicalMulWithWork envelope;
  have clipped := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.maxWithWork product.1;
  have rounded := clipped.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  (rounded.1, product.2 + clipped.2 + rounded.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.maximizerWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d →
    List (Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d × ℕ :=
fun {d} gradient vertices =>
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxWithWork
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotWithWork gradient) vertices;
  ((Option.map Prod.fst selected.1).getD (Vector.replicate d OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero),
    selected.2 + d + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupTermsWithWork : {M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → List (Fin M) → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M} h envelope values indices =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
    (fun i =>
      have test := (envelope.get i).equalWithWork h;
      (if test.1 = true then values.get i else OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, test.2 + 2))
    indices
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.selectGroupWithWork.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  (members : List ι) → members ≠ [] → (ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → ι × ℕ :=
fun {ι} members hne estimate =>
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxWithWork
      (fun member =>
        have result := estimate member;
        (result.1.neg, result.2 + 1))
      members;
  ((Option.map Prod.fst selected.1).getD (members.head hne), selected.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finiteOptimizerWithWork : {d : ℕ} →
  List (Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → ℕ → ℕ → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d × ℕ :=
fun {d} vertices linear G x =>
  Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finiteOptimizerWithWork._f vertices linear G)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.selectionWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {d : ℕ} →
    List (α × Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → ℕ → ℕ → Option α × ℕ :=
fun {α} {d} vertices linear G j =>
  have iterate := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finiteOptimizerWithWork (List.map Prod.snd vertices) linear G j;
  have gradient := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientWithWork G linear iterate.1;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxWithWork
      (fun vertex => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotWithWork gradient.1 vertex.2) vertices;
  (Option.map (fun chosen => chosen.1.1) selected.1, iterate.2 + gradient.2 + selected.2 + 1)
