def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    {β : Type u_3} →
      (κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
        List κ →
          (List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β :=
fun {ι} {κ} {β} reader x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    (List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β)
    x (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect._f reader) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.normalize.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    (base : List Bool) →
      0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base →
        ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {ι} program base hbase remaining =>
  program.bind fun word =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedWithWork (word, 0) base hbase remaining)
def OAI.EditApproximation.substring.{u} : {α : Type u} → List α → ℕ → ℕ → List α :=
fun {α} word lo hi => List.drop lo (List.take hi word)
structure OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval (length : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 1
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval.lo : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval.hi : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval.ordered : self.lo ≤ self.hi
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval.valid : self.hi ≤ length
constructor:
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval.mk {length : ℕ} (lo hi : ℕ) (ordered : lo ≤ hi) (valid : hi ≤ length) :
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval length
def OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceDeficit.{u} : {α : Type u} → [DecidableEq α] → α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] a word => word.length - if a ∈ word then 1 else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.natListMinimum : ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ :=
fun fallback x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.natListMinimum._f fallback)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePadTo.{u} : {α : Type u} → ℕ → List α → List (Option α) :=
fun {α} n word => List.map some word ++ List.replicate (n - word.length) none
def OAI.EditApproximation.connectionBudget : {n : ℕ} → ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {n} start states finish =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun states => ℕ → ℕ) states (OAI.EditApproximation.connectionBudget._f finish) start
def OAI.EditApproximation.singletonCost.{u} : {α : Type u} → [DecidableEq α] → α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] a word => if word = [] then 1 else OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceDeficit a word
def OAI.EditApproximation.makeTargetState : (n : ℕ) → ℕ → ℕ → Option (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n lo hi => if h : lo ≤ hi ∧ hi ≤ n then some { lo := lo, hi := hi, ordered := ⋯, valid := ⋯ } else none
def OAI.EditApproximation.targetEndpointDistance : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} q r => q.lo.dist r.lo + q.hi.dist r.hi
def OAI.EditApproximation.targetStates : (n : ℕ) → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n =>
  List.flatMap (fun lo => List.filterMap (fun hi => OAI.EditApproximation.makeTargetState n lo hi) (List.range (n + 1)))
    (List.range (n + 1))
def OAI.EditApproximation.shortSourceCost.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → List α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] source target =>
  match source with
  | [] => target.length
  | a :: tail => OAI.EditApproximation.singletonCost a target
def OAI.EditApproximation.exactSuffixDP.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → List α → List α → List ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List α → List ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.exactSuffixDP._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.exactSuffixAnswer.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} → [DecidableEq α] → List α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] source target => (OAI.EditApproximation.exactSuffixDP source target).getD 0 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n :=
fun {n M} parent child =>
  have proof_balancedCut_monotone_7 := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild._proof_1;
  have proof_balancedCut_last_9 := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild._proof_3;
  have proof_balancedCut_bounds_8 := ⋯;
  { lo := OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M ↑child,
    hi := OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M (↑child + 1), ordered := ⋯, valid := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n :=
fun {n} spacing state =>
  have proof_gridRound_mono_10 := OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval._proof_1;
  have proof_gridRound_le_11 := ⋯;
  { lo := OAI.EditApproximation.gridRound spacing state.lo, hi := OAI.EditApproximation.gridRound spacing state.hi,
    ordered := ⋯, valid := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetStates : (n : ℕ) →
  ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n g radius q =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun lo =>
      List.filterMap (fun hi => OAI.EditApproximation.makeTargetState n lo hi)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween g (q.hi - radius) (q.hi + radius)))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween g (q.lo - radius) (q.lo + radius))
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalStateGap.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  List α → (target : List α) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ :=
fun {α} source target q => ↑q.hi - ↑q.lo - ↑source.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBox : (n : ℕ) → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n g radius lo hi =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun s =>
      List.filterMap (fun t => OAI.EditApproximation.makeTargetState n s t)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween g (hi - radius) (hi + radius)))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween g (lo - radius) (lo + radius))
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J ny => OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny
def OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork : (n : ℕ) → ℕ → ℕ → Option (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun n lo hi =>
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork._proof_12;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  let first := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork lo.bits hi.bits;
  let second := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork hi.bits n.bits;
  if h : (first.1 && second.1) = true then
    (some { lo := lo, hi := hi, ordered := ⋯, valid := ⋯ }, first.2 + second.2 + 4)
  else (none, first.2 + second.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.cellTargetStates : (n : ℕ) → ℕ → ℚ → ℕ × ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n g side cell =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun lo =>
      List.filterMap (fun hi => OAI.EditApproximation.makeTargetState n lo hi)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.cellGridPoints side g cell.2))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.cellGridPoints side g cell.1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.gridGapStates : (n : ℕ) → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n g anchor radius sourceLength gapRadius =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun lo =>
      List.filterMap (fun hi => OAI.EditApproximation.makeTargetState n lo hi)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween g (lo + sourceLength - gapRadius) (lo + sourceLength + gapRadius)))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsBetween g (anchor - radius) (anchor + radius))
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalChildTables : ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M ny => Fin M → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalRationalAction : ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M ny =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.RationalBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
    (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M
def OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetCells : {n : ℕ} → ℚ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × ℕ) :=
fun {n} side radius q =>
  List.flatMap (fun lo => List.map (fun hi => (lo, hi)) (OAI.EditApproximation.localCellCoordinates side q.hi radius))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.localCellCoordinates side q.lo radius)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {M n} a b =>
  have child := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork ↑a.1 ↑b.1;
  have lo := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a.2.lo b.2.lo;
  have hi := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a.2.hi b.2.hi;
  (child.1 && lo.1 && hi.1, child.2 + lo.2 + hi.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {n} a b =>
  have lo := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a.lo b.lo;
  have hi := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a.hi b.hi;
  (lo.1 && hi.1, lo.2 + hi.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.childInputCode : {M n : ℕ} → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} input => ↑input.1 + M * (input.2.lo + (n + 1) * input.2.hi)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeWithWork : {M n : ℕ} → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M n} input =>
  have width := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork n 1;
  have high := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width.1) input.2.hi;
  have coordinate :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork input.2.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue high.1);
  have scaled := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork M (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue coordinate.1);
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (↑input.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue scaled.1);
  (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue result.1, width.2 + high.2 + coordinate.2 + scaled.2 + result.2 + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.querySliceHeadWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source : List α) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length → Option α × ℕ :=
fun {α} source interval =>
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork interval.lo interval.hi;
  (if test.1 = Ordering.lt then source[interval.lo]? else none, test.2 + interval.lo.size + interval.hi.size + 3)
@[irreducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedBitProgramValue.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery { child // rank child < rank key } OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {κ} rank program =>
  WellFounded.Nat.fix (fun x => rank x) fun key a =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.value (fun child => a ↑child ⋯) (program key)
def OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledRefinementWordWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun N pass =>
  if pass < OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N then
    have e :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N) pass -
        OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N;
    have value := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoWithWork ↑e;
    (value.1, value.2 + 5)
  else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 1, 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} a b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation ↑a.1 ↑b.1 + OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation a.2.lo b.2.lo +
      OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation a.2.hi b.2.hi +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} a b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation a.lo b.lo + OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation a.hi b.hi + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildAllocation : {n M : ℕ} → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {n M} parent i =>
  have width := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork parent.hi parent.lo).1;
  have next := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (↑i) 1).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation parent.hi parent.lo +
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (↑i) 1 +
        OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCutAllocation parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width) M ↑i +
      OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCutAllocation parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width) M
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next) +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.zeroConnectionAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {n} start x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => ℕ → ℕ → ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.zeroConnectionAllocation._f start x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeAllocation : {M n : ℕ} → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} input =>
  have width := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork n 1).1;
  have high := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width) input.2.hi).1;
  have coordinate :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork input.2.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue high)).1;
  have scaled := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork M (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue coordinate)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation n 1 +
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width) input.2.hi +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation input.2.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue high) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation M (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue coordinate) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (↑input.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue scaled)
def OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateAllocation : {n : ℕ} → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} spacing q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryGridRoundAllocation spacing q.lo +
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryGridRoundAllocation spacing q.hi +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.actionEqualAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} a b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.allAllocation
    (fun i =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation (a i).lo (b i).lo +
        OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation (a i).hi (b i).hi)
    (List.finRange M)
structure OAI.EditApproximation.RawUnskippedBand (M ny : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 2
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.RawUnskippedBand.center : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny
  OAI.EditApproximation.RawUnskippedBand.actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
  OAI.EditApproximation.RawUnskippedBand.nonempty : self.actions ≠ []
constructor:
  OAI.EditApproximation.RawUnskippedBand.mk {M ny : ℕ} (center : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
    (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) (nonempty : actions ≠ []) :
    OAI.EditApproximation.RawUnskippedBand M ny
def OAI.EditApproximation.sourceIntervalAt : (M n d : ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M d → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n :=
fun M n x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.TreeLayer M x → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceIntervalAt._f M n) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.initialConeValue.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  List α →
    (target : List α) →
      (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
        ℚ →
          ℚ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ :=
fun {α} source target U a L0 query center =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.scaledSeedValue (U center) a (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalStateGap source target center) +
    L0 * ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.targetEndpointDistance query center)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildStateList : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ → ℕ → Fin M → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b P i =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBox ny (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) (10 * b)
    (r.lo + ((OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo - parent.lo))
    (r.lo + ((OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi - parent.lo))
def OAI.EditApproximation.bandCutShift : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → Fin M → ℚ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent states j => ↑(states j).lo - ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent j).lo
def OAI.EditApproximation.localInitialConeCenters : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℚ →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P A U q =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun b =>
      List.filter (fun r => decide (↑b / 2 ≤ ↑(U r) ∧ ↑(U r) ≤ 4 * A * ↑b))
        (OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetStates n (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) (U q) q))
    (List.filter (fun b => decide (↑(U q) / (16 * A ^ 2) ≤ ↑b ∧ ↑b ≤ 4 * A * ↑(U q)))
      (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N))
def OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork : (n : ℕ) →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun n g radius lo hi =>
  have leftLow := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork lo radius;
  have leftHigh := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork lo radius;
  have rightLow := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork hi radius;
  have rightHigh := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork hi radius;
  have left :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftLow.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftHigh.1);
  have right :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightLow.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightHigh.1);
  have states :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun s => OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n s) right.1) left.1;
  (states.1, leftLow.2 + leftHigh.2 + rightLow.2 + rightHigh.2 + left.2 + right.2 + states.2 + 6)
def OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n × ℕ :=
fun {n M} parent i =>
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork._proof_12;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractor_value_17 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork._proof_7;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalSubtractWithWork_value_54 := ⋯;
  have proof_saturatingSubtractWithWork_value_50 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalMulWithWork_value_55 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalDivModWithWork_spec_24 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalAddWithWork_value_52 := ⋯;
  have proof_balancedCutWithWork_value_51 := ⋯;
  let width := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork parent.hi parent.lo;
  let next := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (↑i) 1;
  let left := OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCutWithWork parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width.1) M ↑i;
  let right :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCutWithWork parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width.1) M
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue next.1);
  have heql := ⋯;
  have heqh := ⋯;
  ({ lo := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue left.1, hi := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue right.1, ordered := ⋯,
      valid := ⋯ },
    width.2 + next.2 + left.2 + right.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.cellRepresentativeStates : (n : ℕ) →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℚ → (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℚ) → ℚ → ℕ × ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun n P F b a initial theta cell =>
  List.filter (fun r => decide (↑b / (4 * a) ≤ initial r ∧ initial r ≤ 8 * ↑F * ↑b))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.cellTargetStates n (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) (theta * ↑b) cell)
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupRoundedStateList.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ → Fin M → ℚ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent b exponent cell i h =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.gridGapStates target.length (OAI.EditApproximation.groupRoundSpacing exponent h)
    (⌊↑cell.1 * (2 ^ (-↑exponent) * ↑b)⌋₊ + ((OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo - parent.lo)) (12 * b)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.substring source (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo
        (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi).length
    ⌈2 * h⌉₊
def OAI.EditApproximation.rawInitialConeCenters : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℚ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P A value q =>
  List.flatMap (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetStates n (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) value q)
    (List.filter (fun b => decide (↑value / (16 * A ^ 2) ≤ ↑b ∧ ↑b ≤ 4 * A * ↑value))
      (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N))
def OAI.EditApproximation.historyInputCode : {M n : ℕ} → Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} input => Nat.pair input.2.1 (OAI.EditApproximation.childInputCode (input.1, input.2.2))
def OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineSourceKeys : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℚ →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℚ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P F a initial q =>
  List.map (fun b => (b, OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) q))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow N (initial q) a ↑F)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryCodeWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M n} input =>
  have child := OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeWithWork (input.1, input.2.2);
  have pair := OAI.EditApproximation.queryPairWithWork input.2.1 child.1;
  (pair.1, child.2 + pair.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedChildInputsWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {M n} inputs =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedInputsWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeWithWork) inputs
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryEqualWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {M n} a b =>
  have child := OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork (a.1, a.2.2) (b.1, b.2.2);
  have time := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a.2.1 b.2.1;
  (child.1 && time.1, child.2 + time.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryGapStatesWithWork : (n : ℕ) →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun n g anchor radius sourceLength gapRadius =>
  have lo := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork anchor radius;
  have hi := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork anchor radius;
  have left :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lo.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue hi.1);
  have states :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun first =>
        have center := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork first sourceLength;
        have lower :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center.1) gapRadius;
        have upper :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center.1) gapRadius;
        have right :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower.1)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper.1);
        have valid := OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n first) right.1;
        (valid.1, center.2 + lower.2 + upper.2 + right.2 + valid.2 + 4))
      left.1;
  (states.1, lo.2 + hi.2 + left.2 + states.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOnlineKeyEqualWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {n} a b =>
  have scale := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork a.1 b.1;
  have state := OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork a.2 b.2;
  (scale.1 && state.1, scale.2 + state.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCode : {M J n : ℕ} → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J n → ℕ :=
fun {M J n} state =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.childInputCode (0, state.2) + (n + 1) ^ 2 * OAI.EditApproximation.physicalNodeCode state.1
def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedBitLocalWork.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery { child // rank child < rank key } OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      κ → ℕ :=
fun {κ} rank program key =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.work (fun child => OAI.EditApproximation.rankedBitProgramValue rank program ↑child)
    (fun x => 0) (program key)
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementParameterWordsWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ →
    (word : List Bool) →
      0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue word → OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords × ℕ :=
fun N pass word hpositive =>
  have online := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork word hpositive;
  have factor := OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledRefinementWordWithWork N pass;
  have decrement := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementDecrementWithWork word hpositive;
  ({ tau := online.1.2, factor := factor.1, eta := online.1.1, kappa := online.1.2, delta := decrement.1 },
    online.2 + factor.2 + decrement.2 + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryEqualAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} a b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualAllocation (a.1, a.2.2) (b.1, b.2.2) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation a.2.1 b.2.1 +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOnlineKeyEqualAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} a b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation a.1 b.1 + OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualAllocation a.2 b.2 + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun n g radius lo hi =>
  have leftLow := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork lo radius).1;
  have leftHigh := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork lo radius).1;
  have rightLow := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork hi radius).1;
  have rightHigh := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork hi radius).1;
  have left :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftLow)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftHigh)).1;
  have right :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightLow)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightHigh)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation lo radius +
              OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation lo radius +
            OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation hi radius +
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation hi radius +
        OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsAllocation g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftLow)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftHigh) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsAllocation g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightLow)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightHigh) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
      (fun s => OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n s) right)
      (fun s =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n s)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateAllocation n s) right)
      left
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryCodeAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} input =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeAllocation (input.1, input.2.2) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryPairAllocation input.2.1
      (OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeWithWork (input.1, input.2.2)).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedChildInputsAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {M n} inputs =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedInputsAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeWithWork)
    OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualAllocation
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryChildCodeAllocation)
    inputs
def OAI.EditApproximation.actionMemberAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} a x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.actionMemberAllocation._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryGapStatesAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun n g anchor radius sourceLength gapRadius =>
  have lo := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork anchor radius).1;
  have hi := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork anchor radius).1;
  have left :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lo)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue hi)).1;
  have build := fun first =>
    have center := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork first sourceLength;
    have lower :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center.1) gapRadius;
    have upper :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center.1) gapRadius;
    have right :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower.1)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper.1);
    have valid := OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n first) right.1;
    (valid.1, center.2 + lower.2 + upper.2 + right.2 + valid.2 + 4);
  have allocation := fun first =>
    have center := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork first sourceLength).1;
    have lower :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center) gapRadius).1;
    have upper :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center) gapRadius).1;
    have right :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsWithWork g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper)).1;
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation first sourceLength +
            OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center) gapRadius +
          OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue center) gapRadius +
        OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsAllocation g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lower)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue upper) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n first)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateAllocation n first) right;
  OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation anchor radius +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation anchor radius +
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridPointsAllocation g (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue lo)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue hi) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build allocation left
def OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ → ℕ → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny} M parent r b P =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.finiteAssignments M (OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildStateList parent r b P)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval : {M J : ℕ} →
  (nx : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx :=
fun {M J} nx node => OAI.EditApproximation.sourceIntervalAt M nx (↑node.fst) node.snd
def OAI.EditApproximation.localInitialConeTable.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  List α →
    (target : List α) →
      (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
        ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℚ → ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ :=
fun {α} source target U N P F A a q =>
  if U q = 0 then 0
  else
    OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMinimum ((32 * ↑F + 1) * ↑N)
      (List.map (OAI.EditApproximation.initialConeValue source target U a (32 * ↑F) q)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.localInitialConeCenters N P A U q))
def OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ → ℕ → Fin M → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b P i =>
  have child := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork parent i;
  have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
  have radius := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 10 b;
  have leftOffset := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.1.lo parent.lo;
  have rightOffset := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.1.hi parent.lo;
  have left := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork r.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftOffset.1);
  have right := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork r.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightOffset.1);
  have grid :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork ny (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue left.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue right.1);
  (grid.1, child.2 + spacing.2 + radius.2 + leftOffset.2 + rightOffset.2 + left.2 + right.2 + grid.2 + 8)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedHistoryInputsWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
    List (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=

    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryCodeWithWork) inputs
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryNeededInputsWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  List
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)
        (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) M) →
    List
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)
          (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) M) →
      List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n M} selected online =>
  have first := OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsWithWork selected;
  have second := OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsWithWork online;
  have sorted := OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedChildInputsWithWork (first.1 ++ second.1);
  (sorted.1, first.2 + second.2 + first.1.length + sorted.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryParameters : ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords :=
fun N pass =>
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_computed_small_word_pos_125 := ⋯;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.refinementParameterWordsWithWork N pass (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N).bits ⋯).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ → ℕ → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b P i =>
  have child := (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork parent i).1;
  have spacing := (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1;
  have radius := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 10 b).1;
  have leftOffset := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.lo parent.lo).1;
  have rightOffset := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.hi parent.lo).1;
  have left := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork r.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftOffset)).1;
  have right :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork r.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightOffset)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildAllocation parent i + OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
              OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation 10 b +
            OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation child.lo parent.lo +
          OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation child.hi parent.lo +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation r.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue leftOffset) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation r.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue rightOffset) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxAllocation ny (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue left)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue right)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedHistoryInputsAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=

    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryCodeWithWork)

    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEncodedLEAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryCodeWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryHistoryCodeAllocation)
    inputs
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryNeededInputsAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  List
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)
        (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) M) →
    List
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)
          (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) M) →
      ℕ :=
fun {n M} selected online =>
  have first := (OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsWithWork selected).1;
  have second := (OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsWithWork online).1;

        OAI.EditApproximation.queryActionInputsAllocation online +
      first.length +
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedChildInputsAllocation (first ++ second)

def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun values =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassTotalWithWork (Vector.ofFn values.get);
  (result.1, result.2 + 6 * values.length)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.connectionTermsWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} start x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ)
    x OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.connectionTermsWithWork._f start x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandEnvelopeReadWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M n} b band read i =>
  have values := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun action => read i (action i)) band;
  have floor := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M);
  have maximum := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero.maximumWithWork values.1;
  have input := floor.1.maxWithWork maximum.1;
  have result := input.1.dyadicCeilingWithWork;
  (result.1, values.2 + floor.2 + maximum.2 + input.2 + result.2 + band.length + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineLinearReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read eta kappa t i =>
  have coefficient := kappa.coefficientWithWork;
  have terms :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork t fun s =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimisticTermReadWithWork mass read coefficient.1 (↑s) i;
  have history := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassTotalWithWork terms.1;
  have current := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadWithWork mass read t i;
  have total := history.1.canonicalAddWithWork current.1;
  have result := eta.canonicalMulWithWork total.1;
  (result.1, coefficient.2 + terms.2 + history.2 + current.2 + total.2 + result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.targetCellsWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × ℕ) × ℕ :=
fun {n} side radius q =>
  have left := side.cellCoordinatesWithWork q.lo radius;
  have right := side.cellCoordinatesWithWork q.hi radius;
  have cells :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun lo => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun hi => ((lo, hi), 1)) right.1) left.1;
  (cells.1, left.2 + right.2 + cells.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesWithWork : (n : ℕ) →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ × ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun n g side cell =>
  have left := side.cellGridPointsWithWork g cell.1;
  have right := side.cellGridPointsWithWork g cell.2;
  have states :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun lo => OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n lo) right.1) left.1;
  (states.1, left.2 + right.2 + states.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {M n} band =>
  have raw :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => ((i, action i), 1)) (List.finRange M)) band;
  have unique := OAI.EditApproximation.dedupByWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork raw.1;
  (unique.1, raw.2 + unique.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandCoordinateWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
        Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M n} mass total action coordinate =>
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork coordinate (coordinate.1, action coordinate.1);
  have result :=
    if test.1 = true then (mass.get coordinate.1).divWithWork total else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, 1);
  (result.1, test.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeScaleReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          List Bool →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} P F a read radius query e =>
  have b := 2 ^ e;
  have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
  have points :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) query.lo query.hi;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
      (fun r =>
        have word := read r;
        have guard := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a word.1;
        (guard.1, guard.2 + word.2))
      points.1;
  have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((b, r), 1)) selected.1;
  (labeled.1, spacing.2 + points.2 + selected.2 + labeled.2 + e + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F a value q =>
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value;
  have radius := value.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have centers :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun b =>
        have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
        have states :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1) q.lo q.hi;
        (states.1, spacing.2 + states.2 + 1))
      scales.1;
  have unique := OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork centers.1;
  (unique.1, scales.2 + radius.2 + centers.2 + unique.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWideInputsWithWork : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
          List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent P representatives =>
  have raw :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun br =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
          (fun i =>
            have states := OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i;
            have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((i, r), 1)) states.1;
            (labeled.1, states.2 + labeled.2 + 1))
          (List.finRange M))
      representatives;
  have unique := OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork raw.1;
  (unique.1, raw.2 + unique.2 + M + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawHistoryInputsWithWork : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
            List (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent P history representatives =>
  have inputs :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun br =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
          (fun i =>
            have states := OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i;
            have times :=
              OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
                (fun t => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((i, t, r), 1)) states.1)
                (List.range history);
            (times.1, states.2 + times.2 + history + 2))
          (List.finRange M))
      representatives;
  (inputs.1, inputs.2 + M + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupRoundedStatesWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent b exponent cell i h =>
  have theta := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent;
  have spacing := theta.roundedSpacingWithWork h;
  have child := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork parent i;
  have offset := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.1.lo parent.lo;
  have base := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat cell.1).canonicalMulWithWork theta;
  have position := base.1.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have floor := position.1.naturalFloorWithWork;
  have anchor :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue floor.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue offset.1);
  have radius := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 12 b;
  have length := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.1.hi child.1.lo;
  have twice := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulWithWork h;
  have gap := twice.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have states :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryGapStatesWithWork ny (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue anchor.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue length.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue gap.1);
  (states.1,
    spacing.2 + child.2 + offset.2 + base.2 + position.2 + floor.2 + anchor.2 + radius.2 + length.2 + twice.2 + gap.2 +
          states.2 +
        exponent +
      12)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineIntegerReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {n} keys values b q =>
  have word :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gatheredWordLookupWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.queryOnlineKeyEqualWithWork
      keys values (b, q);
  have floor := word.1.naturalFloorWithWork;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue floor.1, word.2 + floor.2 + 1)

      Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=

    values (i, q)

    values (input.1, s, input.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →

      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=

def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialGridReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
        ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} P A read radius q b =>
  have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
  have points :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1) radius q.lo q.hi;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
      (fun r =>
        have word := read r;
        have guard :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCenterGuardWithWork b (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue word.1)
            A;
        (guard.1, guard.2 + word.2))
      points.1;
  (selected.1, spacing.2 + points.2 + selected.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialConeCentersWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P A value q =>
  have radius := value.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N;
  have active :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
      (fun b =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1) b
          A)
      scales.1;
  have centers :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun b =>
        have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
        have states :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1) q.lo q.hi;
        (states.1, spacing.2 + states.2 + 1))
      active.1;
  (centers.1, radius.2 + scales.2 + active.2 + centers.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.connectionTermsAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun {n} start x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ) x
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.connectionTermsAllocation._f start x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} q r =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q.lo).distanceAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo) +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q.hi).distanceAllocation
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi) +
            ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q.lo).distanceWithWork
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo)).1.canonicalAddAllocation
              ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q.hi).distanceWithWork
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi)).1 +
          q.lo.size +
        q.hi.size +
      r.lo.size +
    r.hi.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength r =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi).subAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo) +
          ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi).subWithWork
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo)).1.subAllocation
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat sourceLength) +
        r.hi.size +
      r.lo.size +
    sourceLength.size
