def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {M n} band =>
  have build := fun action =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => ((i, action i), 1)) (List.finRange M);
  have raw := (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork build band).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build
      (fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1) (List.finRange M) + M) band +
    OAI.EditApproximation.dedupByAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualAllocation raw
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → (α → ℕ) → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} score allocation x =>
  List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxAllocation._f score allocation)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.anchorGuardAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b states i =>
  have left :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointWithWork r.lo parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo).1;
  have right :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointWithWork r.lo parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi).1;
  have radius := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 6 b).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointAllocation r.lo parent.lo (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo +
          OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointAllocation r.lo parent.lo
            (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation 6 b +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalDistanceLeAllocation left (states i).lo radius +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalDistanceLeAllocation right (states i).hi radius
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandEnvelopeReadAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {M n} b band read footprint i =>
  have values := (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun action => read i (action i)) band).1;
  have floor :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M)).1;
  have maximum := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero.maximumWithWork values).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun action => footprint i (action i)) band +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b).divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M) +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero.maximumAllocation values +
        floor.maxAllocation maximum +
      (floor.maxWithWork maximum).1.dyadicCeilingAllocation +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.targetCellsAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} side radius q =>
  have left := (side.cellCoordinatesWithWork q.lo radius).1;
  have right := (side.cellCoordinatesWithWork q.hi radius).1;
  side.cellCoordinatesAllocation q.lo radius + side.cellCoordinatesAllocation q.hi radius +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
      (fun lo => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun hi => ((lo, hi), 1)) right)
      (fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1) right) left
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesAllocation : ℕ →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ × ℕ → ℕ :=
fun n g side cell =>
  have left := (side.cellGridPointsWithWork g cell.1).1;
  have right := (side.cellGridPointsWithWork g cell.2).1;
  side.cellGridPointsAllocation g cell.1 + side.cellGridPointsAllocation g cell.2 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
      (fun lo => OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n lo) right)
      (fun lo =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.filterMapAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateWithWork n lo)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.makeStateAllocation n lo) right)
      left
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F a value q =>
  have build := fun b =>
    have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
    have states :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue value.naturalCeilingWithWork.1) q.lo q.hi;
    (states.1, spacing.2 + states.2 + 1);
  have allocation := fun b =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxAllocation n
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue value.naturalCeilingWithWork.1) q.lo q.hi;
  have centers :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork build
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value).1).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesAllocation N F a value + value.naturalCeilingAllocation +
      OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build allocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value).1 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualAllocation centers
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (P : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        0 < P →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F _hP a value q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesAllocation N F a value +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
      (fun b =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
            OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateAllocation
              (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1) q +
          1)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeScaleReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → List Bool → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {n} P F a read allocation radius query e =>
  have b := 2 ^ e;
  have spacing := (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1;
  have points :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) query.lo query.hi).1;
  have test := fun r =>
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a (read r).1).1,
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a (read r).1).2 + (read r).2);
  have selected := (OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork test points).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
            OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxAllocation n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) query.lo query.hi +
          OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation test
            (fun r => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityAllocation b F a (read r).1 + allocation r)
            points +
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1) selected +
      e +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWideInputsAllocation : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx → ℕ → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent P representatives =>
  have build := fun br =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun i =>
        have states := OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i;
        have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((i, r), 1)) states.1;
        (labeled.1, states.2 + labeled.2 + 1))
      (List.finRange M);
  have one := fun br i =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridAllocation parent br.2 br.1 P i +
      OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build
        (fun br =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
            (fun i =>
              have states := OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i;
              have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((i, r), 1)) states.1;
              (labeled.1, states.2 + labeled.2 + 1))
            (one br) (List.finRange M))
        representatives +
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryEraseAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork
        OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork build representatives).1 +
    M
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawHistoryInputsAllocation : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx → ℕ → ℕ → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent P history representatives =>
  have child := fun br i =>
    have states := OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i;
    have times :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
        (fun t => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((i, t, r), 1)) states.1) (List.range history);
    (times.1, states.2 + times.2 + history + 2);
  have one := fun br i =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridAllocation parent br.2 br.1 P i + history +
      OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
        (fun t =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((i, t, r), 1))
            (OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i).1)
        (fun x =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildGridWithWork parent br.2 br.1 P i).1)
        (List.range history);
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
      (fun br => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork (child br) (List.finRange M))
      (fun br => OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation (child br) (one br) (List.finRange M))
      representatives +
    M
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupRoundedStatesAllocation : {nx : ℕ} →
  {ny : ℕ} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent b exponent cell i h =>
  have theta := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent;
  have spacing := (theta.roundedSpacingWithWork h).1;
  have child := (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork parent i).1;
  have offset := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.lo parent.lo).1;
  have base := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat cell.1).canonicalMulWithWork theta).1;
  have position := (base.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
  have floor := position.naturalFloorWithWork.1;
  have anchor :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue floor)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue offset)).1;
  have radius := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 12 b).1;
  have length := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork child.hi child.lo).1;
  have twice := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulWithWork h).1;
  have gap := twice.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  theta.roundedSpacingAllocation h + OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildAllocation parent i +
                          OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation child.lo parent.lo +
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat cell.1).canonicalMulAllocation theta +
                      base.canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
                    position.naturalFloorAllocation +
                  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue floor)
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue offset) +
                OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulAllocation 12 b +
              OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation child.hi child.lo +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulAllocation h +
          twice.naturalCeilingAllocation +
        OAI.EditApproximation.queryGapStatesAllocation ny (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue anchor) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue length) (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue gap) +
      exponent +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupIndexAllocation : {M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → List (Fin M) → ℕ :=
fun {M} h envelope values indices =>
  h.groupTermsAllocation envelope indices +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation (h.groupTermsWithWork envelope values indices).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} keys words r =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gatheredLookupAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualAllocation keys words r
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineLinearReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {β : Type u_1} →
  {σ : Type u_2} →
    (β → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (ℕ → β × σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (ℕ → β × σ → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → β × σ → ℕ :=
fun {β} {σ} mass read footprint eta kappa t i =>
  have coefficient := kappa.coefficientWithWork.1;
  have terms :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork t fun s =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimisticTermReadWithWork mass read coefficient (↑s) i).1;
  have history := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassTotalWithWork terms).1;
  have current := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadWithWork mass read t i).1;
  (kappa.coefficientAllocation +
              OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation t fun s =>
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimisticTermReadAllocation mass read footprint coefficient (↑s)
                  i) +
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassAllocation terms +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gainReadAllocation mass read footprint t i +
        history.canonicalAddAllocation current +
      eta.canonicalMulAllocation (history.canonicalAddWithWork current).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandCoordinateAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {M n} mass total action coordinate =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualAllocation coordinate (coordinate.1, action coordinate.1) +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.coordinateEqualWithWork coordinate (coordinate.1, action coordinate.1)).1 = true then
      (mass.get coordinate.1).divAllocation total
    else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialConeCentersAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N P A value q =>
  have radius := value.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  have active :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
        (fun b =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) b
            A)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N).1).1;
  have build := fun b =>
    have spacing := (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1;
    have states :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) q.lo q.hi;
    (states.1, (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).2 + states.2 + 1);
  value.naturalCeilingAllocation + OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesAllocation N +
      OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation
        (fun b =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) b
            A)
        (fun b =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) b
            A)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N).1 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build
      (fun b =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
          OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxAllocation n
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) q.lo q.hi)
      active
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.connectionWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} start finish states =>
  have terms := start.connectionTermsWithWork states finish;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork terms.1;
  (result.1, terms.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.envelopeVectorReadWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M × ℕ :=
fun {M n} b band read =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork M (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandEnvelopeReadWithWork b band read)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            Parent →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance L read query action =>
  have words := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => read (action.childState i)) (List.finRange M);
  have dist := distance query action.center;
  have spatial := L.canonicalMulWithWork dist.1;
  have connection := spatial.1.canonicalAddWithWork action.connection;
  have children := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words.1;
  have result := connection.1.canonicalAddWithWork children.1;
  (result.1, words.2 + dist.2 + spatial.2 + connection.2 + children.2 + result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCentersReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              ℕ × ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} P F b a theta initial cell =>
  have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
  have side := theta.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
  have states :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1) side.1
      cell;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
      (fun r =>
        have word := initial r;
        have tested := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a word.1;
        (tested.1, word.2 + tested.2))
      states.1;
  (selected.1, spacing.2 + side.2 + states.2 + selected.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandVerticesWithWork : {M n d : ℕ} →
  Vector (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) d →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
      List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
        List ((Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) × ℕ :=
fun {M n d} coordinates mass band =>
  have total := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassTotalWithWork mass;
  have vertices :=
    List.map
      (fun action =>
        have vertex :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork d fun i =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandCoordinateWithWork mass total.1 action (coordinates.get i);
        ((action, vertex.1), vertex.2))
      band;
  (List.map Prod.fst vertices, total.2 + (List.map Prod.snd vertices).sum + 8 * band.length)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineIndexSetupWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M n} H band =>
  have coordinates := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesWithWork band;
  have sum := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork H coordinates.1.length;
  have offset := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue sum.1) 2;
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork offset.1 80;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue offset.1, coordinates.2 + sum.2 + offset.2 + power.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F a read query =>
  have queryWord := read query;
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsWithWork N F a queryWord.1;
  have radius := queryWord.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeScaleReadWithWork P F a read radius.1 query) scales.1;
  (result.1, queryWord.2 + scales.2 + radius.2 + result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × ℕ × ℕ) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N F exponent a value q =>
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value;
  have radius := value.naturalCeilingWithWork;
  have keys :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun b =>
        have side :=
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
        have cells := side.1.targetCellsWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1) q;
        have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun cell => ((b, cell), 1)) cells.1;
        (labeled.1, side.2 + cells.2 + labeled.2 + 2))
      scales.1;
  (keys.1, scales.2 + radius.2 + keys.2 + exponent + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryMeanWithWork : ℕ →
  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun count words =>
  have total := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words;
  have result := total.1.divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat count);
  (result.1, total.2 + result.2 + count.size + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCentersReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P A read q =>
  have queryWord := read q;
  have Uq := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue queryWord.1;
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N;
  have active :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
      (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork Uq b A) scales.1;
  have centers :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialGridReadWithWork P A read Uq q) active.1;
  (centers.1, queryWord.2 + scales.2 + active.2 + centers.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSeedReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
      List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
        List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ :=
fun {n} value q keys words r =>
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork r q;
  if test.1 = true then
    have result := value.naturalCeilingWithWork;
    (result.1, test.2 + result.2 + 1)
  else
    have found := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadWithWork keys words r;
    have result := found.1.naturalCeilingWithWork;
    (result.1, test.2 + found.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
      List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
        List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} value q keys words r =>
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork r q;
  if test.1 = true then (value, test.2 + 1)
  else
    have found := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadWithWork keys words r;
    (found.1, test.2 + found.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.connectionAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n} start finish states =>
  start.connectionTermsAllocation states finish +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation (start.connectionTermsWithWork states finish).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {M n} band =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesAllocation band +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesWithWork band).1.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.maximizerAllocation : {d : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → List (Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) → ℕ :=
fun {d} gradient vertices =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotWithWork gradient)
      (fun v => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListAllocation gradient v (List.finRange d)) vertices +
    d
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineIndexSetupAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {M n} H band =>
  have coordinates := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesWithWork band).1;
  have sum := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork H coordinates.length).1;
  have offset := (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue sum) 2).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesAllocation band +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation H coordinates.length +
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue sum) 2 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation offset 80
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.envelopeVectorReadAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → ℕ :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation M
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandEnvelopeReadAllocation b band read footprint)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.roundedBandReadAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      (band : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) →
        band ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} exponent b band hne read allocation =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation M fun i =>
    have envelope := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandEnvelopeReadWithWork b band read i).1;
    have spacing :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).roundedSpacingWithWork envelope).1;
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandEnvelopeReadAllocation b band read allocation i +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).roundedSpacingAllocation envelope +
          OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateAllocation spacing (band.head hne i) +
        exponent +
      2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N F exponent a value q =>
  have radius := value.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  have build := fun b =>
    have side :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b);
    have cells := side.1.targetCellsWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) q;
    have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun cell => ((b, cell), 1)) cells.1;
    (labeled.1, side.2 + cells.2 + labeled.2 + 2);
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesAllocation N F a value + value.naturalCeilingAllocation +
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build
          (fun b =>
            have side :=
              ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulAllocation
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
                side.targetCellsAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) q +
              OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1)
                (side.targetCellsWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) q).1)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value).1 +
      exponent +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCellAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ × ℕ → ℕ :=
fun n P exponent key =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation key.1 P +
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulAllocation
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat key.1) +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesAllocation n
          (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork key.1 P).1)
          ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat key.1)).1
          key.2 +
      exponent +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F a read allocation query =>
  allocation query + OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsAllocation N F a (read query).1 +
      (read query).1.naturalCeilingAllocation +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeScaleReadWithWork P F a read
        (read query).1.naturalCeilingWithWork.1 query)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeScaleReadAllocation P F a read allocation
        (read query).1.naturalCeilingWithWork.1 query)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryExponentsWithWork N F a (read query).1).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCentersReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → ℕ × ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {n} P F b a theta initial allocation cell =>
  have spacing := (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1;
  have side := (theta.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
  have states :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing) side
        cell).1;
  have test := fun r =>
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a (initial r).1).1,
      (initial r).2 + (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a (initial r).1).2);
  OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
        theta.canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesAllocation n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing) side
        cell +
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation test
      (fun r => allocation r + OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityAllocation b F a (initial r).1)
      states
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.selectGroupAllocation.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  List ι → (ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) → (ι → ℕ) → ℕ :=
fun {ι} members estimate allocation =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxAllocation
    (fun member => ((estimate member).1.neg, (estimate member).2 + 1)) allocation members
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (Parent → Parent → ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              (Child → ℕ) → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M → ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance distanceAllocation L read footprint query action =>
  have words := (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => read (action.childState i)) (List.finRange M)).1;
  have dist := (distance query action.center).1;
  have spatial := (L.canonicalMulWithWork dist).1;
  have connection := (spatial.canonicalAddWithWork action.connection).1;
  have children := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun i => footprint (action.childState i)) (List.finRange M) +
            distanceAllocation query action.center +
          L.canonicalMulAllocation dist +
        spatial.canonicalAddAllocation action.connection +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation words +
    connection.canonicalAddAllocation children
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandVerticesAllocation : {M n d : ℕ} →
  Vector (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) d →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {M n d} coordinates mass band =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassAllocation mass +
      (List.map
          (fun action =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation d fun i =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandCoordinateAllocation mass
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandMassTotalWithWork mass).1 action (coordinates.get i))
          band).sum +
    4 * band.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSeedReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
      List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
        List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} value q keys words r =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualAllocation r q +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork r q).1 = true then value.naturalCeilingAllocation
    else
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadAllocation keys words r +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadWithWork keys words r).1.naturalCeilingAllocation
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOverrideParentReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
      List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} q keys words r =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualAllocation r q +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.queryIntervalEqualWithWork r q).1 = true then 0
    else OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryParentReadAllocation keys words r
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r states read j p =>
  have words :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun i => if ↑i < j then read i (states i) else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, 1)) (List.finRange M);
  have finish :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat
          (OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M j)).canonicalAddWithWork
      p;
  have connection :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).connectionWithWork finish.1 (List.take j (List.ofFn states));
  have total := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words.1;
  have result := connection.1.canonicalAddWithWork total.1;
  (result.1,
    words.2 + finish.2 + connection.2 + total.2 + result.2 +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M j).size +
          r.lo.size +
        6 * M +
      5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              Parent →
                List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance L fallback read query actions =>
  have scores :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadWithWork distance L read query) actions;
  have result := fallback.minimumWithWork scores.1;
  (result.1, scores.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsWithWork : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent N P F a initial q =>
  have representatives := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial q;
  have inputs := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWideInputsWithWork M parent P representatives.1;
  (inputs.1, representatives.2 + inputs.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} r states =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).connectionAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi)
          (List.ofFn states) +
        r.lo.size +
      r.hi.size +
    M
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r states read allocation j p =>
  have words :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
        (fun i => if ↑i < j then read i (states i) else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, 1))
        (List.finRange M)).1;
  have finish :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat
            (OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M j)).canonicalAddWithWork
        p).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun i => if ↑i < j then allocation i (states i) else 0)
                  (List.finRange M) +
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M j)).canonicalAddAllocation
                  p +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).connectionAllocation finish
                (List.take j (List.ofFn states)) +
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation words +
          ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).connectionWithWork finish
                  (List.take j (List.ofFn states))).1.canonicalAddAllocation
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words).1 +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M j).size +
      r.lo.size +
    2 * M
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimizerAllocation : {d : ℕ} →
  List (Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) → Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {d} vertices linear G x =>
  Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimizerAllocation._f vertices linear G)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (P : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          0 < P →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F exponent hP a initial allocation q =>
  have groups := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysWithWork N F exponent a (initial q).1 q).1;
  have build := fun key =>
    have centers :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCentersReadWithWork P F key.1 a
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) initial key.2;
    have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((key.1, r), 1)) centers.1;
    (labeled.1, centers.2 + labeled.2 + 1);
  allocation q + OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadAllocation N P F a initial allocation q +
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysAllocation N F exponent a (initial q).1 q +
          OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build
            (fun key =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCentersReadAllocation P F key.1 a
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) initial allocation key.2 +
                OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1)
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCentersReadWithWork P F key.1 a
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) initial key.2).1)
            groups +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysAllocation N P F hP a (initial q).1 q +
      2 * (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial q).1.length +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork build groups).1.length
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsAllocation : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent N P F a initial allocation q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadAllocation N P F a initial allocation q +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWideInputsAllocation M parent P
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial q).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (Parent → Parent → ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (Child → ℕ) → Parent → List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) → ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance distanceAllocation L fallback read footprint query actions =>
  have scores :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadWithWork distance L read query) actions).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadAllocation distance distanceAllocation L read footprint
        query)
      actions +
    fallback.minimumAllocation scores
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixMinimumReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
        actions ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r actions hne read j p =>
  have fallback := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadWithWork parent r (actions.head hne) read j p;
  have scores :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadWithWork parent r action read j p) actions;
  have result := fallback.1.minimumWithWork scores.1;
  (result.1, fallback.2 + scores.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localContinuationReadWithWork.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  Parent →
                    List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) →
                      List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) →
                        List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance L delta fallback old current query wide groups online =>
  have warm := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadWithWork distance L fallback old query wide;
  have cone := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadWithWork distance L warm.1 current query groups;
  have scores :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadWithWork distance L current query) online;
  have onlineMin := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineMinimumWithWork scores.1;
  have result := delta.refinementUpdateWithWork cone.1 onlineMin.1;
  (result.1, warm.2 + cone.2 + scores.2 + onlineMin.2 + result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixMinimumReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
        actions ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r actions hne read allocation j p =>
  have fallback := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadWithWork parent r (actions.head hne) read j p).1;
  have scores :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
        (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadWithWork parent r action read j p) actions).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadAllocation parent r (actions.head hne) read allocation j p +
      OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
        (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixReadAllocation parent r action read allocation j p)
        actions +
    fallback.minimumAllocation scores
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localContinuationReadAllocation.{u_1, u_2} : {Parent : Type u_1} →
  {Child : Type u_2} →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (Parent → Parent → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (Parent → Parent → ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  (Child → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                    (Child → ℕ) →
                      (Child → ℕ) →
                        Parent →
                          List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) →
                            List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) →
                              List (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction Parent Child M) → ℕ :=
fun {Parent} {Child} {M} distance distanceAllocation L delta fallback old current oldAllocation currentAllocation query
    wide groups online =>
  have warm := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadWithWork distance L fallback old query wide).1;
  have cone := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadWithWork distance L warm current query groups).1;
  have scores :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadWithWork distance L current query) online).1;
  have onlineMin := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineMinimumWithWork scores).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadAllocation distance distanceAllocation L fallback old oldAllocation
            query wide +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadAllocation distance distanceAllocation L warm current
            currentAllocation query groups +
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanActionReadAllocation distance distanceAllocation L current
            currentAllocation query)
          online +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineMinimumAllocation scores +
    delta.refinementUpdateAllocation cone onlineMin
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointActionAllocation : {ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → ℕ :=
fun {ny M} center states => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionAllocation center states + M + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.selectionAllocation.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {d : ℕ} →
    List (α × Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d) →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction d → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {α} {d} vertices linear G j =>
  have iterate :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finiteOptimizerWithWork (List.map Prod.snd vertices) linear G j).1;
  have gradient := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientWithWork G linear iterate).1;
  vertices.length + OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.optimizerAllocation (List.map Prod.snd vertices) linear G j +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gradientAllocation G linear iterate +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scoredArgmaxAllocation
      (fun vertex => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotWithWork gradient vertex.2)
      (fun vertex => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.dotListAllocation gradient vertex.2 (List.finRange d))
      vertices
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cutGuardReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
        actions ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Fin M → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r actions hne read states velocity tolerance j =>
  if ↑j = 0 then (true, 1)
  else
    have shift :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (states j).lo).subWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent j).lo);
    have offset :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).subWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat parent.lo);
    have prediction :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixMinimumReadWithWork parent r actions hne read (↑j) shift.1;
    have result := shift.1.affineErrorWithWork offset.1 velocity prediction.1 tolerance;
    (result.1,
      shift.2 + offset.2 + prediction.2 + result.2 + (states j).lo.size +
              (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent j).lo.size +
            r.lo.size +
          parent.lo.size +
        5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cutGuardReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
        actions ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r actions hne read allocation states velocity tolerance j =>
  if ↑j = 0 then 0
  else
    have shift :=
      ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (states j).lo).subWithWork
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent j).lo)).1;
    have offset :=
      ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).subWithWork
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat parent.lo)).1;
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (states j).lo).subAllocation
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent j).lo) +
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).subAllocation
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat parent.lo) +
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixMinimumReadAllocation parent r actions hne read allocation
                  (↑j) shift +
              shift.affineErrorAllocation offset velocity
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.prefixMinimumReadWithWork parent r actions hne read (↑j) shift).1
                tolerance +
            (states j).lo.size +
          (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent j).lo.size +
        r.lo.size +
      parent.lo.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandPredicateReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
                actions ≠ [] →
                  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b tau theta velocity actions hne read allocation states =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.zeroConnectionAllocation r.lo (List.ofFn states) r.hi + M +
        OAI.EditApproximation.allAllocation
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.anchorGuardAllocation parent r b states) (List.finRange M) +
      tau.bandToleranceAllocation theta b M +
    OAI.EditApproximation.allAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cutGuardReadAllocation parent r actions hne read allocation states velocity
        (tau.bandToleranceWithWork theta b M).1)
      (List.finRange M)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalConnectionBudget : {n : ℕ} →
  ℚ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℚ → ℚ :=
fun {n} start x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => ℚ → ℚ → ℚ) x OAI.EditApproximation.rationalConnectionBudget._f start x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.bandChildEnvelope : {ny M : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → Fin M → ℚ :=
fun {ny M} b band initial i =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rationalDyadicCeiling
    (max (↑b / ↑M) (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMaximum 0 (List.map (fun action => initial i (action i)) band)))
def OAI.EditApproximation.bandEnvelopeExponent : {ny M : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → Fin M → ℤ :=
fun {ny M} b band initial i =>
  Int.clog 2
    (max (↑b / ↑M) (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMaximum 0 (List.map (fun action => initial i (action i)) band)))
def OAI.EditApproximation.zeroConnectionWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {n} start x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.zeroConnectionWithWork._f start x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  (spacing : ℕ) → 0 < spacing → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n × ℕ :=
fun {n} spacing hspacing q =>
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractor_value_17 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalDivModWithWork_spec_24 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryGridRoundWithWork_value_25 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryIntervalRoundWithWork_value_14 := ⋯;
  let output := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryIntervalRoundWithWork spacing q.lo q.hi;
  have heq := ⋯;
  ({ lo := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue output.1.1, hi := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue output.1.2,
      ordered := ⋯, valid := ⋯ },
    output.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.actionEqualWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {n M} a b =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.allWithWork
    (fun i =>
      have left := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork (a i).lo (b i).lo;
      have right := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork (a i).hi (b i).hi;
      (left.1 && right.1, left.2 + right.2 + 1))
    (List.finRange M)
def OAI.EditApproximation.localRefinementRepresentatives : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℚ →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℚ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P F a initial q =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun b =>
      List.map (fun r => (b, r))
        (List.filter (fun r => decide (↑b / (4 * a) ≤ initial r ∧ initial r ≤ 8 * ↑F * ↑b))
          (OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetStates n (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) ⌈initial q⌉₊ q)))
    (List.filter (fun b => decide (initial q / (16 * ↑F) ≤ ↑b ∧ ↑b ≤ 8 * a * initial q))
      (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N))
def OAI.EditApproximation.sharedBandRationalAction : {ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalRationalAction M ny :=
fun {ny M} center states => { center := center, connection := 0, childState := fun i => (i, states i) }
def OAI.EditApproximation.localGroupQueryKeys : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℚ → (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℚ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × ℕ × ℕ) :=
fun {n} N F exponent a initial q =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun b =>
      List.map (fun cell => (b, cell)) (OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetCells (2 ^ (-↑exponent) * ↑b) ⌈initial q⌉₊ q))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow N (initial q) a ↑F)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWidePrefixCost : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → ℕ → ℚ → ℚ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r states child j p =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rationalConnectionBudget (↑r.lo) (List.take j (List.ofFn states))
      (↑(OAI.EditApproximation.balancedCut parent.lo (parent.hi - parent.lo) M j) + p) +
    ∑ i, if ↑i < j then child i (states i) else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.bandRoundSpacing : {ny M : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {ny M} thetaExponent b band initial i =>
  2 ^ (OAI.EditApproximation.bandEnvelopeExponent b band initial i - ↑thetaExponent).toNat
def OAI.EditApproximation.actionMemberWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {n M} a x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.actionMemberWithWork._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.wideEarlierInputCover : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
          Finset (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent P history representatives =>
  representatives.toFinset.biUnion fun br =>
    Finset.univ.biUnion fun i =>
      (Finset.range history).biUnion fun t =>
        Finset.image (fun r => (i, t, r)) (OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildStateList parent br.2 br.1 P i).toFinset
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEarlierRepresentatives : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℚ →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℚ) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P F exponent a initial q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.localRefinementRepresentatives N P F a initial q ++
      List.flatMap
        (fun key =>
          List.map (fun r => (key.1, r))
            (OAI.EditApproximation.cellRepresentativeStates n P F key.1 a initial (2 ^ (-↑exponent)) key.2))
        (OAI.EditApproximation.localGroupQueryKeys N F exponent a initial q) ++
    List.map (fun b => (b, OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) q))
      (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow N (initial q) a ↑F)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWidePrefixMinimum : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
        actions ≠ [] → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → ℕ → ℚ → ℚ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r actions hnonempty child j p =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMinimum
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWidePrefixCost parent r (actions.head hnonempty) child j p)
    (List.map (fun action => OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWidePrefixCost parent r action child j p) actions)
def OAI.EditApproximation.roundedBandAction : {ny M : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny :=
fun {ny M} thetaExponent b band initial action i =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval (OAI.EditApproximation.bandRoundSpacing thetaExponent b band initial i) (action i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryEarlierInputCover : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℚ →
                (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                    Finset (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent N P F exponent history a initial q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.wideEarlierInputCover parent P history
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEarlierRepresentatives N P F exponent a initial q)
def OAI.EditApproximation.canonicalRoundedBandAction : {ny M : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      (band : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
        band ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny :=
fun {ny M} thetaExponent b band hnonempty initial =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.roundedBandAction thetaExponent b band initial (band.head hnonempty)
def OAI.EditApproximation.parameterClogWithWork : ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun n =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.inputBitLengthWithWork (n - 1);
  (result.1, result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.parameterLogWithWork : ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun n =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.inputBitLengthWithWork n;
  (result.1 - 1, result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.parameterRootSearch : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun n x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.parameterRootSearch._f n)
def OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun a x => Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork._f a)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseInputStorage : {M J : ℕ} →
  List ℕ → (target : List ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ :=
fun {M J} source target entry =>
  have interval := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length entry.1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePreparedInputStorage source target interval.lo interval.hi entry.2.lo entry.2.hi
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryPassWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords) × ℕ :=
fun N pass =>
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have seed := OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledSeedWordWithWork N pass;
  have parameters :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.refinementParameterWordsWithWork N pass (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N).bits ⋯;
  ((seed.1, parameters.1), seed.2 + parameters.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.storedIntegerBits : List ℤ → ℕ :=
fun values => 1 + (List.map (fun z => z.natAbs.size + 2) values).sum
def OAI.EditApproximation.parameterSqrtWithWork : ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun n => OAI.EditApproximation.parameterRootSearch n n
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedQuerySetupWithWork : (N : ℕ) →
  (Vector (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × OAI.EditApproximation.RefinementParameterWords)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) ×
      List Bool) ×
    ℕ :=
fun N =>
  have parameters :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) fun i =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryPassWithWork N ↑i;
  have base := OAI.EditApproximation.refinementBaseWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N).bits;
  ((parameters.1, base.1), parameters.2 + base.2 + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerParametersWithWork : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.IntegerParameters × ℕ :=
fun N =>
  have first := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterClogWithWork (N + 2);
  have heightExponent := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterClogWithWork first.1;
  have height := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork 2 heightExponent.1;
  have smallExponent := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterClogWithWork (max 1 heightExponent.1);
  have small := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork 2 smallExponent.1;
  have logarithm := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterLogWithWork small.1;
  have firstRoot := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterSqrtWithWork logarithm.1;
  have secondRoot := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterSqrtWithWork firstRoot.1;
  have branching := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork 2 (heightExponent.1 / 20);
  have coarseBranching := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork height.1 secondRoot.1;
  have factor := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork height.1 (secondRoot.1 + 4);
  have grid := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork height.1 (secondRoot.1 + 20);
  have rounds := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork 2 ((3 * heightExponent.1 + 3) / 4);
  have spacingDenominator := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork small.1 2;
  ({ H := height.1, ell := small.1, p := secondRoot.1, B := coarseBranching.1, initialFactor := factor.1,
      M := branching.1, P := grid.1, T := rounds.1, S := height.1 / spacingDenominator.1 },
    first.2 + heightExponent.2 + height.2 + smallExponent.2 + small.2 + logarithm.2 + firstRoot.2 + secondRoot.2 +
                  branching.2 +
                coarseBranching.2 +
              factor.2 +
            grid.2 +
          rounds.2 +
        spacingDenominator.2 +
      15)
structure OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest (M J ny : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 3
fields:
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.pass : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.copy : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.index : ℕ
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.state : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny
constructor:
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.mk {M J ny : ℕ} (pass copy : ℕ)
    (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) (index : ℕ) (state : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :
    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny
def OAI.EditApproximation.rawRefinementCenters : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℚ → ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P F a value q =>
  (List.flatMap
      (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.localTargetStates n (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) ⌈value⌉₊ q)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow N value a ↑F)).eraseDups
def OAI.EditApproximation.wideWarmupInputs : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent P representatives =>
  (List.flatMap
      (fun br =>
        List.flatMap
          (fun i => List.map (fun r => (i, r)) (OAI.EditApproximation.wideChildStateList parent br.2 br.1 P i))
          (List.finRange M))
      representatives).eraseDups
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryMeanRead.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  (count : ℕ) →
    (Fin count → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {ι} count reader =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge (count + 1)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect reader (List.ofFn fun i => i) fun words =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryMeanWithWork count words))
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalNodeCodeWithWork : {M J : ℕ} → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M J} node =>
  have path := OAI.EditApproximation.treeLayerCodeWithWork M (↑node.fst) node.snd;
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork M J;
  (path.1 + power.1 * ↑node.fst, path.2 + power.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParametersWithWork : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.RationalParameters × ℕ :=
fun ell =>
  have fourth := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork ell 4;
  have fifth := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork ell 5;
  have tenth := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork ell 10;
  have twentieth := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork ell 20;
  have fortieth := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork ell 40;
  ({ F := ↑ell, initialSlope := 32 * ↑ell, coneSlope := 128 * ↑ell, delta := 1 / ↑tenth.1, kappa := 1 / ↑fourth.1,
      tau := 1 / ↑fourth.1, theta := 1 / ↑fortieth.1, gamma := 1 / ↑twentieth.1, eta := ↑fifth.1 },
    fourth.2 + fifth.2 + tenth.2 + twentieth.2 + fortieth.2 + 20)
def OAI.EditApproximation.onlineMassWithWork : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun M P H =>
  have width := 40 * P + 2;
  have square := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork width 2;
  have dimension := H + M * square.1 + 2;
  have rounds := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork dimension 80;
  (rounds.1 * (rounds.1 + 1) / 2, square.2 + rounds.2 + 9)
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementInitialSupport : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℚ → ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) :=
fun {n} N P F exponent a value q =>
  q ::
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rawRefinementCenters N P F a value q ++
        List.flatMap
          (fun key =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.cellTargetStates n (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing key.1 P)
              (2 ^ (-↑exponent) * ↑key.1) key.2)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.localGroupQueryKeys N F exponent a (fun x => value) q) ++
      List.map (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) q)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow N value a ↑F))
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementBandInputList : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℚ →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent N P F exponent a initial q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.wideWarmupInputs parent P
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryEarlierRepresentatives N P F exponent a initial q)
def OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRequest : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny :=
fun {M J ny} T S copy parent state =>
  { pass := parent.pass - 1, copy := copy, node := parent.node, index := T + S, state := state }
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCodeWithWork : {M J n : ℕ} → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J n → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M J n} entry =>
  have node := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalNodeCodeWithWork entry.1;
  have width := n + 1;
  (entry.2.lo + width * entry.2.hi + width * width * node.1, node.2 + 8)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalBoundedWithWork : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {M J n} passes copies T S request =>
  have pass := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork request.pass passes;
  have copy := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork request.copy copies;
  have cap := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork T S;
