  have index := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork request.index.bits cap.1;
  (decide (pass.1 = Ordering.lt) && decide (copy.1 = Ordering.lt) && index.1, pass.2 + copy.2 + cap.2 + index.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.globalParameterWork : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParametersWithWork N).2 +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParametersWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)).2 +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.onlineMassWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M
        (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N)).2
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalLargeInputWithWork : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Bool × ℕ :=
fun N epsilon =>
  have inspected := OAI.EditApproximation.inspectAccuracyWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) epsilon;
  if inspected.1.isSome = true then
    have product :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)).canonicalMulWithWork epsilon;
    have accuracy := product.1.ltWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 10);
    have threshold :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (2 ^ 1000);
    have mass :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.onlineMassWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M
        (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N);
    have width := OAI.EditApproximation.inputBitLengthWithWork mass.1;
    have capacity := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) width.1;
    (decide (threshold.1 ≠ Ordering.lt ∧ accuracy.1 ≠ true ∧ capacity.1 ≠ Ordering.lt),
      inspected.2 + product.2 + accuracy.2 + threshold.2 + mass.2 + width.2 + capacity.2 + 10)
  else (false, inspected.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalTableCodeAllocation : {M J n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n → ℕ :=
fun {M J n} request => 2 * ↑request.node.fst + 2 * J + 20
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalBoundedAllocation : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n → ℕ :=
fun {M J n} passes copies T S request =>
  request.pass.bits.length + passes.bits.length + request.copy.bits.length + copies.bits.length +
      OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddAllocation T S +
    OAI.EditApproximation.naturalWordLEAllocation request.index.bits
      (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalAddWithWork T S).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalTableCodeWithWork : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M J n} copies T S request =>
  have entry := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCodeWithWork (request.node, request.state);
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.parameterPowerWithWork M J;
  have capacity := (n + 1) * (n + 1) * (power.1 * (J + 1));
  (request.index + (T + S + 1) * (request.copy + copies * (entry.1 + capacity * request.pass)), entry.2 + power.2 + 15)
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.remainingDepth : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny} request => J - ↑request.node.fst
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.rank : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny} T S request => request.pass * (T + S + 1) + request.index
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny → Prop :=
fun {M J ny} passes copies T S request => request.pass < passes ∧ request.copy < copies ∧ request.index ≤ T + S
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J →
        ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy node j state => { pass := pass, copy := copy, node := node, index := j, state := state }
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J →
        ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy node T t state => { pass := pass, copy := copy, node := node, index := T + t, state := state }
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.sameIndexChild : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (request : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny) →
    ↑request.node.fst < J →
      Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny :=
fun {M J ny} request hbelow i state =>
  { pass := request.pass, copy := request.copy, node := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild request.node hbelow i,
    index := request.index, state := state }
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny} T S request => request.remainingDepth + OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.rank T S request
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
    (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
      ↑node.fst < J →
        (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
          Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
              { child //
                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup pass copy node (t + 1) q) } :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy T S t node hbelow q input =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_warmup_previous_child_progress_81 := ⋯;
  have proof_combinedRank_warmup_child_79 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read
    ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup pass copy
        (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) t input.2,
      ⋯⟩
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRefinementCurrentQuery : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
    (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
      ↑node.fst < J →
        (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
          Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
              { child //
                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) } :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy T S t node hbelow q input =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_sameIndexChild_progress_95 := ⋯;
  have proof_combinedRank_sameIndexChild_94 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read
    ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy
        (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) T t input.2,
      ⋯⟩
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRefinementEarlierQuery : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
    (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
      ↑node.fst < J →
        (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
          Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
              { child //
                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) } :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy T S t node hbelow q input =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_refinement_history_child_progress_96 := ⋯;
  if hs : input.2.1 < t then
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read
      ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) T input.2.1 input.2.2,
        ⋯⟩
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
  else OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRefinementWarmupRead : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
    0 < t →
      (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
        (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
          (otherNode : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
            ↑node.fst ≤ ↑otherNode.fst →
              (j : ℕ) →
                j ≤ T →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                      { child //
                        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                          OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) } :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy T S t ht node q otherNode hnode j hj r =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup pass copy otherNode j r, ⋯⟩
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
def OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRead : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (T S : ℕ) →
    (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny) →
      0 < parent.pass →
        OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
              { child //
                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S parent } :=
fun {M J ny} T S parent hpass state copy =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_rank_lt_previous_pass_98 := @OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRead._proof_2;
  have proof_previousPassRequest_progress_97 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRequest T S copy parent state, ⋯⟩
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J ny passes copies T S =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J ny ⊕
    { request // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S request }
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousPass : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r } →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      Fin copies → { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r } :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} parent state copy =>
  ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRequest T S (↑copy) (↑parent) state, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalTableRead : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (T S : ℕ) →
      (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n) →
        { r //
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S r <
              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S parent } →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
            { r //
              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S r <
                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S parent }
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {M J n} copies T S parent child =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge ((OAI.EditApproximation.physicalTableCodeWithWork copies T S ↑child).2 + 1)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.read child OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyTableKey : List ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun source target N =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M
    (OAI.EditApproximation.intervalTreeDepth (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M source.length) target.length
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).T (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).S
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPhysicalCodeWithWork : {M J n passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J n passes copies T S → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {M J n passes copies T S} x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl entry =>
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCodeWithWork entry;
    (2 * result.1, result.2 + 2)
  | Sum.inr request =>
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalTableCodeWithWork copies T S ↑request;
    (2 * result.1 + 1, result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalWarmupRead : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
      0 < t →
        (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
          (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
            (otherNode : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
              ↑node.fst ≤ ↑otherNode.fst →
                (j : ℕ) →
                  j ≤ T →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
                        { child //
                          OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) }
                        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {M J n} copies pass copy T S t ht node q otherNode hnode j hj r =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalTableRead copies T S
    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q)
    ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup pass copy otherNode j r, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalCurrentRead : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
      (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
        ↑node.fst < J →
          (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
            Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
                { child //
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) }
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {M J n} copies pass copy T S t node hbelow q input =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_sameIndexChild_progress_95 := ⋯;
  have proof_combinedRank_sameIndexChild_94 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalTableRead copies T S
    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q)
    ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy
        (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) T t input.2,
      ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalEarlierRead : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
      (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
        ↑node.fst < J →
          (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
            Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
                { child //
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) }
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {M J n} copies pass copy T S t node hbelow q input =>
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_refinement_history_child_progress_96 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge ((OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork input.2.1 t).2 + 1)
    (if hs : (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork input.2.1 t).1 = Ordering.lt then
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalTableRead copies T S
        (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q)
        ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy
            (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) T input.2.1 input.2.2,
          ⋯⟩
    else OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0))
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPhysicalRemaining : {M J n passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J n passes copies T S → ℕ :=
fun {M J n passes copies T S} x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl val => 0
  | Sum.inr request => (↑request).remainingDepth
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPhysicalCodeAllocation : {M J n passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J n passes copies T S → ℕ :=
fun {M J n passes copies T S} x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl entry => 2 * ↑entry.1.fst + J + 12
  | Sum.inr request => OAI.EditApproximation.physicalTableCodeAllocation ↑request + 2
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalMassRead : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
      0 < t →
        (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
          ↑node.fst < J →
            (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
              Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
                  { child //
                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) }
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {M J n} copies pass copy T S t ht node hbelow q input =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryMeanRead T fun j =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalWarmupRead copies pass copy T S t ht node q
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) ⋯ ↑j ⋯ input.2
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerClosedRange : ℤ → ℤ → List ℤ :=
fun lo hi => List.map (fun i => lo + ↑i) (List.range (hi - lo + 1).toNat)
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupSampleCount : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℚ → ℕ :=
fun M Q b h => min M ⌈↑Q * ↑M * h / ↑b⌉₊
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupScaleTrueCost : {M : ℕ} → ℚ → (Fin M → ℚ) → (Fin M → ℚ) → ℚ :=
fun {M} h envelope value => ∑ i, if envelope i = h then value i else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.selectGroupMember.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} → (members : List ι) → members ≠ [] → (ι → ℚ) → ι :=
fun {ι} members hnonempty estimate =>
  (List.argmax (fun member => -estimate member) members).getD (members.head hnonempty)
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupDyadicScales : ℕ → ℕ → ℚ → List ℚ :=
fun M b F =>
  List.map (fun exponent => 2 ^ exponent)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerClosedRange (Int.clog 2 (↑b / ↑M)) (Int.log 2 (64 * F * ↑b)))
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupScaleEstimate : {M : ℕ} →
  (k : ℕ) → ℚ → (Fin M → ℚ) → (Fin M → ℚ) → (Fin k → Fin M) → ℚ :=
fun {M} k h envelope value draw =>
  if k = M then OAI.EditApproximation.groupScaleTrueCost h envelope value
  else ↑M / ↑k * ∑ j, if envelope (draw j) = h then value (draw j) else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseDrawMeasure : (B : ℕ) →
  [NeZero B] →
    [inst : MeasurableSpace (Fin B)] →
      [MeasurableSingletonClass (Fin B)] →
        (D : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D) :=
fun B [NeZero B] [MeasurableSpace (Fin B)] [MeasurableSingletonClass (Fin B)] x =>
  Nat.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseDrawMeasure._f B)
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupMemberEstimate : {M T : ℕ} →
  (Fin T → ℚ) →
    (count : Fin T → ℕ) → ℚ → (Fin M → ℚ) → (Fin M → ℚ) → ((scale : Fin T) → Fin (count scale) → Fin M) → ℚ :=
fun {M T} scales count connection envelope value draw =>
  connection +
    ∑ scale, OAI.EditApproximation.groupScaleEstimate (count scale) (scales scale) envelope value (draw scale)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScalesWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → ℕ → List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun eM eb eF =>
  have exponents := OAI.EditApproximation.integerClosedRange (↑eb - ↑eM) ↑(6 + eF + eb);
  have values :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoWithWork exponents;
  (values.1, values.2 + exponents.length + eM.size + eb.size + eF.size + 8)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandPredicateReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
                actions ≠ [] →
                  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b tau theta velocity actions hne read states =>
  have connection := OAI.EditApproximation.zeroConnectionWithWork r.lo (List.ofFn states) r.hi;
  have anchors :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.allWithWork
      (fun i =>
        have leftAnchor :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointWithWork r.lo parent.lo
            (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo;
        have rightAnchor :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.translatedEndpointWithWork r.lo parent.lo
            (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi;
        have radius := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalMulWithWork 6 b;
        have left :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalDistanceLeWithWork leftAnchor.1 (states i).lo
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1);
        have right :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.naturalDistanceLeWithWork rightAnchor.1 (states i).hi
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius.1);
        (left.1 && right.1, leftAnchor.2 + rightAnchor.2 + radius.2 + left.2 + right.2 + 3))
      (List.finRange M);
  have tolerance := tau.bandToleranceWithWork theta b M;
  have cuts :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.allWithWork
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cutGuardReadWithWork parent r actions hne read states velocity tolerance.1)
      (List.finRange M);
  (connection.1 && anchors.1 && cuts.1, connection.2 + anchors.2 + tolerance.2 + cuts.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.indexedGroupScales : (raw : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
  (M b : ℕ) →
    (F : ℚ) →
      List.map OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.value raw = OAI.EditApproximation.groupDyadicScales M b F →
        Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.groupDyadicScales M b F).length → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun raw M b F h i => raw.get ⟨↑i, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (P : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        0 < P →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F hP a value q =>
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractor_value_17 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_binaryNaturalDivModWithWork_spec_24 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_seedGridSpacingWithWork_value_86 := ⋯;
  have proof_seedGridSpacingWithWork_pos_85 := ⋯;
  have scales := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryScalesWithWork N F a value;
  have keys :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun b =>
        let spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P;
        have center := OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1) ⋯ q;
        ((b, center.1), spacing.2 + center.2 + 2))
      scales.1;
  (keys.1, scales.2 + keys.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScalesAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun eM eb eF =>
  have exponents := OAI.EditApproximation.integerClosedRange (↑eb - ↑eM) ↑(6 + eF + eb);
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoAllocation
            exponents +
          exponents.length +
        eM.size +
      eb.size +
    eF.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandFromActionsReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
                  actions ≠ [] → List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b tau theta velocity read actions hne =>
  have margin := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subWithWork tau;
  have guard := velocity.absolute.leWithWork margin.1;
  if guard.1 = true then
    have filtered :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandPredicateReadWithWork parent r b tau theta velocity actions hne read)
        actions;
    (filtered.1, margin.2 + guard.2 + filtered.2 + 2)
  else ([], margin.2 + guard.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSupportWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (P : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          0 < P →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F exponent hP a value q =>
  have raw := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersWithWork N P F a value q;
  have groups := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysWithWork N F exponent a value q;
  have cells :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun key =>
        have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork key.1 P;
        have side :=
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat key.1);
        have states :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1)
            side.1 key.2;
        (states.1, spacing.2 + side.2 + states.2 + exponent + 3))
      groups.1;
  have online := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysWithWork N P F hP a value q;
  have onlineStates := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun key => (key.2, 1)) online.1;
  (q :: (raw.1 ++ cells.1 ++ onlineStates.1),
    raw.2 + groups.2 + cells.2 + online.2 + onlineStates.2 + 2 * raw.1.length + cells.1.length + 5)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (P : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          0 < P →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F exponent hP a initial q =>
  have query := initial q;
  have reps := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial q;
  have groups := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysWithWork N F exponent a query.1 q;
  have cells :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
      (fun key =>
        have centers :=
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellCentersReadWithWork P F key.1 a
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) initial key.2;
        have labeled := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun r => ((key.1, r), 1)) centers.1;
        (labeled.1, centers.2 + labeled.2 + 1))
      groups.1;
  have online := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysWithWork N P F hP a query.1 q;
  (reps.1 ++ cells.1 ++ online.1,
    query.2 + reps.2 + groups.2 + cells.2 + online.2 + 2 * reps.1.length + cells.1.length + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandFromActionsReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                  (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) → actions ≠ [] → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b tau theta velocity read allocation actions hne =>
  have margin := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subWithWork tau).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one.subAllocation tau + velocity.absolute.leAllocation margin +
    if (velocity.absolute.leWithWork margin).1 = true then
      OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandPredicateReadWithWork parent r b tau theta velocity actions hne read)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandPredicateReadAllocation parent r b tau theta velocity actions hne read
          allocation)
        actions
    else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSupportAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (P : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          0 < P →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} N P F exponent hP a value q =>
  have raw := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersWithWork N P F a value q).1;
  have groups := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysWithWork N F exponent a value q).1;
  have build := fun key =>
    have spacing := OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork key.1 P;
    have side :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat key.1);
    have states :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cellStatesWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing.1) side.1
        key.2;
    (states.1, spacing.2 + side.2 + states.2 + exponent + 3);
  have cells := (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork build groups).1;
  have online := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysWithWork N P F hP a value q).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersAllocation N P F a value q +
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupKeysAllocation N F exponent a value q +
              OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation build
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCellAllocation n P exponent) groups +
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysAllocation N P F hP a value q +
          OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun x => 1) online +
        2 * raw.length +
      cells.length +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryBandInputsWithWork : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        (P : ℕ) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              0 < P →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                      List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent N P F exponent hP a initial q =>
  have representatives :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesWithWork N P F exponent hP a initial q;
  have inputs := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWideInputsWithWork M parent P representatives.1;
  (inputs.1, representatives.2 + inputs.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierInputsWithWork : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        (P : ℕ) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                0 < P →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                        List (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent N P F exponent history hP a initial q =>
  have representatives :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesWithWork N P F exponent hP a initial q;
  have raw := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawHistoryInputsWithWork M parent P history representatives.1;
  have ordered := OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedHistoryInputsWithWork raw.1;
  (ordered.1, representatives.2 + raw.2 + ordered.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierInputsAllocation : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        (P : ℕ) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                0 < P →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent N P F exponent history hP a initial allocation q =>
  have reps :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesWithWork N P F exponent hP a initial q).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesAllocation N P F exponent hP a initial allocation q +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawHistoryInputsAllocation M parent P history reps +
    OAI.EditApproximation.queryOrderedHistoryInputsAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawHistoryInputsWithWork M parent P history reps).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryBandInputsAllocation : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        (P : ℕ) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              0 < P →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent N P F exponent hP a initial allocation q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesAllocation N P F exponent hP a initial allocation q +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWideInputsAllocation M parent P
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierRepresentativesWithWork N P F exponent hP a initial q).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.run.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult κ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult κ :=
fun {ι} {κ} ask x x_1 =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult κ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.run._f ask) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.bind.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι →
    (ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι :=
fun {ι} x x_1 =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    (ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) x
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.bind._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} → (ι → κ) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery κ :=
fun {ι} {κ} f x => OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys._f f)
def OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.restrict.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  (P : ι → Prop) →
    [DecidablePred P] → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery { key // P key } :=
fun {ι} P [DecidablePred P] x =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.restrict._f P)
def OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.requestWork.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult κ) →
      (ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → ℕ :=
fun {ι} {κ} ask cost x x_1 =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.requestWork._f ask cost) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (κ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) →
      List κ → (List ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι :=
fun {ι} {κ} reader x x_1 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x => (List ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι)
    x (OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect._f reader) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {α : Type u_2} →
    [DecidableEq α] →
      {M J : ℕ} →
        List α →
          (target : List α) →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℕ →
                  ℚ →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι :=
fun {ι} {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J} source target N P F A seedRead state =>
  have piece := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source state.1;
  if piece.length ≤ 1 then
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
      ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.shortSourceCost piece (OAI.EditApproximation.substring target state.2.lo state.2.hi))
  else
    (seedRead state).bind fun answer =>
      have value := ⌈answer⌉₊;
      have keys := OAI.EditApproximation.rawInitialConeCenters N P A value state.2;
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect (fun q => seedRead (state.1, q)) keys fun answers =>
        have localTable := fun q =>
          if q = state.2 then value else ⌈OAI.EditApproximation.finiteAnswerTable keys answers q⌉₊;
        OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
          (OAI.EditApproximation.localInitialConeTable piece target localTable N P F A
            (OAI.EditApproximation.refinementFactor A F) state.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryInitialGather.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {β : Type u_2} →
    {n : ℕ} →
      ℕ →
        (P : ℕ) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              0 < P →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
                        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                      (List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
                          List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
                        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β :=
fun {ι} {β} {n} N P F exponent hP a q reader next =>
  (reader q).bind fun value =>
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSupportWithWork N P F exponent hP a value q)).bind
      fun keys => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect reader keys (next keys)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryBandGather.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {β : Type u_2} →
    {nx ny : ℕ} →
      (M : ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
          ℕ →
            (P : ℕ) →
              ℕ →
                ℕ →
                  0 < P →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                        OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                          (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                            (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                                OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                              (List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                                  List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                                    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
                                OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β :=
fun {ι} {β} {nx ny} M parent N P F exponent hP a initial q massReader initialReader next =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryBandInputsWithWork M parent N P F exponent hP a initial q)).bind
    fun keys =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect massReader keys fun mass =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect initialReader keys (next keys mass)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRefinementMassQuery : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  (pass copy T S t : ℕ) →
    0 < t →
      (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
        ↑node.fst < J →
          (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
            Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
              OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                { child //
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement pass copy node T t q) } :=
fun {M J ny} pass copy T S t ht node hbelow q input =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect
    (fun j =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRefinementWarmupRead pass copy T S t ht node q
        (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild node hbelow input.1) ⋯ ↑j ⋯ input.2)
    (List.ofFn fun j => j) fun values => OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done (values.sum / ↑T)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalMedianSeedQuery : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (T S : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny) →
            0 < parent.pass →
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                  { child //
                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S parent } :=
fun {M J ny} N T S copies multiplier parent hpass state =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect
    (fun copy => OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRead T S parent hpass state ↑copy) (List.finRange copies)
    fun values =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
      ↑(min N (OAI.EditApproximation.upperMedian (List.map (fun value => ⌈↑multiplier * value⌉₊) values)))
def OAI.EditApproximation.globalSeedRead : {M J ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (T S : ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        (ℕ → ℕ) →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J ny → ℕ) →
            (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J ny) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J ny →
                OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                  { child //
                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S parent } :=
fun {M J ny} N T S copies multiplier initial parent state =>
  if state.1 = parent.node then
    if hp : parent.pass = 0 then OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done ↑(initial state)
    else OAI.EditApproximation.physicalMedianSeedQuery N T S copies (multiplier (parent.pass - 1)) parent ⋯ state.2
  else OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.value : OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational → ℚ :=
fun a => ↑a.num / ↑a.den
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.zero : OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational :=
{ num := 0, den := 1, den_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.zero._proof_1 }
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.mul : OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational →
  OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational → OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational :=
fun a b => { num := a.num * b.num, den := a.den * b.den, den_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.lt : OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational →
  OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational → Bool :=
fun a b => decide (a.num * ↑b.den < b.num * ↑a.den)
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.inv : OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational →
  OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational :=
fun a =>
  if h : a.num = 0 then OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.zero
  else { num := a.num.sign * ↑a.den, den := a.num.natAbs, den_pos := ⋯ }
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.div : OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational →
  OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational → OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational :=
fun a b => a.mul b.inv
inductive OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree.{u} : ℕ → Type u → ℕ → Type u
number of parameters: 2
constructors:
OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree.leaf : {B : ℕ} →
  {α : Type u} → Option α → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α 0
OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree.branch : {B : ℕ} →
  {α : Type u} →
    {D : ℕ} →
      (Fin B → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α D) → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α (D + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseTargetFrame.{u} : {α : Type u} → ℕ → List α → List (Option α) :=
fun {α} k target => List.replicate k none ++ List.map some target ++ List.replicate k none
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseRootShift : (k : ℕ) → Fin (2 * k + 1) :=
fun k => ⟨k, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.endpointActionConnection : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} q states => OAI.EditApproximation.connectionBudget q.lo (List.ofFn states) q.hi
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseSourceTreeOfList.{u} : (B : ℕ) →
  {α : Type u} → (D : ℕ) → List α → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α D :=
fun B {α} x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => List α → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α x) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseSourceTreeOfList._f B) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupIndexWithWork : {M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → List (Fin M) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M} h envelope values indices =>
  have terms := h.groupTermsWithWork envelope values indices;
  have total := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork terms.1;
  (total.1, terms.2 + total.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} q r =>
  have left :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q.lo).distanceWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo);
  have right :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat q.hi).distanceWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi);
  have result := left.1.canonicalAddWithWork right.1;
  (result.1, left.2 + right.2 + result.2 + q.lo.size + q.hi.size + r.lo.size + r.hi.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n M} r states =>
  have result :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo).connectionWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi)
      (List.ofFn states);
  (result.1, result.2 + r.lo.size + r.hi.size + M + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength r =>
  have targetLength :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.hi).subWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat r.lo);
  have result := targetLength.1.subWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat sourceLength);
  (result.1, targetLength.2 + result.2 + r.hi.size + r.lo.size + sourceLength.size + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScaleSourceWithWork : {M : ℕ} →
  (k : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
        Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
          (Fin k → Fin M × ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M} k h envelope values draw =>
  have indices :=
    if k = M then (List.finRange M, 0) else OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork draw (List.finRange k);
  have subtotal := h.groupIndexWithWork envelope values indices.1;
  have factor :=
    if k = M then (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one, 1)
    else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat k);
  have result := factor.1.canonicalMulWithWork subtotal.1;
  (result.1, indices.2 + subtotal.2 + factor.2 + result.2 + M.size + k.size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n M} r read states =>
  have words := OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => read i (states i)) (List.finRange M);
  have connection := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionWithWork r states;
  have children := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words.1;
  have result := connection.1.canonicalAddWithWork children.1;
  (result.1, words.2 + connection.2 + children.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength U a L query center =>
  have gap := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork sourceLength center;
  have seed := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledSeedWithWork (U center) a gap.1;
  have distance := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork query center;
  have spatial := L.canonicalMulWithWork distance.1;
  have result := seed.1.canonicalAddWithWork spatial.1;
  (result.1, gap.2 + seed.2 + distance.2 + spatial.2 + result.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointActionWithWork : {ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
          (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M ×
        ℕ :=
fun {ny M} center states =>
  have connection := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionWithWork center states;
  ({ center := center, connection := connection.1, childState := fun i => (i, states i) }, connection.2 + M + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} r read footprint states =>
  have words := (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (fun i => read i (states i)) (List.finRange M)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation (fun i => footprint i (states i)) (List.finRange M) +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionAllocation r states +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation words +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointConnectionWithWork r states).1.canonicalAddAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words).1 +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength U a L query center =>
  have gap := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork sourceLength center).1;
  have seed := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledSeedWithWork U a gap).1;
  have distance := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork query center).1;
  have spatial := (L.canonicalMulWithWork distance).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapAllocation sourceLength center +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.scaledSeedAllocation U a gap +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceAllocation query center +
      L.canonicalMulAllocation distance +
    seed.canonicalAddAllocation spatial
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScaleSourceAllocation : {M : ℕ} →
  (k : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
        Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → (Fin k → Fin M × ℕ) → (Fin k → ℕ) → ℕ :=
fun {M} k h envelope values draw allocation =>
  have indices :=
    if k = M then (List.finRange M, 0) else OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork draw (List.finRange k);
  have factor :=
    if k = M then (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one, 1)
    else (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M).divWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat k);
  ((if k = M then 0 else OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation allocation (List.finRange k)) +
            h.groupIndexAllocation envelope values indices.1 +
          if k = M then 0
          else
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat M).divAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat k)) +
        factor.1.canonicalMulAllocation (h.groupIndexWithWork envelope values indices.1).1 +
      M.size +
    k.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.gapGuardAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M → Vector (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M → Fin M → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent envelope rounded i =>
  have sourceLength :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalSubtractWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi
        (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo).1;
  have gap :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue sourceLength)
        (rounded.get i)).1;
  have bound := ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulWithWork (envelope.get i)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitSubtractAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi.bits
          (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo.bits false +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue sourceLength)
          (rounded.get i) +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 2).canonicalMulAllocation (envelope.get i) +
    gap.absolute.leAllocation bound
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideMinimumReadWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) →
        actions ≠ [] → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n M} r read actions hne =>
  have fallback := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadWithWork r read (actions.head hne);
  have scores :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadWithWork r read)
      actions;
  have result := fallback.1.minimumWithWork scores.1;
  (result.1, fallback.2 + scores.2 + result.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupMemberSourceWithWork : {M T : ℕ} →
  (Fin T → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
    (count : Fin T → ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
          Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
            ((s : Fin T) → Fin (count s) → Fin M × ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M T} scales count connection envelope values draw =>
  have terms :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
      (fun s =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScaleSourceWithWork (count s) (scales s) envelope values (draw s))
      (List.finRange T);
  have subtotal := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork terms.1;
  have result := connection.canonicalAddWithWork subtotal.1;
  (result.1, terms.2 + subtotal.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesWithWork : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  (M : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
        ℕ →
          List
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) ×
            ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent representatives P =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapWithWork
    (fun br =>
      have grids := OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridWithWork M parent br.2 br.1 P;
      have actions :=
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
          (fun states => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointActionWithWork br.2 states.get) grids.1;
      (actions.1, grids.2 + actions.2 + 1))
    representatives
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength read a L query center =>
  have seed := read center;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeWithWork sourceLength
      (fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue seed.1) a L query center;
  (result.1, seed.2 + result.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideMinimumReadAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
        (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) → actions ≠ [] → ℕ :=
fun {n M} r read footprint actions hne =>
  have fallback := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadWithWork r read (actions.head hne)).1;
  have scores :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadWithWork r read)
        actions).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadAllocation r read footprint (actions.head hne) +
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointScoreReadAllocation r read footprint) actions +
      fallback.minimumAllocation scores +
    0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupMemberSourceAllocation : {M T : ℕ} →
  (Fin T → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
    (count : Fin T → ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
          Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
            ((s : Fin T) → Fin (count s) → Fin M × ℕ) → ((s : Fin T) → Fin (count s) → ℕ) → ℕ :=
fun {M T} scales count connection envelope values draw allocation =>
  have terms :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
        (fun s =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScaleSourceWithWork (count s) (scales s) envelope values (draw s))
        (List.finRange T)).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
        (fun s =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.groupScaleSourceAllocation (count s) (scales s) envelope values (draw s)
            (allocation s))
        (List.finRange T) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation terms +
    connection.canonicalAddAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork terms).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesAllocation : {nx ny : ℕ} →
  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx → List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny} M parent representatives P =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
    (fun br =>
      have grids := OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridWithWork M parent br.2 br.1 P;
      have actions :=
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
          (fun states => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointActionWithWork br.2 states.get) grids.1;
      (actions.1, grids.2 + actions.2 + 1))
    (fun br =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridAllocation M parent br.2 br.1 P +
        OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
          (fun states => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointActionAllocation br.2 states.get)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridWithWork M parent br.2 br.1 P).1)
    representatives
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeFromActionsReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r sourceLength b tau theta read actions =>
  if hne : actions = [] then ([], 1)
  else
    have Z := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideMinimumReadWithWork r read actions hne;
    have positive := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero.ltWithWork Z.1;
    if positive.1 = true then
      have gap := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork sourceLength r;
      have velocity := gap.1.divWithWork Z.1;
      have result :=
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandFromActionsReadWithWork parent r b tau theta velocity.1 read actions
          hne;
      (result.1, Z.2 + positive.2 + gap.2 + velocity.2 + result.2 + 3)
    else ([], Z.2 + positive.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.physicalContinuationReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  List
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                        (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) →
                    List
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                          (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent representatives P F delta fallback old current groups online query =>
  have wide := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesWithWork M parent representatives P;
  have L :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 128).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F);
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localContinuationReadWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork L.1 delta fallback old current query wide.1 groups
      online;
  (result.1, wide.2 + L.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialMinimumReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
              List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength read N F a query centers =>
  have queryWord := read query;
  have zeroTest := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue queryWord.1) 0;
  if zeroTest.1 = true then (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, queryWord.2 + zeroTest.2 + 1)
  else
    have L :=
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 32).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F);
    have coefficient := L.1.canonicalAddWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one;
    have fallback := coefficient.1.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat N);
    have scores :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeReadWithWork sourceLength read a L.1 query) centers;
    have result := fallback.1.minimumWithWork scores.1;
    (result.1, queryWord.2 + zeroTest.2 + L.2 + coefficient.2 + fallback.2 + scores.2 + result.2 + F.size + N.size + 6)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent N P F a initial child query =>
  have fallback := initial query;
  have representatives := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial query;
  have actions :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesWithWork M parent representatives.1 P;
  have L :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 128).canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F);
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork L.1 fallback.1 child query actions.1;
  (result.1, fallback.2 + representatives.2 + actions.2 + L.2 + result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeFromActionsReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r sourceLength b tau theta read allocation actions =>
  if hne : actions = [] then 0
  else
    have Z := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideMinimumReadWithWork r read actions hne).1;
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideMinimumReadAllocation r read allocation actions hne +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero.ltAllocation Z +
      if (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero.ltWithWork Z).1 = true then
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapAllocation sourceLength r +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork sourceLength r).1.divAllocation Z +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandFromActionsReadAllocation parent r b tau theta
            ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.stateGapWithWork sourceLength r).1.divWithWork Z).1 read allocation
            actions hne
      else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.physicalContinuationReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                    (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                      List
                          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                            (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) →
                        List
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                              (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent representatives P F delta fallback old current oldAllocation currentAllocation groups online
    query =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesAllocation M parent representatives P +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 128).canonicalMulAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localContinuationReadAllocation
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceAllocation
      ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 128).canonicalMulWithWork
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1
      delta fallback old current oldAllocation currentAllocation query
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesWithWork M parent representatives P).1 groups
      online
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent N P F a initial initialAllocation child childAllocation query =>
  have representatives :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial query).1;
  have actions :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesWithWork M parent representatives P).1;
  initialAllocation query +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadAllocation N P F a initial initialAllocation
            query +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.wideActionsFromRepresentativesAllocation M parent representatives P +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 128).canonicalMulAllocation
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bellmanReadAllocation
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceWithWork
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.endpointDistanceAllocation
      ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 128).canonicalMulWithWork
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1
      (initial query).1 child childAllocation query actions
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedRepresentativeReadWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent center sourceLength b P tau theta read =>
  have wide := OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridWithWork M parent center b P;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeFromActionsReadWithWork parent center sourceLength b tau theta
      read (List.map Vector.get wide.1);
  (result.1, wide.2 + result.2 + wide.1.length + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialProgramReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength N P F A a read query =>
  have centers := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCentersReadWithWork N P A read query;
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialMinimumReadWithWork sourceLength read N F a query centers.1;
  (result.1, centers.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedRepresentativeReadAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent center sourceLength b P tau theta read allocation =>
  have wide := (OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridWithWork M parent center b P).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionGridAllocation M parent center b P + wide.length +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.representativeFromActionsReadAllocation parent center sourceLength b tau theta
      read allocation (List.map Vector.get wide)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRefinementFinishAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) →
                  ℕ →
                    List (Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                      List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                        List
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                              (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) →
                          List
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
                                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) →
                            List (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                              List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent N P F a delta initial initialAllocation t earlierKeys earlierWords groups online currentKeys
    currentWords query =>
  initialAllocation query +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadAllocation N P F a initial initialAllocation query +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.physicalContinuationReadAllocation parent
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localRepresentativeReadWithWork N P F a initial query).1 P F delta
