      (initial query).1 (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryHistoryReadWithWork earlierKeys earlierWords (t - 1))
      (fun input =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork currentKeys currentWords input.1 input.2)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryHistoryReadAllocation earlierKeys earlierWords (t - 1))
      (fun input =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadAllocation currentKeys currentWords input.1 input.2)
      groups online query
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineRangeWithWork : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent P exponent H tau child key =>
  have width := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork parent.hi parent.lo;
  have band :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedRepresentativeReadWithWork parent key.2
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width.1) key.1 P tau
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) child;
  have setup := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineIndexSetupWithWork H band.1;
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork setup.1.bits 80;
  have mass := OAI.EditApproximation.triangularMassWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue power.1);
  (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue mass.1, width.2 + band.2 + setup.2 + power.2 + mass.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cachedInitialProgramReadWithWork : {n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {n} sourceLength N P F A a read query =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedWithWork
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialProgramReadWithWork sourceLength N P F A a read query) [true]
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineRangeAllocation : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    ℕ →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ) → ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℕ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent P exponent H tau child allocation key =>
  have width := (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork parent.hi parent.lo).1;
  have band :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedRepresentativeReadWithWork parent key.2
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width) key.1 P tau
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) child).1;
  have setup := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineIndexSetupWithWork H band).1;
  have power := (OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork setup.bits 80).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractAllocation parent.hi parent.lo +
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedRepresentativeReadAllocation parent key.2
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue width) key.1 P tau
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent) child allocation +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineIndexSetupAllocation H band +
      OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation setup.bits 80 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.triangularMassAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue power)
def OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.count : OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts → ℕ :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.empty => 0
  | OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.node n left right => n

  OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.empty => OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.empty
  | OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.node count l right => l
def OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.right : OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts :=
fun x =>
  match x with
  | OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.empty => OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.empty
  | OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.node count left r => r

fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.storageBits._f

fun tree x x_1 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts) x
    OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.add._f tree x_1

def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.bind.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {α : Type u_2} →
    {β : Type u_3} →
      {program : OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι α} →
        program.Footprint →
          (next : α → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
            ((value : α) → (next value).Footprint) → (program.bind next).Footprint :=
fun {ι} {α} {β} {program} heap next allocation =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.brecOn heap
    (@OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.bind._f ι α β next allocation)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.compute.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →

  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.charge allocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.done execution.1)

fun {ι} {β} {steps} {program} allocation tail => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.charge allocation tail
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.runCells.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (ι →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult κ) →
      (ι → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ) →
        {program : OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction} →
          program.Footprint → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun {ι} {κ} ask childCells {program} x x_1 =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.brecOn (motive := fun {program} x =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ) x
    (@OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.runCells._f ι κ ask childCells) x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →

def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    {β : Type u_3} →
      (reader : κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
        ((key : κ) → (reader key).Footprint) →
          (inputs : List κ) →
            (next : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
              ((words : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) → (next words).Footprint) →
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect reader inputs next).Footprint :=
fun {ι} {κ} {β} reader allocation x x_1 x_2 =>
  List.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    (x_3 : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
      ((words : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) → (x_3 words).Footprint) →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect reader x x_3).Footprint)
    x (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect._f reader allocation) x_1 x_2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.mapKeysWithWork.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    {β : Type u_3} →
      (f : ι → κ × ℕ) →
        (ι → ℕ) →
          {program : OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β} →

  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.brecOn heap
    (@OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.mapKeysWithWork._f ι κ β f allocation)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.restrictWithWork.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  (P : ι → Prop) →
    (test : ι → Bool × ℕ) →
      (htest : ∀ (key : ι), (test key).1 = true ↔ P key) →
        (ι → ℕ) →
          {program : OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction} →
            program.Footprint → (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.restrictWithWork P test htest program).Footprint :=
fun {ι} P test htest allocation {program} heap =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.brecOn heap
    (@OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.restrictWithWork._f ι P test htest allocation)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.normalize.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {program : OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction} →
    program.Footprint →
      (base : List Bool) →
        (hbase : 0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base) →
          (remaining : ℕ) → (program.normalize base hbase remaining).Footprint :=
fun {ι} {program} heap base hbase remaining =>
  heap.bind
    (fun word =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedWithWork (word, 0) base hbase remaining))
    fun word =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedWithWork (word, 0) base hbase remaining)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedAllocation (word, 0) 0 base hbase remaining)
def OAI.EditApproximation.bandActionPredicate : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        ℚ →
          ℚ →
            ℚ →
              (actions : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)) →
                actions ≠ [] →
                  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) →
                    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → Prop :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b tau theta velocity actions hnonempty child states =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.endpointActionConnection r states = 0 ∧
    (∀ (i : Fin M),
        (r.lo + ((OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo - parent.lo)).dist (states i).lo ≤ 6 * b ∧
          (r.lo + ((OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi - parent.lo)).dist (states i).hi ≤ 6 * b) ∧
      ∀ (j : Fin M),
        0 < ↑j →
          |OAI.EditApproximation.bandCutShift parent states j - (↑r.lo - ↑parent.lo) -
                velocity *
                  OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWidePrefixMinimum parent r actions hnonempty child (↑j)
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.bandCutShift parent states j)| ≤
            tau * theta * ↑b / ↑M
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryOnlineSourceGather.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {β : Type u_2} →
    {nx ny M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
        ℕ →
          (P : ℕ) →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℕ →
                  0 < P →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                              (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                                  ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                                (List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                                    List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
                                  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β :=
fun {ι} {β} {nx ny M} parent N P F exponent H hP tau a initial child q reader next =>
  have query := initial q;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge query.2
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineKeysWithWork N P F hP a query.1 q)).bind
      fun keys =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect
        (fun key =>
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineRangeWithWork parent P exponent H tau child key)).bind
            (reader key))
        keys (next keys))
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseLabelChildren.{u_1} : {B d : ℕ} →
  {α : Type u_1} →
    ℚ →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α d → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B d → ℚ → ℕ) →
            List
                (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α d ×
                  Fin B × OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B d) →
              ℕ :=
fun {B d} {α} Q lam allowance work x =>
  List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseLabelChildren._f Q lam allowance work)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedBandActions : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℚ →
            ℚ →
              ℚ →
                (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) →
                  List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b P tau theta velocity child =>
  have actions := OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent r b P;
  if h : actions = [] then []
  else
    if |velocity| ≤ 1 - tau then
      List.filter
        (fun states =>
          decide (OAI.EditApproximation.bandActionPredicate parent r b tau theta velocity actions h child states))
        actions
    else []
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalEndpointActionValue : {ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → ℚ :=
fun {ny M} r states child => ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.endpointActionConnection r states) + ∑ i, child i (states i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalActualWideActions : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
    List (ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      ℕ →
        List
          (OAI.EditApproximation.RationalBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny)
            (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) M) :=
fun {nx ny M} parent representatives P =>
  List.flatMap
    (fun br =>
      List.map
        (fun states =>
          { center := br.2, connection := ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.endpointActionConnection br.2 states),
            childState := fun i => (i, states i) })
        (OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent br.2 br.1 P))
    representatives
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupQueriedChildLabels : (M Q b : ℕ) →
  (h : ℚ) → (Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.groupSampleCount M Q b h) → Fin M) → Finset (Fin M) :=
fun M Q b h draw =>
  if OAI.EditApproximation.groupSampleCount M Q b h = M then Finset.univ else Finset.image draw Finset.univ
def OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWideActionMinimum : {nx ny M : ℕ} →
  (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx) →
    (r : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
      (b P : ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent r b P ≠ [] →
          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → ℚ) → ℚ :=
fun {nx ny M} parent r b P hnonempty child =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rationalMinimum
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalEndpointActionValue r
      ((OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent r b P).head hnonempty) child)
    (List.map (fun states => OAI.EditApproximation.rationalEndpointActionValue r states child)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent r b P))
def OAI.EditApproximation.groupCurrentInputCover.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        (b : ℕ) →
          ℕ →
            (Q : ℕ) →
              ℕ × ℕ →
                (h : ℚ) →
                  (Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.groupSampleCount M Q b h) → Fin M) →
                    Finset (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent b exponent Q cell h draw =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.groupQueriedChildLabels M Q b h draw).biUnion fun i =>
    Finset.image (fun r => (i, r))
      (OAI.EditApproximation.groupRoundedStateList source target parent b exponent cell i h).toFinset
def OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℚ →
                ℚ →
                  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                    List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent r b P tau theta child =>
  if h : OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent r b P = [] then []
  else
    have Z := OAI.EditApproximation.rationalWideActionMinimum parent r b P h child;
    if 0 < Z then
      OAI.EditApproximation.computedBandActions parent r b P tau theta
        (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalStateGap (OAI.EditApproximation.substring source parent.lo parent.hi) target r /
          Z)
        child
    else []
structure OAI.EditApproximation.UnskippedBand.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} (source target : List α) {M : ℕ}
  (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length) (b P thetaExponent : ℕ) (tau F : ℚ)
  (child initial : Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) : Type
number of parameters: 12
fields:

        self.center b P tau (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) child ≠
      []

        OAI.EditApproximation.bandChildEnvelope b
          (OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent self.center b P tau
            (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) child)
          initial i ≤
      64 * F * ↑b

        (OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent self.center b P tau
          (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) child)
        ⋯ initial ∈
      OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent self.center b P

      |OAI.EditApproximation.rationalStateGap
            (OAI.EditApproximation.substring source (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo
              (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi)
            target
            (OAI.EditApproximation.canonicalRoundedBandAction thetaExponent b
              (OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent self.center b P tau
                (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) child)
              ⋯ initial i)| ≤
        2 *
          OAI.EditApproximation.bandChildEnvelope b
            (OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent self.center b P tau
              (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) child)
            initial i

def OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineBandG.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℚ →
                  (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent P exponent H b tau child r =>
  H +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bandCoordinates
          (OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent r b P tau (2 ^ (-↑exponent))
              child).toFinset).card +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.testUnskippedBand.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length) →
        (b P thetaExponent : ℕ) →
          (tau F : ℚ) →
            (child initial : Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                Option
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.UnskippedBand source target parent b P thetaExponent tau F child initial) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent b P thetaExponent tau F child initial center =>
  let band :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent center b P tau (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) child;
  if hband : band = [] then none
  else
    if hsum : ∑ i, OAI.EditApproximation.bandChildEnvelope b band initial i ≤ 64 * F * ↑b then
      let rounded := OAI.EditApproximation.canonicalRoundedBandAction thetaExponent b band hband initial;
      if hwide : rounded ∈ OAI.EditApproximation.wideActionList M parent center b P then
        if hgap :
            ∀ (i : Fin M),
              |OAI.EditApproximation.rationalStateGap
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.substring source (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).lo
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChild parent i).hi)
                    target (rounded i)| ≤
                2 * OAI.EditApproximation.bandChildEnvelope b band initial i then
          some { center := center, nonempty := hband, sum_guard := hsum, wide_guard := hwide, imbalance_guard := hgap }
        else none
      else none
    else none
def OAI.EditApproximation.unskippedBandList.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length) →
        (b P thetaExponent : ℕ) →
          (tau F : ℚ) →
            (child initial : Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
              List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) →
                List (OAI.EditApproximation.UnskippedBand source target parent b P thetaExponent tau F child initial) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent b P thetaExponent tau F child initial centers =>
  List.filterMap (OAI.EditApproximation.testUnskippedBand source target parent b P thetaExponent tau F child initial)
    centers
def OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineCandidate.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℕ →
                  ℚ →
                    ℚ →
                      ℚ →
                        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                          (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalChildTables M target.length →
                              ℕ →
                                OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                  ℕ → Option (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalRationalAction M target.length) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent P F exponent H b tau eta kappa child initial children t r integer =>
  have proof_optimizerTriangularMass_succ_66 := OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineCandidate._proof_1;
  have proof_optimizerTriangularMass_twice_67 := ⋯;
  have proof_optimizerTriangularMass_positive_77 := ⋯;
  have proof_keyedOnlineBandG_positive_78 := @OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineCandidate._proof_7;
  have band := OAI.EditApproximation.representativeBandActions source target parent r b P tau (2 ^ (-↑exponent)) child;
  have envelope := OAI.EditApproximation.bandChildEnvelope b band initial;
  let G := OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineBandG source target parent P exponent H b tau child r;
  let K := G ^ 80;
  have accepted :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.boundedOnlineInteger (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass K) ⋯ integer;
  if hband : band = [] then none
  else
    match OAI.EditApproximation.testUnskippedBand source target parent b P exponent tau (↑F) child initial r with
    | none => none
    | some val =>
      some
        (OAI.EditApproximation.sharedBandRationalAction r
          (OAI.EditApproximation.finiteOptimizerActionDraw band hband envelope
            (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalOnlineLinear eta kappa
              (OAI.EditApproximation.childGain envelope fun s coordinate => children coordinate.1 s coordinate.2)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.childDecrement envelope fun s coordinate => children coordinate.1 s coordinate.2)
              t)
            G K accepted))
def OAI.EditApproximation.cellUnskippedBands.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length) →
        (b P F thetaExponent : ℕ) →
          (tau : ℚ) →
            ℚ →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                (child initial : Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                  ℕ × ℕ →
                    List
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.UnskippedBand source target parent b P thetaExponent tau (↑F) child
                        initial) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent b P F thetaExponent tau a parentInitial child initial cell =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.unskippedBandList source target parent b P thetaExponent tau (↑F) child initial
    (OAI.EditApproximation.cellRepresentativeStates target.length P F b a parentInitial (2 ^ (-↑thetaExponent)) cell)
def OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineCandidates.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℕ →
                  ℚ →
                    ℚ →
                      ℚ →
                        ℚ →
                          (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                            (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                              (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalChildTables M target.length →
                                  ℕ →
                                    (ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
                                      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                        List (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalRationalAction M target.length) :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent N P F exponent H tau a eta kappa parentInitial child initial children t integer q =>
  List.filterMap
    (fun b =>
      have r := OAI.EditApproximation.roundInterval (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacing b P) q;
      if ↑b / (4 * a) ≤ parentInitial r ∧ parentInitial r ≤ 8 * ↑F * ↑b then
        OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineCandidate source target parent P F exponent H b tau eta kappa child initial
          children t r (integer b r)
      else none)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicQueryWindow N (parentInitial q) a ↑F)

@[irreducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedMemoEvaluate.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery { child // rank child < rank key }) →
      ℕ → (κ → ℕ) → κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult κ :=
fun {κ} rank program bits encode key memory =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rankedMemoEvaluate._unary rank program bits encode ⟨key, memory⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.localWarmupStepQuery.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {nx ny M : ℕ} →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℚ →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) →
                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι :=
fun {ι} {nx ny M} parent N P F a initialRead childRead q =>
  (initialRead q).bind fun value =>
    have centers := OAI.EditApproximation.rawRefinementCenters N P F a value q;
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect initialRead centers fun answers =>
      have initial := fun r => if r = q then value else OAI.EditApproximation.finiteAnswerTable centers answers r;
      have representatives := OAI.EditApproximation.localRefinementRepresentatives N P F a initial q;
      have inputs := OAI.EditApproximation.wideWarmupInputs parent P representatives;
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect childRead inputs fun values =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
          (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalBellman initial
            (fun q r => ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.targetEndpointDistance q r)) (128 * ↑F)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalActualWideActions parent representatives P)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.finiteAnswerTable inputs values) q)
def OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineSourceRange.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (source target : List α) →
    {M : ℕ} →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℚ →
                (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℚ) →
                  ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ :=
fun {α} source target {M} parent P exponent H tau child key =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass
    (OAI.EditApproximation.keyedOnlineBandG source target parent P exponent H key.1 tau child key.2 ^ 80)
def OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceBuild : ℕ →
  ℕ → List (ℕ × ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts :=
fun symbolBits positionBits x => List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceBuild._f symbolBits positionBits)
def OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceBuildWithWork : ℕ →
  ℕ → List (ℕ × ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts × ℕ :=
fun symbolBits positionBits x =>
  List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceBuildWithWork._f symbolBits positionBits)
def OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceQueryWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo
    OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun table symbolBits positionBits a lo hi =>
  have key := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryMemoKey symbolBits a;
  have found := table.lookupCosted key;
  have tree := found.1.getD OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.empty;
  have first := tree.prefixWithWork positionBits lo;
  have last := tree.prefixWithWork positionBits hi;
  (decide (first.1 < last.1), symbolBits + found.2 + first.2 + last.2 + 2)
@[irreducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedBitMemoEvaluate.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery { child // rank child < rank key } OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      ℕ →
        (κ → ℕ) →
          κ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitRecursiveMemoResult κ :=
fun {κ} rank program bits encode key memory =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rankedBitMemoEvaluate._unary rank program bits encode ⟨key, memory⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupStepQuery.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {α : Type u_2} →
    [DecidableEq α] →
      {M J : ℕ} →
        List α →
          (target : List α) →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℕ →
                  ℚ →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) →
                      (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι) →
                        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery ι :=
fun {ι} {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J} source target N P F A seedRead childRead node q =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.localWarmupStepQuery (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length node) N P F
    (OAI.EditApproximation.refinementFactor A F)
    (fun r => OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F A seedRead (node, r)) childRead q
def OAI.EditApproximation.indexedSingletonCostWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo
    OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun table symbolBits positionBits a lo hi =>
  if lo = hi then (1, 1)
  else
    have query := OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceQueryWithWork table symbolBits positionBits a lo hi;
    (hi - lo - if query.1 = true then 1 else 0, query.2 + 4)
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceIndex : List ℤ →
  List ℤ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts :=
fun source target =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceBuild (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputSymbolBits source target)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputPositionBits target)
    (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target).zipIdx
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceBuildWithWork : List ℤ →
  List ℤ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts × ℕ :=
fun source target =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.occurrenceBuildWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputSymbolBits source target)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputPositionBits target)
    (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target).zipIdx
@[irreducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedMemoAddressWork.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery { child // rank child < rank key }) →
      ℕ → (κ → ℕ) → (κ → ℕ) → κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → ℕ :=
fun {κ} rank program bits encode cost key memory =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rankedMemoAddressWork._unary rank program bits encode cost ⟨key, memory⟩
@[irreducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedMemoKernelWork.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery { child // rank child < rank key }) →
      ℕ → (κ → ℕ) → (κ → ℕ) → κ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo ℚ → ℕ :=
fun {κ} rank program bits encode cost key memory =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rankedMemoKernelWork._unary rank program bits encode cost ⟨key, memory⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupRequestQuery.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J : ℕ} →
      List α →
        (target : List α) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℚ →
                  (pass copy T S j : ℕ) →
                    (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalNode M J) →
                      (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) →
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length →
                            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                              { child //
                                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup pass copy node j q) }) →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                            { child //
                              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
                                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                                  (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup pass copy node j q) } :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J} source target N P F A pass copy T S j node q seedRead =>
  match j, seedRead with
  | 0, seedRead => OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F A seedRead (node, q)
  | t.succ, seedRead =>
    if hbelow : ↑node.fst < J then
      if
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length node).hi -
              (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length node).lo ≤
            1 then
        OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F A seedRead (node, q)
      else
        OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupStepQuery source target N P F A seedRead
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery pass copy T S t node hbelow q) node q
    else OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F A seedRead (node, q)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalLocalOnlineKey : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J N n =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J ×
    Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N).length × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ClampedPhysicalSeed : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun M J ny N => OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J ny → Fin (N + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.finalTargetState : (n : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n :=
fun n => { lo := 0, hi := n, ordered := ⋯, valid := ⋯ }
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCommonKey : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.CommonRangeKey (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalLocalOnlineKey M J N n) B ⊕
    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalLocalGroupKey M J N n exponent

def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  (band : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) →
    Vector (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) (OAI.EditApproximation.listedBandCoordinates band).length ×
      ℕ :=
fun {M n} band =>
  have proof_arithmeticFlatMapWithWork_value_69 :=
    @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_arithmeticMapWithWork_value_70 := @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork._proof_2;
  have proof_memberByWithWork_value_71 := @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork._proof_3;
  have proof_dedupByWithWork_value_72 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork._proof_12;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_eq_3 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_eq_73 := ⋯;
  have proof_naturalEqualWithWork_value_74 := ⋯;
  have proof_coordinateEqualWithWork_value_75 := ⋯;
  have proof_bandCoordinatesWithWork_value_68 := ⋯;
  let coordinates := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.bandCoordinatesWithWork band;
  (Vector.ofFn fun i => coordinates.1.get ⟨↑i, ⋯⟩, coordinates.2 + coordinates.1.length + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.reduceOnlineIntegerWithWork : (K : ℕ) →
  0 < K → ℕ → Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass K) × ℕ :=
fun K hK integer =>
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;

  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_binaryNaturalDivModWithWork_spec_24 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;

  have proof_binaryNaturalAddWithWork_value_52 := ⋯;

  have proof_optimizerTriangularMass_twice_67 := ⋯;
  have proof_triangularMassWithWork_value_65 := ⋯;

  let divided := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivModWithWork integer (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue mass.1);
  (⟨OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue divided.2.1, ⋯⟩, mass.2 + divided.2.2 + integer.size + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedPreparedActionReadWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
    (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
            ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Option (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {M n} mass read eta kappa band G j t =>
  have coordinates := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork band;
  have vertices := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandVerticesWithWork coordinates.1 mass band;
  have linear :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.listedBandCoordinates band).length fun i =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineLinearReadWithWork (fun child => mass.get child) read eta kappa t
        (coordinates.1.get i);
  have selected := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.selectionWithWork vertices.1 linear.1 G j;
  (selected.1, coordinates.2 + vertices.2 + linear.2 + selected.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedPreparedActionKernelAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
    (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {M n} mass read footprint eta kappa band G j t =>
  have coordinates := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork band).1;
  have vertices := (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandVerticesWithWork coordinates mass band).1;
  have linear :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.listedBandCoordinates band).length fun i =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineLinearReadWithWork (fun child => mass.get child) read eta kappa t
          (coordinates.get i)).1;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandVerticesAllocation coordinates mass band +
      OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.listedBandCoordinates band).length fun i =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineLinearReadAllocation (fun child => mass.get child) read footprint eta
          kappa t (coordinates.get i)) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.selectionAllocation vertices linear G j
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandActionReadWithWork : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
              ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Option (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) × ℕ :=
fun {M n} b initial read eta kappa band G j t =>
  have mass := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.envelopeVectorReadWithWork b band initial;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedPreparedActionReadWithWork mass.1 read eta kappa band G j t;
  (selected.1, mass.2 + selected.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedPreparedActionAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
    (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {M n} mass read footprint eta kappa band G j t =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorAllocation band +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedPreparedActionKernelAllocation mass read footprint eta kappa band G j t
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedSampledOnlineReadWithWork : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    ℕ →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (band : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) →
                band ≠ [] →
                  ℕ →
                    (K : ℕ) →
                      ℕ →
                        Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass K) →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryBellmanAction (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)
                              (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) M ×
                            ℕ :=
fun {n M} center b initial read eta kappa band hne G K t integer =>
  have chosen := OAI.EditApproximation.triangularChoiceWithWork K integer;
  have selected :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandActionReadWithWork b initial read eta kappa band G (↑chosen.1) t;
  have states := selected.1.getD (band.head hne);
  ({ center := center, connection := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero, childState := fun i => (i, states i) },
    chosen.2 + selected.2 + M + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandActionAllocation : {M n : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
        (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {M n} b initial initialAllocation read footprint eta kappa band G j t =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.envelopeVectorReadAllocation b band initial initialAllocation +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedPreparedActionAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.envelopeVectorReadWithWork b band initial).1 read footprint eta kappa band G
      j t
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedSampledOnlineAllocation : {n M : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
    ℕ →
      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
          (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
            (ℕ → Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                  (band : List (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) →
                    band ≠ [] → ℕ → (K : ℕ) → ℕ → Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.optimizerTriangularMass K) → ℕ :=
fun {n M} _center b initial initialAllocation read readAllocation eta kappa band _hne G K t integer =>
  have chosen := (OAI.EditApproximation.triangularChoiceWithWork K integer).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.triangularChoiceAllocation K integer +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedBandActionAllocation b initial initialAllocation read readAllocation
          eta kappa band G (↑chosen) t +
      M +
    1
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} → {κ : Type u_2} → (κ → ℕ) → σ ⊕ κ → ℕ :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl val => 0
  | Sum.inr key => tableRank key + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.keyCode.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} → (σ → ℕ) → (κ → ℕ) → σ ⊕ κ → ℕ :=
fun {σ} {κ} sourceCode tableCode x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl source => 2 * sourceCode source
  | Sum.inr table => 2 * tableCode table + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.child.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (tableRank : κ → ℕ) →
      (parent : κ) →
        σ ⊕ { key // tableRank key < tableRank parent } →
          { key //
            OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank tableRank key <
              OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank tableRank (Sum.inr parent) } :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank parent x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl source => ⟨Sum.inl source, ⋯⟩
  | Sum.inr table => ⟨Sum.inr ↑table, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.body.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (tableRank : κ → ℕ) →
      ((parent : κ) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery (σ ⊕ { key // tableRank key < tableRank parent })) →
        (σ → ℚ) →
          (key : σ ⊕ κ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
              { next //
                OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank tableRank next <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank tableRank key } :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank tableBody sourceValue key =>
  match key with
  | Sum.inl source => OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done (sourceValue source)
  | Sum.inr table =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys (OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.child tableRank table)
      (tableBody table)
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.bitBody.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (tableRank : κ → ℕ) →
      ((parent : κ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery (σ ⊕ { key // tableRank key < tableRank parent })
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
        (σ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
          (key : σ ⊕ κ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
              { next //
                OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank tableRank next <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.rank tableRank key }
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank tableBody sourceValue key =>
  match key with
  | Sum.inl source => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute (sourceValue source)
  | Sum.inr table =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.mapKeysWithWork
      (fun key => (OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.child tableRank table key, 1)) (tableBody table)
def OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.depth : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J ny passes copies T S → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl entry => J - ↑entry.1.fst
  | Sum.inr request => (↑request).remainingDepth
def OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.index : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J ny passes copies T S → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl val => 0
  | Sum.inr request => OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.rank T S ↑request + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J ny passes copies T S → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} request => request.depth + request.index
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousRead : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
    0 < (↑parent).pass →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
        Fin copies →
          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
            { child // child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) } :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} parent hpass state copy =>
  have proof_table_rank_lt_99 := @OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousRead._proof_1;
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_rank_lt_previous_pass_98 := @OAI.EditApproximation.previousPassRead._proof_2;
  have proof_previousPassRequest_progress_97 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read ⟨Sum.inr (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousPass parent state copy), ⋯⟩
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedWarmupChild : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
    ↑(↑parent).node.fst < J →
      0 < (↑parent).index →
        Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
            { child // child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) } :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} parent hbelow hindex input =>
  have proof_table_rank_lt_99 := @OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousRead._proof_1;
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_warmup_previous_child_progress_81 := ⋯;
  have proof_combinedRank_warmup_child_79 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read
    ⟨Sum.inr
        ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup (↑parent).pass (↑parent).copy
            (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild (↑parent).node hbelow input.1) ((↑parent).index - 1) input.2,
          ⋯⟩,
      ⋯⟩
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  ℕ →
    (ℕ → ℕ) →
      (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J ny →
          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
            { child // child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) } :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} N multiplier parent state =>
  have proof_coarse_rank_lt_100 := @OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery._proof_2;
  if hn : state.1 = (↑parent).node then
    if hp : (↑parent).pass = 0 then
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.read ⟨Sum.inl state, ⋯⟩ OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
    else
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.collect (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousRead parent ⋯ state.2)
        (List.finRange copies) fun values =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done
          ↑(min N
              (OAI.EditApproximation.upperMedian
                (List.map (fun value => ⌈↑(multiplier ((↑parent).pass - 1)) * value⌉₊) values)))
  else OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedWarmupQuery.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J passes copies T S : ℕ} →
      List α →
        (target : List α) →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                (ℕ → ℚ) →
                  (ℕ → ℕ) →
                    (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                        { child // child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) } :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J passes copies T S} source target N P F factor multiplier parent =>
  if hz : (↑parent).index = 0 then
    OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F (factor (↑parent).pass)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery N multiplier parent) ((↑parent).node, (↑parent).state)
  else
    if hbelow : ↑(↑parent).node.fst < J then
      if
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).hi -
              (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).lo ≤
            1 then
        OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F (factor (↑parent).pass)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery N multiplier parent) ((↑parent).node, (↑parent).state)
      else
        OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupStepQuery source target N P F (factor (↑parent).pass)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery N multiplier parent)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedWarmupChild parent hbelow ⋯) (↑parent).node (↑parent).state
    else
      OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F (factor (↑parent).pass)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery N multiplier parent) ((↑parent).node, (↑parent).state)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth.{u} : {α : Type u} → List α → List α → ℕ :=
fun {α} x y => max x.length y.length
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex : ℕ → Type :=
fun n => Fin (Nat.clog 2 n + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseGuess : (n : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex n → ℕ :=
fun n i => 2 ^ ↑i
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryTree.{u} : (B D : ℕ) →
  {α : Type u} → List α → List α → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B (Option α) D :=
fun B D {α} x y =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.coarseSourceTreeOfList B D
    (OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePadTo (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) x)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialMeasure : (B D n : ℕ) →
  [NeZero B] →
    [inst : MeasurableSpace (Fin B)] →
      [MeasurableSingletonClass (Fin B)] →
        MeasureTheory.Measure
          (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex n → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D) :=
fun B D n [NeZero B] [MeasurableSpace (Fin B)] [MeasurableSingletonClass (Fin B)] =>
  MeasureTheory.Measure.pi fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.coarseDrawMeasure B D
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseActiveChildrenWork.{u} : {B D : ℕ} →
  {α : Type u} →
    ℚ →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α D → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D → ℚ → ℕ) →
            List
                (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α D ×
                  Fin B × OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D) →
              ℕ :=
fun {B D} {α} Q lam allowance work x =>
  List.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseActiveChildrenWork._f Q lam allowance work)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseFullSeedDraws.{u_1, u_2} : ℕ →
  {I : Type u_1} → {α : Type u_2} → (I → ℕ) → (I → List α) → (I → List α) → Type u_1 :=
fun B {I} {α} depth x y =>
  (i : I) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth (x i) (y i)) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B (depth i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseFullSeedMeasure.{u_1, u_2} : (B : ℕ) →
  [NeZero B] →
    {I : Type u_1} →
      {α : Type u_2} →
        [Fintype I] →
          (depth : I → ℕ) →
            (x y : I → List α) →
              [inst : MeasurableSpace (Fin B)] →
                [MeasurableSingletonClass (Fin B)] →
                  MeasureTheory.Measure (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseFullSeedDraws B depth x y) :=
fun B [NeZero B] {I} {α} [Fintype I] depth x y [MeasurableSpace (Fin B)] [MeasurableSingletonClass (Fin B)] =>
  MeasureTheory.Measure.pi fun i =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialMeasure B (depth i) (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth (x i) (y i))
def OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionAcceptedLaw.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  [Fintype ι] → (range : ι → ℕ) → [∀ (i : ι), NeZero (range i)] → MeasureTheory.Measure ((i : ι) → Fin (range i)) :=
fun {ι} [Fintype ι] range [∀ (i : ι), NeZero (range i)] =>
  MeasureTheory.Measure.pi fun i => (PMF.uniformOfFintype (Fin (range i))).toMeasure
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} → (ι → ℕ) → Type u_1 :=
fun {ι} range => (ι → ℕ) × ((i : ι) → Fin (range i))
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.fractionStorage : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
  ℕ :=
fun x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.brecOn x OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.fractionStorage._f
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.valueStorage.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  (α → ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo α → ℕ :=
fun {α} bits x => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.brecOn x (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.valueStorage._f bits)
def OAI.EditApproximation.indexedLeafWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
  ℕ → ℕ → Option ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun table symbolBits positionBits symbol lo hi =>
  match symbol with
  | none => (hi - lo, 2)
  | some a => OAI.EditApproximation.indexedSingletonCostWithWork table symbolBits positionBits a lo hi
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceStorage : List ℤ → List ℤ → ℕ :=
fun source target =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo.valueStorage OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts.storageBits
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceIndex source target)
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerStoredDataSpace : List ℤ → List ℤ → ℕ :=
fun source target =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.storedIntegerBits source + OAI.EditApproximation.storedIntegerBits target +
        OAI.EditApproximation.storedNaturalBits (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.storedNaturalBits (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) +
    OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceStorage source target
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryIndexedLeafWithWork : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo
    OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
  ℕ → ℕ → Option ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun table symbolBits positionBits symbol lo hi =>
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.indexedLeafWithWork table symbolBits positionBits symbol lo hi;
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat result.1, result.2 + result.1.size + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.binaryIndexedLeafAllocation : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo
    OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
  ℕ → ℕ → Option ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun table symbolBits positionBits symbol lo hi =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.indexedLeafAllocation symbolBits positionBits symbol lo hi +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.indexedLeafWithWork table symbolBits positionBits symbol lo hi).1.size +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryIndexedSliceWithWork : (source : List ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
    (targetLength : ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval targetLength →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
          ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun source interval targetLength q occurrence symbolBits positionBits =>
  have symbol := OAI.EditApproximation.querySliceHeadWithWork source interval;
  have result := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryIndexedLeafWithWork occurrence symbolBits positionBits symbol.1 q.lo q.hi;
  (result.1, symbol.2 + result.2 + 1)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryIndexedSliceAllocation : (source : List ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →
    (targetLength : ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval targetLength →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun source interval targetLength q occurrence symbolBits positionBits =>
  interval.lo.bits.length + interval.hi.bits.length + 1 +
    OAI.EditApproximation.binaryIndexedLeafAllocation occurrence symbolBits positionBits
      (OAI.EditApproximation.querySliceHeadWithWork source interval).1 q.lo q.hi
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialGridReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {n} read allocation P A radius q b =>
  have spacing := (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1;
  have points :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxWithWork n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing) radius q.lo q.hi).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
      OAI.EditApproximation.gridStateBoxAllocation n (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue spacing) radius q.lo q.hi +
    OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation
      (fun r =>
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCenterGuardWithWork b
              (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (read r).1) A).1,
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCenterGuardWithWork b
                (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (read r).1) A).2 +
            (read r).2))
      (fun r =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCenterGuardAllocation b
            (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (read r).1) A +
          allocation r)
      points
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
            OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} read allocation sourceLength a L query center =>
  allocation center +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeAllocation sourceLength
      (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (read center).1) a L query center
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCentersReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} read allocation N P A q =>
  have Uq := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (read q).1;
  have scales := (OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesWithWork N).1;
  have active :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.filterWithWork
        (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork Uq b A) scales).1;
  allocation q + OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScalesAllocation N +
      OAI.EditApproximation.filterAllocation
        (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardWithWork Uq b A)
        (fun b => OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialScaleGuardAllocation Uq b A) scales +
    OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticFlatMapAllocation
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialGridReadWithWork P A read Uq q)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialGridReadAllocation read allocation P A Uq q) active
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialMinimumReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → ℕ :=
fun {n} read allocation sourceLength N F a query centers =>
  have Uq := OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (read query).1;
  allocation query + OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualAllocation Uq 0 +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork Uq 0).1 = true then 0
    else
      have L :=
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 32).canonicalMulWithWork
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F)).1;
      have coefficient := (L.canonicalAddWithWork OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one).1;
      have fallback := (coefficient.canonicalMulWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat N)).1;
      have scores :=
        (OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapWithWork
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeReadWithWork sourceLength read a L query) centers).1;
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 32).canonicalMulAllocation
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat F) +
                  L.canonicalAddAllocation OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.one +
                coefficient.canonicalMulAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat N) +
              OAI.EditApproximation.arithmeticMapAllocation
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialConeReadAllocation read allocation sourceLength a L query)
                centers +
            fallback.minimumAllocation scores +
          F.size +
        N.size
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialProgramReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} read allocation sourceLength N P F A a query =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCentersReadAllocation read allocation N P A query +
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialMinimumReadAllocation read allocation sourceLength N F a query
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialCentersReadWithWork N P A read query).1
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.cachedInitialProgramReadAllocation : {n : ℕ} →
  (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → List Bool × ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n → ℕ :=
fun {n} read allocation sourceLength N P F A a query =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.finishCachedAllocation
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialProgramReadWithWork sourceLength N P F A a read query)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.initialProgramReadAllocation read allocation sourceLength N P F A a query)
    [true] OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.zero._proof_1 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersAllocation : List Bool → ℕ :=
fun word => OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation word 5 + OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation word 4 + 2
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementDecrementAllocation : List Bool → ℕ :=
fun word => OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation word 10 + 1
def OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledRefinementAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N pass =>
  if pass < OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N then
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoAllocation
        ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N) pass -
            OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) +
      2
  else 2
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementBaseAllocation : List Bool → ℕ :=
fun word =>
  have power := OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerWithWork word 10;
  have increment := OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddWithWork power.1 [true] false;
  OAI.EditApproximation.bitPowerAllocation word 10 + OAI.EditApproximation.bitAddAllocation power.1 [true] false +
      OAI.EditApproximation.trimBitWordAllocation increment.1 +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.refinementParameterAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → List Bool → ℕ :=
fun N pass word =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersAllocation word +
        OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledRefinementAllocation N pass +
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementDecrementAllocation word +
    5
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryPassAllocation : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N pass =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.signedPowerTwoAllocation
        ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicSeedExponent (OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedInitialExponent N) pass) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.refinementParameterAllocation N pass (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N).bits +
    2
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedQuerySetupAllocation : ℕ → ℕ :=
fun N =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.vectorMapAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) fun i =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryPassAllocation N ↑i) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.refinementBaseAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N).bits +
    5
def OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.nat : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational :=
fun n => { num := ↑n, den := 1, den_pos := OAI.EditApproximation.UnreducedRational.zero._proof_1 }
def OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedGroupMemberSourceWithWork : {M T : ℕ} →
  (Q b : ℕ) →
    (scales : Fin T → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
        Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
          Vector OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction M →
            ((t : Fin T) → Fin (OAI.EditApproximation.groupSampleCount M Q b (scales t).value) → Fin M × ℕ) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M T} Q b scales connection envelope values draw =>
  have proof_vectorMapWithWork_value_27 :=
    @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedGroupMemberSourceWithWork._proof_3;

  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordMinWithWork_value_29 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitSubtractor_value_17 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork._proof_7;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := OAI.EditApproximation.sourceChildWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_eq_3 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
