def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarCode : {M J N n exponent F Q R B : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalScalarKey M J N n exponent F Q R B → ℕ :=
fun {M J N n exponent F Q R B} key =>
  match key with
  | ⟨Sum.inl online, time⟩ =>
    2 *
      Nat.pair (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCode (↑online.1.1, online.1.2.2))
        (Nat.pair (↑online.1.2.1) (Nat.pair ↑↑online.2 ↑time))
  | ⟨Sum.inr group, index⟩ =>
    2 *
        Nat.pair (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalNodeCode ↑group.1)
          (Nat.pair (↑group.2.1)
            (Nat.pair (↑group.2.2.1)
              (Nat.pair (↑group.2.2.2) (Nat.pair (↑index.fst) (Nat.pair ↑index.snd.fst ↑index.snd.snd))))) +
      1
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarGroupQueries : (M J N n exponent F Q R B : ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
    Fin R → (b : ℕ) → ℕ × ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalGroupQueries M J N n exponent F Q R B b :=
fun M J N n exponent F Q R B node time b cell =>
  have proof_physicalScaleIndex_get_93 := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarGroupQueries._proof_1;
  if hb : b ∈ OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N then
    if hc : cell.1 ≤ n * 2 ^ exponent ∧ cell.2 ≤ n * 2 ^ exponent then
      let key := (node, OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex N b hb, ⟨cell.1, ⋯⟩, ⟨cell.2, ⋯⟩);
      ⋯.mp (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarGroupQueriesAt M J N n exponent F Q R B key time)
    else fun x x_1 => OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done 0
  else fun x x_1 => OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done 0

    Fin R →
      OAI.EditApproximation.GroupScalarKey M F Q →
        OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalScalarKey M J N n exponent F Q R B) :=
fun M J N n exponent F Q R B node time key =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarGroupQueries M J N n exponent F Q R B node time key.fst.1 key.fst.2 key.snd.fst
    key.snd.snd

  else
    OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F (factor request.pass)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.globalSeedRead N T S copies multiplier initial request) (request.node, request.state)

                                                        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                                                            child <
                                                          OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                                                            request }) :=
fun {σ} {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P F exponent H Q T S copies factor multiplier
    tau eta kappa delta readGroup readInteger initial request =>
  if hactive :
      T < request.index ∧
        ↑request.node.fst < J ∧
          ¬(OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length request.node).hi -
                (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length request.node).lo ≤
              1 then
    have ht := ⋯;
    have hrequest := ⋯;
    have node := ⟨request.node, ⋯⟩;
    have time := Fin.ofNat R (request.index - T - 1);
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys (Sum.map id fun child => ⟨↑child, ⋯⟩)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRandomDataRequestQuery source target N P F exponent H Q tau
        (OAI.EditApproximation.refinementFactor (factor request.pass) F) eta kappa delta request.pass request.copy T S
        (request.index - T) ht request.node ⋯ (readGroup request.pass request.copy node time)
        (readInteger request.pass request.copy node time) request.state)
  else
    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys Sum.inr
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInactiveGlobalQuery source target N P F T S copies factor multiplier initial
        request)
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.entryCapacity : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ :=
fun M J n => (n + 1) ^ 2 * (M ^ J * (J + 1))
def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.code : {M J n : ℕ} →
  ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n → ℕ :=
fun {M J n} copies T S request =>
  request.index +
    (T + S + 1) *
      (request.copy +
        copies *
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCode (request.node, request.state) +
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.entryCapacity M J n * request.pass))
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.supportedChild.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (tableRank : κ → ℕ) → (κ → Prop) → (parent : κ) → σ ⊕ { key // tableRank key < tableRank parent } → Prop :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank supported parent x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl val => True
  | Sum.inr table => supported ↑table
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.supportedChildMap.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (tableRank : κ → ℕ) →
      (supported : κ → Prop) →
        (parent : { key // supported key }) →
          { key // OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.supportedChild tableRank supported (↑parent) key } →
            σ ⊕ { key // tableRank ↑key < tableRank ↑parent } :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank supported parent x =>
  match x with
  | ⟨Sum.inl source, property⟩ => Sum.inl source
  | ⟨Sum.inr table, hs⟩ => Sum.inr ⟨⟨↑table, hs⟩, ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.restrictBody.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {κ : Type u_2} →
    (tableRank : κ → ℕ) →
      (supported : κ → Prop) →
        ((parent : κ) → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery (σ ⊕ { key // tableRank key < tableRank parent })) →
          (parent : { key // supported key }) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery (σ ⊕ { key // tableRank ↑key < tableRank ↑parent }) :=
fun {σ} {κ} tableRank supported tableBody parent =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys
    (OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.supportedChildMap tableRank supported parent)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.restrict
      (OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.supportedChild tableRank supported ↑parent) (tableBody ↑parent))
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRandomBoundedQuery.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {α : Type u_2} →
    [DecidableEq α] →
      {M J R : ℕ} →
        [NeZero M] →
          [NeZero R] →
            List α →
              (target : List α) →
                ℕ →
                  ℕ →
                    (F : ℕ) →
                      ℕ →
                        ℕ →
                          (Q T S passes copies : ℕ) →
                            (ℕ → ℚ) →
                              (ℕ → ℕ) →
                                ℚ →
                                  ℚ →
                                    ℚ →
                                      ℚ →
                                        (ℕ →
                                            ℕ →
                                              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
                                                Fin R →
                                                  OAI.EditApproximation.GroupScalarKey M F Q →
                                                    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery σ) →
                                          (ℕ →
                                              ℕ →
                                                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
                                                  Fin R →
                                                    ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                                      ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery σ) →
                                            (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
                                              (request :
                                                  { request //
                                                    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S
                                                      request }) →
                                                OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                                                  (σ ⊕
                                                    { child //
                                                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                                                          ↑child <
                                                        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S
                                                          ↑request }) :=
fun {σ} {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P F exponent H Q T S passes copies factor
    multiplier tau eta kappa delta readGroup readInteger initial request =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.restrictBody (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRandomGlobalQuery source target N P F exponent H Q T S copies factor multiplier tau
      eta kappa delta readGroup readInteger initial)
    request
def OAI.EditApproximation.chargedRandomGlobalQuery.{u_1, u_2} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {α : Type u_2} →
    [DecidableEq α] →
      {M J R : ℕ} →
        [NeZero M] →
          [NeZero R] →
            List α →
              (target : List α) →
                ℕ →
                  ℕ →
                    (F : ℕ) →
                      ℕ →
                        ℕ →
                          (Q T S passes copies : ℕ) →
                            (ℕ → ℚ) →
                              (ℕ → ℕ) →
                                ℚ →
                                  ℚ →
                                    ℚ →
                                      ℚ →
                                        (ℕ →
                                            ℕ →
                                              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
                                                Fin R →
                                                  OAI.EditApproximation.GroupScalarKey M F Q →
                                                    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery σ) →
                                          (ℕ →
                                              ℕ →
                                                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
                                                  Fin R →
                                                    ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                                      ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery σ) →
                                            (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
                                              (request :
                                                  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length passes
                                                    copies T S) →
                                                OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                                                  (σ ⊕ { child // child.rank < request.rank }) :=
fun {σ} {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P F exponent H Q T S passes copies factor
    multiplier tau eta kappa delta readGroup readInteger initial request =>
  have proof_table_rank_lt_99 := @OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousRead._proof_1;
  match request with
  | Sum.inl entry => OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.done ↑(initial entry)
  | Sum.inr parent =>
    if (↑parent).index ≤ T then
      OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys Sum.inr
        (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedWarmupQuery source target N P F factor multiplier parent)
    else
      if ↑(↑parent).node.fst < J then
        if
            (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).hi -
                (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).lo ≤
              1 then
          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys Sum.inr
            (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F (factor (↑parent).pass)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery N multiplier parent) ((↑parent).node, (↑parent).state))
        else
          OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys (Sum.map id fun child => ⟨Sum.inr ↑child, ⋯⟩)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalRandomBoundedQuery source target N P F exponent H Q T S passes copies factor
              multiplier tau eta kappa delta readGroup readInteger initial parent)
      else
        OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys Sum.inr
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalInitialQuery source target N P F (factor (↑parent).pass)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSeedQuery N multiplier parent) ((↑parent).node, (↑parent).state))
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {M J R : ℕ} → List α → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies =>
  Fin (passes + 1) ×
    Fin (copies + 1) ×
      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalScalarKey M J N target.length exponent F Q R
        (OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound M P H)

  {M J R : ℕ} → List α → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies =>
  (key : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies) →
    Fin
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarRange M J N target.length exponent F Q R
        (OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound M P H) key.2.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyAnswer.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →

      (N P F exponent H Q passes copies : ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarDraw target N P F exponent H Q passes copies →
          OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies → ℚ :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies draw key => ↑↑(draw key)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyGroupRead.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {M J R : ℕ} →
    (target : List α) →
      (N P F exponent H Q passes copies : ℕ) →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
              Fin R →
                OAI.EditApproximation.GroupScalarKey M F Q →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies) :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys
    (fun scalar => (Fin.ofNat (passes + 1) pass, Fin.ofNat (copies + 1) copy, scalar))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarGroupRead M J N target.length exponent F Q R
      (OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound M P H) node time key)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyOnlineRead.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →

    (target : List α) →
      (N P F exponent H Q passes copies : ℕ) →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
              Fin R →
                ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                  ℕ →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies) :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies pass copy node time key range =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.FiniteQuery.mapKeys
    (fun scalar => (Fin.ofNat (passes + 1) pass, Fin.ofNat (copies + 1) copy, scalar))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarOnlineRead M J N target.length exponent F Q R
      (OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound M P H) node time key range)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.QueryFamilyMemoKey : {M J R passes copies T S : ℕ} →

  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies ⊕
    OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length (passes + 1) copies T S
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryFamilySourceWordWithWork : {M J R passes copies : ℕ} →
  (target : List ℕ) →
    (N P F exponent H Q : ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarDraw target N P F exponent H Q passes copies →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ :=
fun {M J R passes copies} target N P F exponent H Q draw key =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat ↑(draw key), (↑(draw key)).size + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryFamilyMemoRemaining : {M J R passes copies T S : ℕ} →

fun {M J R passes copies T S} target N P F exponent H Q x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl val => 0
  | Sum.inr request => OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPhysicalRemaining request
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilySeed : List ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ → Type :=

    (OAI.EditApproximation.intervalTreeDepth (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M source.length) target.length
    N

    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2)

  OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilySeed source target N × OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyDraw source target N
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyCode.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →

fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies key =>
  Nat.pair (↑key.1) (Nat.pair (↑key.2.1) (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarCode key.2.2))
def OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.code : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J ny passes copies T S → ℕ :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl entry => 2 * OAI.EditApproximation.physicalEntryCode entry
  | Sum.inr request => 2 * OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.code copies T S ↑request + 1

      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2))
    OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.code
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialCandidate.{u} : {α : Type u} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    (B D : ℕ) →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          (x y : List α) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D → ℚ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] B D Q lam x y i draws =>
  have k := OAI.EditApproximation.coarseGuess (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) i;
  2 *
    (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseThresholdEstimate k Q lam
        (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseTargetFrame k
          (OAI.EditApproximation.coarsePadTo (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) y))
        (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryTree B D x y) draws (↑k) 0 (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseRootShift k) +
      ↑k)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialMinimum.{u} : {α : Type u} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    (B D : ℕ) →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          (x y : List α) →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D) →
              ℚ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] B D Q lam x y draws =>
  Finset.univ.inf' ⋯ fun i => OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialCandidate B D Q lam x y i (draws i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialValue.{u} : {α : Type u} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    (B D : ℕ) →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          ℕ →
            (x y : List α) →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D) →
                ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] B D Q lam N x y draws =>
  if 2 * x.length < y.length then min N y.length
  else if x = y then 0 else min N ⌈OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialMinimum B D Q lam x y draws⌉₊
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseActiveArrayWork.{u} : {B : ℕ} →
  {α : Type u} →
    ℕ →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          {D : ℕ} →
            OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α D → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D → ℚ → ℕ :=
fun {B} {α} k Q lam x x_1 x_2 x_3 =>
  Nat.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseSourceTree B α x → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B x → ℚ → ℕ) x
    (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseActiveArrayWork._f k Q lam) x_1 x_2 x_3
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseFullSeedTable.{u_1, u_2} : ℕ →
  (B : ℕ) →
    {I : Type u_1} →
      {α : Type u_2} →
        [DecidableEq α] →
          (depth : I → ℕ) → (x y : I → List α) → OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseFullSeedDraws B depth x y → I → ℕ :=
fun N B {I} {α} [DecidableEq α] depth x y draws i =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialValue B (depth i) (OAI.EditApproximation.initialThresholdQ N)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.initialLambda N) N (x i) (y i) (draws i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialArrayWork.{u} : {α : Type u} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    (B D : ℕ) →
      ℚ →
        ℚ →
          (x y : List α) →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseGuessIndex (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) →
                OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseThresholdDraws B D) →
              ℕ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] B D Q lam x y draws =>
  if 2 * x.length < y.length then 0
  else
    if x = y then 0
    else
      ∑ i,
        OAI.EditApproximation.coarseActiveArrayWork
          (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseGuess (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) i) Q lam
          (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryTree B D x y) (draws i)
          ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.coarseGuess (OAI.EditApproximation.coarseEntryWidth x y) i)
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerEqualWithWork : List ℤ → List ℤ → Bool × ℕ :=
fun x x_1 => List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List ℤ → Bool × ℕ) x OAI.EditApproximation.integerEqualWithWork._f x_1
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerPreprocessingWork : List ℤ → List ℤ → ℕ :=
fun source target =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputInspectionWithWork source target).2 +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.encodeIntegerSymbolsWithWork source).2 +
          (OAI.EditApproximation.encodeIntegerSymbolsWithWork target).2 +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceBuildWithWork source target).2 +
      (OAI.EditApproximation.integerEqualWithWork source target).2 +
    1
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyMemoKey : List ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun source target N =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyScalarKey source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2) ⊕
    OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyTableKey source target N
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerPreevaluationWork : List ℤ → List ℤ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → ℕ :=
fun source target N epsilon =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.integerPreprocessingWork source target + OAI.EditApproximation.globalParameterWork N +
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalLargeInputWithWork N epsilon).2
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyCodeWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {M J R : ℕ} →
    (target : List α) →
      (N P F exponent H Q passes copies : ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies → ℕ × ℕ :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies key =>
  have scalar := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarCodeWithWork key.2.2;
  have copy := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalPairWithWork (↑key.2.1) scalar.1;
  have pass := OAI.EditApproximation.naturalPairWithWork (↑key.1) copy.1;
  (pass.1, scalar.2 + copy.2 + pass.2 + 2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyMemoCodeWithWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N R : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyScalarKey source target N R ⊕
        OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyTableKey source target N →
      ℕ × ℕ :=
fun source target N R x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl key =>
    have result :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyCodeWithWork target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2) key;
    (2 * result.1, result.2 + 2)
  | Sum.inr key =>
    have result := OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPhysicalCodeWithWork key;
    (2 * result.1 + 1, result.2 + 3)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarGroupQueries : (M J N n exponent F Q R B : ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
    Fin R → (b : ℕ) → ℕ × ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BitPhysicalGroupQueries M J N n exponent F Q R B b :=
fun M J N n exponent F Q R B node time b cell =>
  have proof_log_two_add_one_eq_size_103 := ⋯;
  have proof_powerTwoWord_value_13 := ⋯;
  have proof_arithmeticMapWithWork_value_70 := @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork._proof_2;
  have proof_dyadicScalesWithWork_value_104 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_eq_3 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_eq_73 := ⋯;
  have proof_naturalEqualWithWork_value_74 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryNaturalIndexWithWork_value_105 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryScaleDispatchWithWork_value_106 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryScalePresentWithWork_value_101 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitMulWithWork_value_0 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalMulWithWork_value_55 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryCellValidWithWork_value_102 := ⋯;
  have proof_physicalScaleIndex_get_93 := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarGroupQueries._proof_1;
  if hb : (OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentWithWork N b).1 = true then
    if hc : (OAI.EditApproximation.queryCellValidWithWork n exponent cell).1 = true then
      let key := (node, OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex N b ⋯, ⟨cell.1, ⋯⟩, ⟨cell.2, ⋯⟩);
      ⋯.mp (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarGroupQueriesAt M J N n exponent F Q R B key time)
    else fun x x_1 => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0)
  else fun x x_1 => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarGroupRead : (M J N n exponent F Q R B : ℕ) →
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J →
    Fin R →
      OAI.EditApproximation.GroupScalarKey M F Q →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalScalarKey M J N n exponent F Q R B)
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun M J N n exponent F Q R B node time key =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentWithWork N key.fst.1).2 +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryCellValidWithWork n exponent key.fst.2).2 +
      2)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarGroupQueries M J N n exponent F Q R B node time key.fst.1 key.fst.2
      key.snd.fst key.snd.snd)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarOnlineRead : (M J N n exponent F Q R B : ℕ) →

    Fin R →
      ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
        ℕ →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalScalarKey M J N n exponent F Q R B)
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun M J N n exponent F Q R B node time key range =>
  have proof_log_two_add_one_eq_size_103 := ⋯;
  have proof_powerTwoWord_value_13 := ⋯;
  have proof_arithmeticMapWithWork_value_70 := @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.coordinateVectorWithWork._proof_2;
  have proof_dyadicScalesWithWork_value_104 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_eq_3 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_eq_73 := ⋯;
  have proof_naturalEqualWithWork_value_74 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryNaturalIndexWithWork_value_105 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryScaleDispatchWithWork_value_106 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryScalePresentWithWork_value_101 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryRangeValidWithWork_value_107 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentWithWork N key.1).2 +
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryRangeValidWithWork B range).2 +
      2)
    (if hb : (OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentWithWork N key.1).1 = true then
      if hr : (OAI.EditApproximation.queryRangeValidWithWork B range).1 = true then
        OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarRead M J N n exponent F Q R B
          ⟨Sum.inl
              ((node, OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex N key.1 ⋯, key.2),
                OAI.EditApproximation.positiveDrawRangeOfLE B range ⋯ ⋯),
            time⟩
      else OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0)
    else OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0))
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryFamilyTagWithWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {M J R : ℕ} →

              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies × ℕ :=

  have p := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork (passes + 1) pass ⋯;
  have c := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork (copies + 1) copy ⋯;
  have key := (p.1, c.1, scalar);
  (key,
    p.2 + c.2 + (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyCodeWithWork target N P F exponent H Q passes copies key).2 + 1)

@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.QueryPhysicalChild.{u_1} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {M J n : ℕ} →
    (passes copies T S : ℕ) →
      { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r } → Type (max u_1 0) :=

  σ ⊕
    { child //
      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S child <
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S ↑parent }
def OAI.EditApproximation.querySupportedChildWithWork.{u_1} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {M J n : ℕ} →

      (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.QueryPhysicalChild passes copies T S parent → Bool × ℕ :=
fun {σ} {M J n} passes copies T S parent key =>
  match key with
  | Sum.inl val => (true, 1)
  | Sum.inr child => OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalBoundedWithWork passes copies T S ↑child
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryRestrictPhysicalBody.{u_1} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {M J n : ℕ} →
    (passes copies T S : ℕ) →
      (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery (OAI.EditApproximation.QueryPhysicalChild passes copies T S parent)
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
            (σ ⊕
              { child //
                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S ↑child <
                  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S ↑parent })
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {σ} {M J n} passes copies T S parent program =>
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork._proof_12;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitAdder_value_1 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.onlineParametersWithWork._proof_1;
  have proof_bitAddWithWork_value_2 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalAddWithWork_value_52 := ⋯;
  have proof_queryPhysicalBoundedWithWork_value_109 := ⋯;
  have proof_querySupportedChildWithWork_value_108 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.mapKeysWithWork
    (fun key =>
      (OAI.EditApproximation.SourceRanked.supportedChildMap
          (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S) parent key,
        1))

        (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S) ↑parent)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.querySupportedChildWithWork passes copies T S parent) ⋯ program)

def OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedSeedAt : {M J n passes copies T S : ℕ} →

def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial : List ℕ →
  (target : List ℕ) →
    {M J passes copies T S : ℕ} →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                (ℕ → ℕ) →
                  (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
                      ℕ →
                        ℕ →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery

                                child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) }
                              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun source target {M J passes copies T S} N P F A a multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits positionBits q =>
  have interval := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node;
  have length := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork interval.hi interval.lo;
  have test := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork length.1 [true];
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge (length.2 + test.2 + 2)
    (if test.1 = true then
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryIndexedSliceWithWork source interval target.length q occurrence symbolBits
          positionBits)
    else
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryInitialConeProgramAt (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue length.1) N P F A a
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedSeedAt parent N multiplier) q)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryWarmupProgram.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {nx ny M : ℕ} →
    OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                (Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {ι} {nx ny M} parent N P F a initialRead childRead q =>
  (initialRead q).bind fun value =>
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersWithWork N P F a value q)).bind
      fun centers =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect initialRead centers fun words =>
        have initial := value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words;
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsWithWork M parent N P F a initial q)).bind
          fun inputs =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect childRead inputs fun childWords =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a initial
                (fun input =>
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                q)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedWarmupChild : {M J ny passes copies T S : ℕ} →
  (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
    ↑(↑parent).node.fst < J →
      0 < (↑parent).index →
        Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
            { child // child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) }
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {M J ny passes copies T S} parent hbelow hindex input =>
  have proof_table_rank_lt_99 := @OAI.EditApproximation.chargedPreviousRead._proof_1;
  have proof_combinedRank_lt_of_progress_80 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalWarmupChildQuery._proof_3;
  have proof_warmup_previous_child_progress_81 := ⋯;
  have proof_combinedRank_warmup_child_79 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedRead parent
    ⟨Sum.inr
        ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.warmup (↑parent).pass (↑parent).copy
            (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalChild (↑parent).node hbelow input.1) ((↑parent).index - 1) input.2,
          ⋯⟩,
      ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedWarmup : List ℕ →
  (target : List ℕ) →
    {M J passes copies T S : ℕ} →
      ℕ →
        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
                (ℕ → ℕ) →
                  (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryMemo OAI.EditApproximation.PositionCounts →
                      ℕ →
                        ℕ →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery
                            { child // child.rank < OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest.rank (Sum.inr parent) }
                            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun source target {M J passes copies T S} N P F A a multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits positionBits =>
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_bitOrdering_matches_4 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.invWithWork._proof_12;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_eq_3 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_eq_73 := ⋯;
  have proof_naturalEqualWithWork_value_74 := ⋯;
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge ((OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork (↑parent).index 0).2 + 1)
    (if hz : (OAI.EditApproximation.naturalEqualWithWork (↑parent).index 0).1 = true then
      OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial source target N P F A a multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits
        positionBits (↑parent).state
    else
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge
        ((OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork (↑(↑parent).node.fst) J).2 + 1)
        (if hb : (OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork (↑(↑parent).node.fst) J).1 = Ordering.lt then
          have interval := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node;
          have length := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork interval.hi interval.lo;
          have test := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork length.1 [true];
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge (length.2 + test.2 + 1)
            (if test.1 = true then
              OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial source target N P F A a multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits
                positionBits (↑parent).state
            else
              OAI.EditApproximation.queryWarmupProgram interval N P F a
                (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial source target N P F A a multiplier parent occurrence
                  symbolBits positionBits)
                (OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedWarmupChild parent ⋯ ⋯) (↑parent).state)
        else
          OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial source target N P F A a multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits
            positionBits (↑parent).state))
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedFamilyQueryKey : {M J : ℕ} →

        ℕ →
          ℕ →
            ℕ →
              ℕ →
                ℕ → ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length (passes + 1) copies T S → Type :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies ⊕
    { child // child.rank < parent.rank }
def OAI.EditApproximation.rankedNormalizeBody.{u_1} : {κ : Type u_1} →
  (rank : κ → ℕ) →
    ((key : κ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery { child // rank child < rank key } OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      (base : List Bool) →
        0 < OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue base →
          (κ → ℕ) →
            (key : κ) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery { child // rank child < rank key } OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction :=
fun {κ} rank raw base hbase remaining key => (raw key).normalize base hbase (remaining key)

@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseDraw.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →

  OAI.EditApproximation.CoarseFullSeedDraws B depth (fun state => OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source state.1)
    fun state => OAI.EditApproximation.substring target state.2.lo state.2.hi
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J : ℕ} →
      (source target : List α) →
        (N B : ℕ) →
          (depth : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseDraw source target B depth →
              OAI.EditApproximation.ClampedPhysicalSeed M J target.length N :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J} source target N B depth draw =>
  have proof_coarseInitialValue_le_length_111 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_1;
  have proof_trans_112 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_2;
  have proof_to_nil_113 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_4;
  have proof_single_114 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_5;
  have proof_symm_115 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_6;
  have proof_symm_116 := ⋯;
  have proof_exists_script_117 := ⋯;
  have proof_cost_spec_118 := ⋯;
  have proof_eq_of_zero_119 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_10;
  have proof_cost_le_120 := @OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable._proof_11;
  have proof_cost_refl_121 := ⋯;
  have proof_cost_eq_zero_iff_122 := ⋯;
  have proof_coarseInitialValue_zero_123 := ⋯;
  have proof_computed_fullSeedTable_range_zero_110 := ⋯;
  fun state =>
  ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.coarseFullSeedTable N B depth
      (fun state => OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source state.1)
      (fun state => OAI.EditApproximation.substring target state.2.lo state.2.hi) draw state,
    ⋯⟩
def OAI.EditApproximation.localFinalRootRequest.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {M J : ℕ} →
    (target : List α) →
      (N T S H : ℕ) →
        0 < H →
          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
            OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length
              (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (H ^ 2) T S :=
fun {α} {M J} target N T S H hH rootQ =>
  Sum.inr
    ⟨OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.refinement (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) 0
        ⟨⟨0, ⋯⟩, PUnit.unit⟩ T S rootQ,
      ⋯⟩

        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)

def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyMemoResult : {α : Type} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J R : ℕ} →
      [NeZero M] →
        [NeZero R] →
          List α →
            (target : List α) →
              (N P T S H : ℕ) →
                0 < H →
                  ℕ →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
                            (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N)
                            (H ^ 2) ⊕
                          OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (H ^ 2) T S →
                        ℕ) →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                        OAI.EditApproximation.ClampedPhysicalSeed M J target.length N ×
                            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarDraw target N P
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2) →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P
                                (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
                                H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
                                (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2) ⊕
                              OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length
                                (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (H ^ 2) T S) :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P T S H hH bits encode rootQ input =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.chargedRandomMemoEvaluate source target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
    (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) T S
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (H ^ 2) (OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledSeedFactor N)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.scheduledSeedMultiplier N)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParameters (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)).tau
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParameters (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)).eta
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParameters (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)).kappa
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rationalParameters (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)).delta
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyGroupRead target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
      (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyOnlineRead target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
      (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.clampedSeedNat input.1)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyAnswer target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
      (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2) input.2)
    bits encode (OAI.EditApproximation.localFinalRootRequest target N T S H hH rootQ)
def OAI.EditApproximation.selectionIndicator : Prop → ℝ :=
fun p => if p then 1 else 0
def OAI.EditApproximation.coarseIndexedEntryCostSum : {M J B : ℕ} →
  [NeZero B] →
    (source target : List ℕ) →
      ℕ →
        (depth : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
          OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseDraw source target B depth →
            (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → Prop) → ℝ :=
fun {M J B} [NeZero B] source target N depth draw selected =>
  ∑ entry,
    OAI.EditApproximation.selectionIndicator (selected entry) *
      ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.coarseIndexedEntryBinaryCost N B (depth entry)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source entry.1)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.substring target entry.2.lo entry.2.hi) (draw entry))
def OAI.EditApproximation.querySupportedChildAllocation.{u_1} : {σ : Type u_1} →
  {M J n : ℕ} →
    (passes copies T S : ℕ) →
      (parent : { r // OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.Bounded passes copies T S r }) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.QueryPhysicalChild passes copies T S parent → ℕ :=
fun {σ} {M J n} passes copies T S parent key =>
  match key with
  | Sum.inl val => 0
  | Sum.inr child => OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalBoundedAllocation passes copies T S ↑child
def OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionFailureLaw.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  (range : ι → ℕ) → [∀ (i : ι), NeZero (range i)] → ι → MeasureTheory.Measure ℕ :=
fun {ι} range [∀ (i : ι), NeZero (range i)] i =>
  have proof_rejectionWord_range_124 := OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionFailureLaw._proof_1;
  ProbabilityTheory.geometricMeasure
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionSuccessParameter (range i) (2 ^ OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionBitCount (range i)) ⋯
      ⋯)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionRuntimeArrayLaw.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  [Fintype ι] →
    (range : ι → ℕ) →
      [∀ (i : ι), NeZero (range i)] → MeasureTheory.Measure (OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray range) :=
fun {ι} [Fintype ι] range [∀ (i : ι), NeZero (range i)] =>
  (MeasureTheory.Measure.pi (OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionFailureLaw range)).prod
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionAcceptedLaw range)
def OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionSelectedBits.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  [Fintype ι] →
    (range : ι → ℕ) → (ι → ((i : ι) → Fin (range i)) → Prop) → OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray range → ℝ :=
fun {ι} [Fintype ι] range selected execution =>
  ∑ i,
    (↑(execution.1 i) + 1) * ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionBitCount (range i)) *
      OAI.EditApproximation.selectionIndicator (selected i execution.2)
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  {M J R : ℕ} →
    (target : List α) →
      (N P F exponent H Q passes copies : ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P F exponent H Q passes copies → ℕ :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P F exponent H Q passes copies key =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarRange M J N target.length exponent F Q R
    (OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound M P H) key.2.2

    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedPhysicalCoarseDepth source target N)

  OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
      (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2))

  (N : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedPhysicalCoarseDraw source target N) :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.coarseFullSeedMeasure (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).B
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedPhysicalCoarseDepth source target N)
    (fun entry => OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source entry.1) fun entry =>
    OAI.EditApproximation.substring target entry.2.lo entry.2.hi
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome : List ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ → Type :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedPhysicalCoarseDraw source target N ×
    OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedRejectionRuntime source target N
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionLaw : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N) :=
fun source target N =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.computedPhysicalCoarseLaw source target N).prod
    (OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionRuntimeArrayLaw
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2)))
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome : List ℕ → List ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun source target N => Unit ⊕ OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyRuntimeOutcome : {α : Type} →
  {M J R : ℕ} → List α → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Type :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P H =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.ClampedPhysicalSeed M J target.length N ×
    OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2))
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRuntimeAccepted : {α : Type} →
  {M J R : ℕ} →
    (target : List α) →
      (N P H : ℕ) →
        OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyRuntimeOutcome target N P H →
          OAI.EditApproximation.ClampedPhysicalSeed M J target.length N ×
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarDraw target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
              (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2) :=
fun {α} {M J R} target N P H execution => (execution.1, execution.2.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalLargeInput : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → Prop :=
fun N epsilon =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.inspectAccuracyWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) epsilon).1.isSome = true ∧
    OAI.EditApproximation.largeAccuracyInput N epsilon.value ∧
      (OAI.EditApproximation.localOnlineMassBound (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M
            (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N)).size ≤
        OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyMemoBits : {α : Type} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J R : ℕ} →
      [NeZero M] →
        [NeZero R] →
          List α →
            (target : List α) →
              (N P T S H : ℕ) →
                0 < H →
                  ℕ →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
                            (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N)
                            (H ^ 2) ⊕
                          OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (H ^ 2) T S →
                        ℕ) →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                        OAI.EditApproximation.ClampedPhysicalSeed M J target.length N →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange target N P
                                (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
                                H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
                                (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2)) →
                            ℝ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P T S H hH bits encode rootQ seed execution =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.rejectionSelectedBits
    (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
      (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2))
    (fun key accepted =>
      Sum.inl key ∈
        (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyMemoResult source target N P T S H hH bits encode rootQ
            (seed, accepted)).freshKeys)
    execution
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyResult : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N →
      OAI.EditApproximation.RecursiveMemoResult
        (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
            (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2) ⊕
          OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M
            (OAI.EditApproximation.intervalTreeDepth (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).M source.length)
            target.length (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N + 1) (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).T (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).S) :=
fun source target N input =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyMemoResult source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).T (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).S
    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) ⋯ (16384 * OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N + 5)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyMemoCode source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.finalTargetState target.length) input
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyRejectionWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyRuntimeOutcome target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) →
      ℝ :=
fun source target N execution =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyMemoBits source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
    (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).T (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).S
    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) ⋯ (16384 * OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N + 5)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyMemoCode source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.finalTargetState target.length) execution.1 execution.2
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyMemoCoarseWork.{u_1} : {α : Type u_1} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J R : ℕ} →
      [NeZero M] →
        [NeZero R] →
          (source target : List α) →
            (N P H T S passes copies : ℕ) →
              (ℕ → ℚ) →
                (ℕ → ℕ) →
                  ℚ →
                    ℚ →
                      ℚ →
                        ℚ →
                          OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarDraw target N P
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N) (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H
                              (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies →
                            (B : ℕ) →
                              (depth : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
                                ℕ →
                                  (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P
                                          (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
                                          (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H
                                          (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies ⊕
                                        OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length (passes + 1)
                                          copies T S →
                                      ℕ) →
                                    OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length (passes + 1) copies T
                                        S →
                                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseDraw source target B depth → ℝ :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P H T S passes copies factor multiplier tau eta
    kappa delta draw B depth bits encode root coarse =>
  have initial :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.clampedSeedNat (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable source target N B depth coarse);
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.chargedRandomMemoEvaluate source target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
      (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) T S (passes + 1)
      copies factor multiplier tau eta kappa delta
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyGroupRead target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyOnlineRead target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies)
      initial
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyAnswer target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies
        draw)
      bits encode root;
  ∑ entry,
    OAI.EditApproximation.selectionIndicator (Sum.inr (Sum.inl entry) ∈ result.freshKeys) *
      ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialArrayWork B (depth entry) (OAI.EditApproximation.initialThresholdQ N)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.initialLambda N) (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source entry.1)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.substring target entry.2.lo entry.2.hi) (coarse entry))
@[reducible] def OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseExecution : {α : Type} →
  {M J : ℕ} →
    {R : ℕ} →
      List α → (target : List α) → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) → Type :=
fun {α} {M J R} source target N P H B depth =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseDraw source target B depth ×
    OAI.EditApproximation.RejectionRuntimeArray
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRange target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedSeedPassCount N) (H ^ 2))
def OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseExecutionProjection : {α : Type} →
  [DecidableEq α] →
    {M J R : ℕ} →
      (source target : List α) →
        (N P H B : ℕ) →
          (depth : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseExecution source target N P H B depth →
              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyRuntimeOutcome target N P H :=
fun {α} [DecidableEq α] {M J R} source target N P H B depth execution =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable source target N B depth execution.1, execution.2)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionProjection : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseExecution source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).B
        (OAI.EditApproximation.computedPhysicalCoarseDepth source target N) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyRuntimeOutcome target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
        (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) :=
fun source target N =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseExecutionProjection source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).B
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedPhysicalCoarseDepth source target N)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedFullLaw : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      MeasureTheory.Measure (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome source target N) :=
fun source target N epsilon =>
  if OAI.EditApproximation.physicalLargeInput N epsilon then
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map Sum.inr (OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionLaw source target N)
  else MeasureTheory.Measure.dirac (Sum.inl ())
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionAccepted : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N →
      OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N :=
fun source target N execution =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyRuntimeAccepted target N (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).P
    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N) (OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionProjection source target N execution)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionRejectionWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N → ℝ :=
fun source target N =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyRejectionWork source target N ∘
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionProjection source target N

    (OAI.EditApproximation.finalTargetState target.length)

  (N : ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N →
        OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyMemoKey source target N → ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N → ℝ :=

  have input := OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionAccepted source target N execution;
  ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyKernelWork source target N input (cost input))

                    ℚ →
                      OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarDraw target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
                          (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80)
                          passes copies →
                        (depth : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalEntry M J target.length → ℕ) →
                          ℕ →
                            (OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalFamilyScalarKey target N P
                                    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
                                    (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H
                                    (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies ⊕
                                  OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length (passes + 1) copies T
                                    S →
                                ℕ) →
                              OAI.EditApproximation.ChargedPhysicalRequest M J target.length (passes + 1) copies T S →
                                OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseDraw source target B depth → ℝ :=
fun {M J R B} [NeZero M] [NeZero R] source target N P H T S passes copies factor multiplier tau eta kappa delta draw
    depth bits encode root coarse =>
  have initial :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.clampedSeedNat (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalCoarseSeedTable source target N B depth coarse);
  have result :=
    OAI.EditApproximation.chargedRandomMemoEvaluate source target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
      (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) T S (passes + 1)
      copies factor multiplier tau eta kappa delta
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyGroupRead target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies)
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyOnlineRead target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies)
      initial
      (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalFamilyAnswer target N P (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N)
        (40 * OAI.EditApproximation.smallLogExponent N) H (OAI.EditApproximation.inputSmallLog N ^ 80) passes copies
        draw)
      bits encode root;
  ∑ entry,
    OAI.EditApproximation.selectionIndicator (Sum.inr (Sum.inl entry) ∈ result.freshKeys) *
      ↑(B.size *
          OAI.EditApproximation.coarseInitialLabelCount B (depth entry) (OAI.EditApproximation.initialThresholdQ N)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.initialLambda N) (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSource source entry.1)
            (OAI.EditApproximation.substring target entry.2.lo entry.2.hi) (coarse entry))

                ℕ) →
              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalCoarseExecution source target N

    draw bits encode root =>

def OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyCoarsePreparationWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N → ℝ :=

    (OAI.EditApproximation.clampedSeedNat input.1) input.2 (16384 * OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N + 5)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyMemoCode source target N (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N ^ 2))
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedFinalRoot source target N)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionCoarsePreparationWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N → ℝ :=
fun source target N execution =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyCoarsePreparationWork source target N
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionAccepted source target N execution)
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionReadChargedWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) → OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N → ℝ :=
fun source target N execution =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionIndexedCoarseWork source target N execution +
        OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionCoarseSamplingWork source target N execution +
      OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionNonlocalWork source target N execution +
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionCoarsePreparationWork source target N execution
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionTotalWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N →
        OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyMemoKey source target N → ℕ) →
      OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedExecutionOutcome source target N → ℝ :=
fun source target N cost execution =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionReadChargedWork source target N execution +
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionKernelWork source target N cost execution
def OAI.EditApproximation.computedFullOutputWork : (source target : List ℕ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N →
        OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyMemoKey source target N → ℕ) →
      ℝ →
        ℕ →
          (OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput source target N → ℕ) →
            ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome source target N → ℝ :=
fun source target N cost setup shared output fallbackOutput x =>
  match x with
  | Sum.inl val => setup + ↑(OAI.EditApproximation.chargedSuffixDP source target).2 + ↑fallbackOutput
  | Sum.inr execution =>
    setup + ↑shared + OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionTotalWork source target N cost execution +
      ↑(output (OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionAccepted source target N execution))

                  ℕ :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation pass (passes + 1) +
        OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalDivAllocation copy (copies + 1) +
      OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScalarCodeAllocation scalar +
    9

def OAI.EditApproximation.queryInitialGatherFootprint.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →

      (N P F exponent : ℕ) →
        (hP : 0 < P) →
          (a : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →

                  OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n →
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                ((key : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) → (reader key).Footprint) →
                  (next :
                      List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n) →
                        List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι β) →
                    ((keys : List (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval n)) →
                        (words : List OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) → (next keys words).Footprint) →
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.queryInitialGather N P F exponent hP a q reader next).Footprint :=
fun {ι} {β} {n} N P F exponent hP a q reader hr next hn =>
  (hr q).bind
    (fun value =>
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSupportWithWork N P F exponent hP a value q)).bind
        fun keys => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect reader keys (next keys))
    fun value =>
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSupportWithWork N P F exponent hP a value q)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryInitialSupportAllocation N P F exponent hP a value q)).bind
      (fun keys => OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect reader keys (next keys)) fun keys =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect reader hr keys (next keys) (hn keys)

                                (OAI.EditApproximation.queryOnlineSourceGather parent N P F exponent H hP tau a initial
                                    child q reader next).Footprint :=

    hn =>

            (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineRangeWithWork parent P exponent H tau child

              (reader key))
          keys (next keys))
      fun keys =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect
        (fun key =>
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineRangeWithWork parent P exponent H tau child key)).bind

def OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalTableReadFootprint : {M J n : ℕ} →
  (copies T S : ℕ) →
    (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest M J n) →
      (child :
          { r //
            OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S r <
              OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalTableRequest.combinedRank T S parent }) →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalTableRead copies T S parent child).Footprint :=
fun {M J n} copies T S parent child =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.chargeOf (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalTableCodeAllocation ↑child + 1)
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.read child OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.done fun word =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.done word)
def OAI.EditApproximation.queryMeanReadFootprint.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  (count : ℕ) →
    (reader : Fin count → OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
      ((i : Fin count) → (reader i).Footprint) → (OAI.EditApproximation.queryMeanRead count reader).Footprint :=
fun {ι} count reader hr =>
  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.chargeOf count
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect reader hr (List.ofFn fun i => i)
      (fun words =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryMeanWithWork count words))
      fun words =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryMeanWithWork count words)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListAllocation words +
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.sumListWithWork words).1.divAllocation
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat count) +
          count.size))

  (key : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalScalarKey M J N n exponent F Q R B) →
    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarRead M J N n exponent F Q R B key).Footprint :=

      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.done word)
