def OAI.EditApproximation.queryWarmupProgramFootprint.{u_1} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {nx ny M : ℕ} →
    (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval nx) →
      (N P F : ℕ) →
        (a : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
          (initialRead :
              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
            (childRead :
                Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny →
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
              (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) →
                ((r : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → (initialRead r).Footprint) →
                  ((key : Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval ny) → (childRead key).Footprint) →
                    (OAI.EditApproximation.queryWarmupProgram parent N P F a initialRead childRead q).Footprint :=
fun {ι} {nx ny M} parent N P F a initialRead childRead q hi hc =>
  (hi q).bind
    (fun value =>
      (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersWithWork N P F a value q)).bind
        fun centers =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect initialRead centers fun words =>
          have initial := value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words;
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsWithWork M parent N P F a initial q)).bind
            fun inputs =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect childRead inputs fun childWords =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a initial
                  (fun input =>
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                  q))
    fun value =>
    (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersWithWork N P F a value q)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryRawCentersAllocation N P F a value q)).bind
      (fun centers =>
        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect initialRead centers fun words =>
          have initial := value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words;
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsWithWork M parent N P F a initial q)).bind
            fun inputs =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect childRead inputs fun childWords =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a initial
                  (fun input =>
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                  q))
      fun centers =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect initialRead hi centers
        (fun words =>
          have initial := value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words;
          (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsWithWork M parent N P F a initial q)).bind
            fun inputs =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect childRead inputs fun childWords =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a initial
                  (fun input =>
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                  q))
        fun words =>
        let initial := value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words;
        have allocation := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOverrideParentReadAllocation q centers words;
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsWithWork M parent N P F a initial q)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryWarmupInputsAllocation M parent N P F a initial allocation
                q)).bind
          (fun inputs =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect childRead inputs fun childWords =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a
                  (value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words)
                  (fun input =>
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                  q))
          fun inputs =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.collect childRead hc inputs
            (fun childWords =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a
                  (value.queryOverrideParentReadWithWork q centers words)
                  (fun input =>
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                  q))
            fun childWords =>
            OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadKernelWithWork parent N P F a initial
                (fun input =>
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                q)
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.localWarmupReadAllocation parent N P F a initial allocation
                (fun input =>
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadWithWork inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                (fun input =>
                  OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryChildReadAllocation inputs childWords input.1 input.2)
                q)

        (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork interval.hi interval.lo).1 [true])

      (instDecidableEqBool
        (OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork

                (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).hi
                (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).lo).1
            [true]).1

        ⋯.mpr

              (OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node).hi

            N P F A a (OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedSeedAt parent N multiplier) q
            (OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedSeedAtFootprint parent N multiplier)))
      fun h =>
      ⋯.mpr
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
          (OAI.EditApproximation.queryIndexedSliceWithWork source

            symbolBits positionBits)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.queryIndexedSliceAllocation source interval target.length q occurrence symbolBits
            positionBits)))

                    Fin
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.groupSampleCount M Q b
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.groupDyadicScaleAt M b (↑F) s))) →
                  (⋯.mp actual s j).Footprint)
              heap ⋯)
          ⋯ h)
      ⋯ ⋯;
  fun s j =>
  id
    (Decidable.casesOn (instDecidableEqBool (OAI.EditApproximation.queryScalePresentWithWork N b).1 true)
      (fun h => ⋯.mpr (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0)))
      fun h =>
      ⋯.mpr
        (Decidable.casesOn (instDecidableEqBool (OAI.EditApproximation.queryCellValidWithWork n exponent cell).1 true)
          (fun h_1 =>
            ⋯.mpr (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.done (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat 0)))
          fun h_1 =>
          ⋯.mpr
            (groupTransport
              ((OAI.EditApproximation.dyadicScales N).get (OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex N b ⋯)) b ⋯
              (fun x x_1 =>
                OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarRead M J N n exponent F Q R B
                  ⟨Sum.inr (node, OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex N b ⋯, ⟨cell.1, ⋯⟩, ⟨cell.2, ⋯⟩),
                    ⟨time, ⟨x, x_1⟩⟩⟩)
              (fun x x_1 =>
                OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarReadFootprint M J N n exponent F Q R B
                  ⟨Sum.inr (node, OAI.EditApproximation.physicalScaleIndex N b ⋯, ⟨cell.1, ⋯⟩, ⟨cell.2, ⋯⟩),
                    ⟨time, ⟨x, x_1⟩⟩⟩)
              s j)))

  (node : OAI.EditApproximation.PhysicalInternalNode M J) →
    (time : Fin R) →
      (key : OAI.EditApproximation.GroupScalarKey M F Q) →
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalScalarGroupRead M J N n exponent F Q R B node time key).Footprint :=

  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.chargeOf

      OAI.EditApproximation.queryCellValidAllocation n exponent key.fst.2)

      key.fst.2 key.snd.fst key.snd.snd)

  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_queryNaturalLEWithWork_value_140 := ⋯;
  have proof_binaryNaturalCompareWithWork_lt_53 := ⋯;

  OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge (index.2 + 1)
    (if hi : index.1 = true then
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.mapKeysWithWork (fun key => (Sum.inr key, 1))
        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryChargedWarmup source target N P F (A (↑parent).pass) (a (↑parent).pass) multiplier
          parent occurrence symbolBits positionBits)
    else
      let depth := OAI.EditApproximation.binaryNaturalCompareWithWork (↑(↑parent).node.fst) J;
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge (depth.2 + 1)
        (if hb : depth.1 = Ordering.lt then
          have interval := OAI.EditApproximation.physicalSourceInterval source.length (↑parent).node;
          have length := OAI.EditApproximation.saturatingSubtractWithWork interval.hi interval.lo;
          have short := OAI.EditApproximation.wordLEWithWork length.1 [true];
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge (length.2 + short.2 + 1)
            (if short.1 = true then
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.mapKeysWithWork (fun key => (Sum.inr key, 1))
                (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial source target N P F (A (↑parent).pass) (a (↑parent).pass)
                  multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits positionBits (↑parent).state)
            else
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.mapKeysWithWork
                (fun key => (Sum.map id (fun child => ⟨Sum.inr ↑child, ⋯⟩) key, 1))
                (OAI.EditApproximation.queryFamilyBoundedActiveRawBand source target N P F exponent H Q eM eF hP hM hF
                  tau (a (↑parent).pass) eta kappa delta T S passes copies parent ⋯ ⋯))
        else
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.mapKeysWithWork (fun key => (Sum.inr key, 1))
            (OAI.EditApproximation.queryPhysicalInitial source target N P F (A (↑parent).pass) (a (↑parent).pass)
              multiplier parent occurrence symbolBits positionBits (↑parent).state)))

    (answer.1, answer.2 + source.length + target.length + 1)

    parentAllocation childAllocation initialAllocation currentAllocation draw drawAllocation eb query =>

  have proof_eq_of_num_den_33 := @OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedGroupMemberSourceWithWork._proof_8;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;

  have proof_nat_representation_47 := ⋯;
  have proof_value_nat_48 := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.countedGroupMemberSourceWithWork._proof_23;
  have proof_nat_value_49 := ⋯;
  let b := 2 ^ eb;
  have side :=
    ((OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b)).1;
  have radius := (parentInitial query).1.naturalCeilingWithWork.1;
  have cells := (side.targetCellsWithWork (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) query).1;

            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.inversePowerTwo exponent).canonicalMulAllocation
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.nat b) +
          (parentInitial query).1.naturalCeilingAllocation +
        side.targetCellsAllocation (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue radius) query +

        (fun cell =>
          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedComputedCellSourceWithWorkRawBand source target parent P F
            exponent Q eM eF hM hF hP tau a parentInitial child initial current R hc hi b eb ⋯ (⋯.mpr (draw b cell))

          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedComputedCellSourceAllocationRawBand source target parent P F
            exponent Q eM eF hM hF hP tau a parentInitial child initial current R hc hi parentAllocation childAllocation

            (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.castGroupSourceAllocationRawBand F Q drawAllocation b cell) cell)
        cells +
    eb

      OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length →

fun {α} source target {M} parent P F exponent Q eM eF hM hF hP tau a parentInitial child initial current R hc hi draw eb
    query =>
  have guard := OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.refinementScaleGuardWithWork F (2 ^ eb) a (parentInitial query).1;
  if guard.1 = true then
    have selected :=
      OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedLocalGroupScaleSourceWithWorkRawBand source target parent P F
        exponent Q eM eF hM hF hP tau a parentInitial child initial current R hc hi eb draw query;
    (selected.1, guard.2 + selected.2 + eb + 4)
  else ([], guard.2 + eb + 4)

    parentAllocation childAllocation initialAllocation tableAllocation integerAllocation e query =>

  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_matches_5 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitCompareWithWork_lt_16 := ⋯;

  have proof_bitSubtractionRippleEquation_18 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_value_19 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_lt_pow_length_20 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_length_21 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_16;
  have proof_bitSubtractWithWork_sub_22 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitDivModWithWork_value_23 := ⋯;
  have proof_bitWordValue_bits_15 := OAI.EditApproximation.queryFinOfNatWithWork._proof_19;
  have proof_binaryNaturalDivModWithWork_spec_24 := ⋯;
  have proof_wordLEWithWork_value_26 := ⋯;
  have proof_seedGridSpacingWithWork_value_86 := ⋯;
  have proof_seedGridSpacingWithWork_pos_85 := ⋯;
  let b := 2 ^ e;
  have center :=
    (OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateWithWork
        (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1) ⋯ query).1;
  OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingAllocation b P +
            OAI.EditApproximation.roundStateAllocation
              (OAI.EditApproximation.bitWordValue (OAI.EditApproximation.seedGridSpacingWithWork b P).1) query +
          parentAllocation center +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityAllocation b F a (parentInitial center).1 +
      e +
    if (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.centerEligibilityWithWork b F a (parentInitial center).1).1 = true then
      integerAllocation b center +
        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.preparedOnlineReadAllocationRaw source target parent P F exponent H b ⋯ hP
          tau eta kappa child initial table R hc hi childAllocation initialAllocation tableAllocation t center
          (integer b center).1

def OAI.EditApproximation.queryLocalRefinementFootprintRawBand.{u_1, u_2} : {ι : Type u_1} →
  {α : Type u_2} →
    (source target : List α) →
      {M : ℕ} →
        (parent : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval source.length) →
          (N P F exponent H Q eM eF : ℕ) →
            (hP : 0 < P) →
              (hM : M = 2 ^ eM) →
                (hF : F = 2 ^ eF) →
                  (tau a eta kappa delta : OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                    (t : ℕ) →
                      (parentInitial :
                          OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                            OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                        (child initial :
                            Fin M →
                              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction × ℕ) →
                          (R : ℕ) →
                            (hc :
                                ∀ (i : Fin M) (r : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length),
                                  (child i r).2 ≤ R) →
                              (hi :
                                  ∀ (i : Fin M) (r : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length),
                                    (initial i r).2 ≤ R) →
                                (draw : OAI.EditApproximation.CountedCellGroupDraws M F Q) →
                                  (integer : ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ × ℕ) →
                                    (OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
                                      (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
                                        (Fin M → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
                                          OAI.EditApproximation.CountedCellGroupAllocations M F Q →
                                            (ℕ → OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length → ℕ) →
                                              (q : OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) →
                                                (readEarlier :
                                                    Fin M × ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                                      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι
                                                        OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                                                  (readCurrent :
                                                      Fin M × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length →
                                                        OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery ι
                                                          OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction) →
                                                    ((key :
                                                          Fin M ×
                                                            ℕ × OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) →
                                                        (readEarlier key).Footprint) →
                                                      ((key :
                                                            Fin M ×
                                                              OAI.EditApproximation.TargetInterval target.length) →
                                                          (readCurrent key).Footprint) →
                                                        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryLocalRefinementRawBand source target
                                                            parent N P F exponent H Q eM eF hP hM hF tau a eta kappa
                                                            delta t parentInitial child initial R hc hi draw integer q
                                                            readEarlier readCurrent).Footprint :=
fun {ι} {α} source target {M} parent N P F exponent H Q eM eF hP hM hF tau a eta kappa delta t parentInitial child
    initial R hc hi draw integer parentAllocation childAllocation initialAllocation drawAllocation integerAllocation q
    readEarlier readCurrent he hr =>
  (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.Footprint.computeOf
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierInputsWithWork M parent N P F exponent t hP a parentInitial q)
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryEarlierInputsAllocation M parent N P F exponent t hP a parentInitial
          parentAllocation q)).bind
    (fun earlierKeys =>
      OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect readEarlier earlierKeys fun earlierWords =>
        (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
              (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryOnlineSelectWithWorkRawBand source target parent N P F exponent
                H hP tau a eta kappa parentInitial child initial R hc hi t integer earlierKeys earlierWords q)).bind
          fun onlineActions =>
          have word := parentInitial q;
          OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.charge word.2
            ((OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.querySharedInputsWithWork M N F Q exponent eM eF hM hF parent a
                    word.1 q draw)).bind
              fun sharedKeys =>
              OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect readCurrent sharedKeys fun sharedWords =>
                (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                      (OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction.queryGroupSelectWithWorkRawBand source target parent N P F
                        exponent Q eM eF hP hM hF tau a parentInitial child initial R hc hi draw sharedKeys sharedWords
                        q)).bind
                  fun selectedActions =>
                  (OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.compute
                        (OAI.EditApproximation.queryNeededInputsWithWork selectedActions onlineActions)).bind
                    fun currentKeys =>
                    OAI.EditApproximation.BitQuery.collect readCurrent currentKeys fun currentWords =>

def OAI.EditApproximation.integerAcceptedSpaceTermsFootprintRawBand : (source target : List ℤ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFamilyInput (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
          (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N →
        List ℕ :=
fun source target N epsilon input =>
  let x := List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source;
  let y := List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target;
  [8 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerPreevaluationWork source target N epsilon,
    2 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerStoredDataSpace source target,
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedQuerySetupAllocation N,
    (OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryMemoResultRawBand x y N
          (OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceIndex source target)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputSymbolBits source target)
          (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputPositionBits target) input).memory.fractionStorage,
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedQueryTemporaryCellsFootprintRawBand x y N
      (OAI.EditApproximation.integerOccurrenceIndex source target)

      (OAI.EditApproximation.integerInputPositionBits target) input,

    OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyCoarseArrayCapacity x y N input,
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyCoarseScratchCapacity x y N input,
    OAI.EditApproximation.computedFamilyCoarseControlCapacity x y N input,
    4 * (OAI.EditApproximation.inputHeight N + (OAI.EditApproximation.integerParameters N).B + 4),
    OAI.EditApproximation.integerFinalAllocationRawBand source target N input, 8 * (N + 1)]
def OAI.EditApproximation.integerBinarySpaceFootprintRawBand : (source target : List ℤ) →
  (N : ℕ) →
    OAI.EditApproximation.BinaryFraction →
      OAI.EditApproximation.ComputedFullOutcome (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
          (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N →
        ℕ :=
fun source target N epsilon outcome =>
  if OAI.EditApproximation.physicalLargeInput N epsilon then
    match outcome with
    | Sum.inl val =>
      8 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerPreevaluationWork source target N epsilon +
            2 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerStoredDataSpace source target +
          OAI.EditApproximation.suffixDPAllocation (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
            (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) +
        8 * (N + 1)
    | Sum.inr execution =>
      (OAI.EditApproximation.integerAcceptedSpaceTermsFootprintRawBand source target N epsilon
          (OAI.EditApproximation.computedExecutionAccepted (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
            (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) N execution)).sum
  else
    8 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerPreevaluationWork source target N epsilon +
          2 * OAI.EditApproximation.integerStoredDataSpace source target +
        OAI.EditApproximation.suffixDPAllocation (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode source)
          (List.map OAI.EditApproximation.integerSymbolCode target) +
      8 * (N + 1)
